With `now`, its words take a separate content-width line below the third line, ellipsizing only when the sentence itself exceeds that column; the third line never gives up its width to the state. Without `now`, or with an unknown key, the old event mark and its exact old words stay below. A `done_*` key is shown only on a done item. Search includes `where`; a chat's mute action names `where` when present, while its rule action is unchanged. Fixed words and separators come from `strings_inbox_follow.xml` in English, Simplified Chinese and Japanese, following `spec/fixtures/inbox/now.json` and `row.json`.
- **List-state icons, times and tags**: `MessageItemState` shares `inboxNowIcon` with the detail: `waiting` uses a static clock, AI-working keys use a spinner, Needs-you keys use the needs-you mark, `failed` keeps danger, and done / FYI / folded stay static. Row times use the injected clock and zone with a 24-hour clock today, Yesterday, a weekday within six days, then a short date; follow-up times use tomorrow in words before a localized date and 24-hour clock. A direct chat's kind tag sits beside the title and a count above one is a badge; the separate source / kind / count chip row is gone. The FYI section title is 知悉即可 / FYI / 参考.
- **Event titles keep their room**: an `InboxItemRow`'s first content line is its title and time, never the source name squeezing the title to an ellipsis alone. The title takes the remaining width and may use two lines before ellipsizing; at 1.5× font scale and above the time moves below it so the first glyph stays visible. The source remains identifiable from its avatar mark and existing tags. This is a row defect fix and applies with or without `where`; other `MessageItem` callers keep their previous title-line behavior.
- **Opening**: items with event fields open `InboxEventRoute` (`InboxEventScreen.kt`, right pane on wide screens), whether opened from a row, a notification, a hub deep link, the Persona home's Needs you or the capsule's Open in app; other items retain source-page navigation. `InboxItemRoute` resolves through the same `inboxRowPage` as the list, while a direct chat link still opens the conversation. The event page shows tier/source tags, **View original** at right (the source conversation, mail, connector chat or extension item page), AI summary, `recap`, handling status (in progress / preparing suggestion / failed), suggestion card, handling chain and related items. Row completion, the context menu, multi-select and Hand to AI act on that one item, never on every item in its source conversation.
- **Current-state icons**: `inboxNowIcon` maps `waiting` to the static `PersonaIcons.time` clock, `queued` / `looking` / `agent_working` to the existing spinner, the Needs-you keys to the existing needs-you mark, `failed` to the failure treatment, and `done_*` / `fyi` / `folded` to the existing static done treatment. `JInlineStatus` accepts an optional icon independently of its tone; list-row rendering is unchanged.
- **Lineage and instructions (#6611)**: after the handling chain and before Related, **Thread** shows current ancestor and child titles and states, using `inboxNowForRow` with the old words as the fallback, and offers **Not the same matter** for each pair. **Same matter as…** at the end of that section and in More opens a searchable picker of open items, newest first and excluding the item and every descendant. Each choice shows its title and `inboxRowThirdLine` (`where · sender: preview` when available); search matches title and `where`. Picking one writes the raw parent reference without a confirmation, refreshes the tree and says it linked. A failed descendant read keeps the query and offers Retry, while an old hub without the tree route degrades to no known descendants. Every 409 uses one localized explanation rather than the hub's raw message. The complete current-state sentence, including who and next, stays readable; when it and the action group do not fit on one line, the group wraps below the sentence instead of taking width from it. A suggestion card does not remove the state line's current words and icon or **Conversation ›**; it removes **Say something** / **Try again**. Without a suggestion card, the state line keeps its current words and icon, then **Conversation ›**, then **Say something**; failed handling replaces the last with **Try again** and its localized default instruction. Both use `version: 0` with `action: instruct`, 409 refreshes through the existing stale path, and 403 hides the entry after the existing failure feedback.
- **Historical and deep-linked items**: record lookup tries the live snapshot, then the repository's bounded paged-back records (Done's history and earlier single reads), then `GET /api/v1/inbox/{id}`. The full record is parsed by the same `record(...)` path and cached through `notePagedBack`, so Done, suggestions and source navigation keep the item's manager and hub id. A bare id uses its noted manager, else the default manager; an app id keeps the manager it names. Thus another manager's item can still be opened from its list or a manager-aware route, while an unknown bare id that only that manager holds reports not found after the default's 404. Loading uses `JLoading`; a network, server or unsupported-transport failure shows the existing refresh-failure notice with Retry; only a 404 shows the existing missing-item text. A successful lookup accepts open, done and connector items alike. Concurrent readers share one request through the repository's item-read lock.
- **Suggestion card**: title is the kind (Suggested reply / Suggested Agent task / Conclusion; `handback` always **Suggestion**), then body and rationale. Single-line button rows: reply **Send · Edit · Reject**; Agent **Start · Edit · Reject**; conclusion **Got it · Reject**; decisions **Confirm · Edit · Reject**. Send itself confirms; there is no extra "Send as you?" dialog (one tap sends). Edit opens a sheet with editable text and confirmation above (unchanged text confirms without `text`), and **Say something** / **Tell AI** (`instruct`) below. Every request carries `client_id`, reused for retries of the same answer. FYI conclusion cards have no needs-you outline and stay in the list until Got it. Items archived after manager review (`done_by: manager`) show **Handled by manager** in the tier tag; chain `done` entries use a checkmark and have no link.
- **Stale suggestions**: on `409 suggestion_stale`, `HttpInboxAssistantTransport.suggestion` throws `InboxSuggestionStale` carrying the current item. The repository replaces the list item and the page keeps **Suggestion updated** above the new suggestion until another answer. Other errors (`inbox_item_done`, `assistant_in_doubt`, answer-only device) use shared operation feedback.
- **Handling chain**: the three newest entries by default (oldest of those three first). When there are more than three, **Expand all (N) / Collapse** is the trailing action on the section header itself, with its fold semantics. Each row shows a 24-hour `HH:mm` time—time only today, or a localized date plus the time on earlier days—the icon and the hub's sentence. `via` picks the shared icon meaning: a source mark for a source id, `agents`, `persona` or `person`; without `via`, today's kind icon stays unchanged. A `reported` entry always uses the reported icon, secondary text and an **Assistant says** tag, distinct from the program-recorded steps; it shows `detail` when nonblank (the original words) and falls back to `text` when `detail` is null or blank, including for an older hub. It stays collapsed to two lines by default and uses the existing `ChainTarget.Inline` row toggle only when its visible words overflow or it has messages; expanding shows the original words exactly once without repeating them in the lower detail block, and the toggle collapses them again. Short reported content without messages has no expander, while reported messages may still expand below. Agent and agent-stopped rows read the linked session's current state from `InboxSnapshot.sessions`, using the Agents list's exact `stateWord` in the page's language, but only on the newest chain entry for that session (hub order; exact ids first and `GroupKeys.raw` for grouped machine ids); a session absent from the list has no state. `chainTarget`: `source` with `position` opens Lark chat at that message (no chat means source page); `url` opens the in-App browser; `uid` opens mail; no fields opens source. `session` opens `ChatRoute` with `RouteSource.InboxItem` carrying the current item id; `item` opens that item (its event page when applicable); no link expands `detail` and `messages` in place. The draft sheet's **View process** uses the same sourced route. Back from an assistant conversation returns to the event or original page that opened it; Back from an Agent returns to that page on the unchanged Persona stack. Restored Agent pages use persisted `RouteSource.InboxItem` provenance without replacing their visible NavKey or saved UI state; when a cold restore no longer has the detail stack, Back pushes the existing `InboxItemRoute` so normal item resolution rebuilds the detail before returning.
- **Unit tests**: `InboxEventFieldsTest` in `core:contracts` (fixture states, legacy items, malformed values, unknown kinds), `InboxListFixtureTest` in `core:domain` (three `status.json` cases), `ParityInboxTransportTest.aSuggestionAnswerPostsItsVersionAndAStaleOneCarriesTheItem` in `core:hub` (path, body, two 409 cases), `InboxRepositoryTest.aSuggestionAnswerRedrawsTheItemTheHubAnswersWith` in `core:data`, `InboxEventsTest` in `feature:inbox` (sections, row marks, buttons, edited text, navigation, all chain links, collapse, time). `InboxFollowTest` checks every `now.json` and `row.json` case in all three languages plus the source-specific legacy third lines and injected 24-hour time formats, including the shared `time.json` cases when that fixture is present, with a fixed clock / zone and unknown-key and old-hub fallbacks; `InboxGroupNameRowTest` checks the legacy and follow-up row hierarchy and a complete state at narrow width. The shared phase-1 fixture uses fabricated data and is copied into both modules' `src/test/resources/inbox/phase1.json`. `InboxTimelineTest` checks every shared timeline case, icon meaning and Agent-session lookup; `InboxEventDetailTest` checks header/current-state order, fallbacks, reported and the timeline toggle. `InboxReportedDetailTest` checks null/blank detail fallback, collapsed overflow, one expanded original without duplication, no expander for short content without messages and valid navigation targets. `InboxReportedAdversarialTest` checks expandable messages, boundary conditions and stale-state behavior. `InboxAssistantTransportTest` covers single-item paths and failures; `InboxRepositoryTest` covers listed/history/single-read lookup, complete parsing, manager stamps, caching and concurrent readers; `InboxEventLookupTest` covers loading, missing and retry states; app `InboxHistoryLinksTest` covers the same outcomes for deep links and direct chats. `InboxOneRowTest` covers independent rows and actions, section reads, mixed working items and legacy source navigation; `InboxWorkingProjectionTest` carries the working-section regressions without the old conversation-view projection; `InboxOneRowBadgeTest` pins item-level counts and receipt-first reconciliation; app `InboxOneRowLinksTest` covers event and legacy item links. `InboxSourceCollapsedTest` covers collapsed opening for event and non-event chat/mail items, no original body loading or visible-read/seen reporting before **View original**, the existing reporting after it, Back returning to the detail, and unchanged direct source routes and other legacy pages. App routing tests cover deferred extension originals and direct chat links. `AppNavigatorTest` also covers a restored sourced Agent returning to the processing conversation and detail, an existing unsourced Agent retaining its NavKey while provenance survives recreation, and a cold-restored Agent rebuilding the item link; `ShellPositionTest` round-trips that provenance. `JSectionHeaderOverflowTest` checks that fitting headers keep their actions and overflowing headers keep the full title in a menu without oscillating.
- **Screenshots (no emulator)**: `./gradlew :feature:inbox:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*InboxEventShotsTest*'`, native Robolectric, three styles × light / dark: complete list with five sections and all row marks; six event pages (reply, waiting with expanded chain, urgent, Agent dispatch, conclusion, failure); Glass suggestion-updated and edit sheet. The test also taps Send, stale Reject, edited Send, Say something, manager-handled chain entry and View original, checking requests and destinations. PNGs in `build/inbox-event-shots/`. `InboxSourceCollapsedShotsTest` captures non-event chat and mail details before an original opens in every style, light and dark, at default and 200% text; PNGs in `build/inbox-source-collapsed-shots/`. `InboxEventLookupTest` captures loading, read failure and genuinely missing in all three styles and both themes, plus the failure state at 200% text, in `build/inbox-history-shots/`. `./gradlew :feature:inbox:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*InboxGroupNameRowTest*'` captures component boundary rows, three styles × light / dark × 1x / 2x font scale (24 PNGs), in `build/inbox-row-boundary-shots/legacy/` and `build/inbox-row-boundary-shots/follow/`; both tests assert that the title's first glyph remains visible at 2x and that the complete state fits at narrow width. `./gradlew :feature:inbox:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*InboxShowcaseShotsTest*'` reads `spec/fixtures/inbox/showcase.json` and its `now_clock`, producing the actual five-item list in zh-Hans and en (12 PNGs), sc-1's detail folded and expanded in both languages, three styles × light / dark (24 PNGs), and sc-2's open detail in zh-Hans, three styles × light / dark (6 additional PNGs), for 42 PNGs total in `build/inbox-showcase-shots/`, separately from the synthetic boundary rows. The sc-1 detail captures check c5/c6/c7 folded, c1…c7 expanded, the reported tag and the linked Agent's live state. The sc-2 captures check that the current state and **Conversation ›** (**处理对话 ›** in zh-Hans) remain with the reply suggestion, **Say something** / **Try again** are absent, and the summary precedes the suggestion. `InboxOneRowShotsTest` draws two independent items from one source conversation in all three styles, light and dark, at default and 200% text (`build/inbox-one-row-shots/`). App `InboxUnreadBadgeShotsTest` checks two items in one conversation and zero after reading in the same appearance matrix (`app/build/inbox-unread-shots/`). `InboxSectionHeaderShotsTest` captures section titles and their ordinary/overflow actions in all three styles, light and dark, at default and 200% text (`build/inbox-section-header-shots/`). `./gradlew :feature:inbox:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*InboxReportedShotsTest*'` runs `InboxReportedShotsTest`, capturing the 12-image matrix of three styles × light / dark × collapsed / expanded reported rows in `build/inbox-reported-shots/`.

## Inbox: background refresh does not move the list (#1947)

- **Refresh**: `RealInboxRepository` refreshes only on `{"type":"inbox"}`; bursts are combined (`refreshWindowMillis`, default 1 second, using `sample`), and an event arriving during refresh triggers another after completion. `{"type":"lark"}` only reloads the currently displayed Lark chat, not the list: the hub emits `inbox` when the list changes, as iOS does. Connecting still refreshes immediately; 15-second polling remains a fallback.
- **Loading row**: refresh sets `loading` true only while the list is still empty; `InboxScreen` only then shows "Reading Inbox". With existing content, background refresh inserts no loading row. `InboxSnapshot.refreshes` counts successful refreshes; `InboxExperienceRepository` uses it to detect completion so an empty list can replace old records.
- **Title**: a transition from online to **Connecting… / Updating…** must last 1 second before display (`rememberSettledConnection`); certificate mismatch and other states display immediately.
- **Unit tests**: `InboxRepositoryTest.hubEventBurstsOverAShownListRefreshOnceWithoutLoading`, `emptyListShowsLoadingWhileRefreshing`, `InboxFeatureTest.backgroundRefreshToAnEmptyListEmptiesTheExperience`, `InboxBackgroundRefreshTest`. Slow hub: mock `MOCK_INBOX_DELAY_MS` ([testing.md](../operations/testing.md)).

## Inbox: cloud document items (#2323)

Notifications that @mention you or comment on your Lark documents, sheets and bases (`kind: "doc"`, with a `doc` block and no `lark.feed_id`; specification in [inbox.md](../assistant/inbox.md) ("Docs: mentions in sheets, bases, and documents")) previously opened the mail page and showed "Mail failed to load".

- **Data**: `RealInboxRepository` uses `inboxDocRef` (`InboxDocDetail.kt` in `feature/inbox`) to read `doc` into `InboxRecord.doc`: `url` (fallback `link`), `type`, `notice`, `reply.label`, `error`, `rows` (title, table name, fields, status-column names and values); other fields ignored. Local cache (`CachedInboxRecord`) also stores `source` and `doc`, so opening after cold start still gives a document.
- **Routing** (`inboxItemPage`): Moments post → Lark chat (has `feed_id`) → mail (`mail` block, `source: "mail"` or `kind: "mail"`, without `doc`) → everything else to `InboxItemDetailRoute`. Connector items and unknown shapes no longer open mail.
- **Item page** (`InboxItemDetailScreen`): reuses the extension item shell (`InboxItemExtensionScreen`: triage tier, source tag, AI summary, AI draft / handoff, done); body shows "Sheet · a person mentioned you", **Open sheet** (through `LocalLarkLinkOpener` in `LarkLinkHost`: native document reader when the hub has `lark`, otherwise web sheet), where replies go, why the document couldn't be read, and **Rows that mention you** (one card per row: fields + status-column tags). Unknown items show only preview. Status columns are read-only on Android; editing remains web / iOS.
- **List**: document avatars use document-type icons (`avatarIcon` in `MessageItem`: sheet `SquaresFour`, base `Kanban`, other `FileText`), type tag **@me / Comment**; without summary the second line is "Sheet · a person mentioned you".
- **Tests**: `InboxItemPagesTest.aDocumentsNoticeOpensItsOwnPageAndOnlyMailOpensTheMailPage` (parsing, routing for document / Lark chat / Moments / mail / kind-only mail / connector / unknown items, cache, selection, tags); screenshots `./gradlew :feature:inbox:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*InboxDocShotsTest*'`, three styles × light / dark, also verifies **Open sheet** reaches the link opener; PNGs in `build/inbox-doc-shots/`.

## Inbox: groups, Done's history and selection (#2576)

The design of #2059 (web and iOS / iPad), with the user's choices there: the hub keeps done items 90 days and pages them (#2200), "Select all" is what is shown, "Jump to" is a menu, and Collapsed groups like the rest.

- **Groups**: Needs you, FYI and Collapsed by source or by time, a switch under the search field (`InboxPreferences.grouping`, kept on this device with the other filters); In progress stays flat. By source: the agents' cards ("Agents waiting on you", first either way), Lark, each mail account ("Mail · account"), then connectors and extensions by their own name. By time: today, yesterday, each of the 2–6 days before ("Sat, Nov 7"), 7+ days, 1+ month, by the last message. A section with one group shows no group header.
- **Done**: always by when it was done, "Just now" (the last hour) first. Past what the list holds (its few days, at most its newest 200 done items) the page asks the current assistant's history (`GET /api/v1/inbox/done`, `InboxRepository.doneHistory`, a cursor per manager; an old hub's 404 is no history), merged under Done; "Show older" asks the next pages. Those items are in no list, so the repository keeps their records: Move back and Mark done go to the item's own manager by the hub's id (#2974). Its header has **Jump to**: each group with its count (a tap opens it and scrolls there), Collapse all / Expand all.
- **Done's counts** (#3016, the hub's #2967): when Done opens, the current assistant's done items per day in the phone's zone (`GET /api/v1/inbox/done/days?tz=&sources=all`, `InboxRepository.doneDays`). With them, and no filter or search on, Done lists every time group with its exact count, loaded or not (Today without Just now; `InboxGroups.doneCounts` / `withCounts`), and Done's title and the Done chip both count their sum. Opening or jumping to a group that isn't all in fetches its own first page, the one before the group's end (`InboxGroups.span`, `before` without an id), and its Show older goes on from the hub's `next`; a group is whole once its items are in or its pages run out. The list's few days are whole only up to its newest 200 done items (#5549); past them their groups page in like the rest. With a filter or search, or a hub that answers 404 / 400 (no counts, or a zone it doesn't know), it is as before: what is loaded, and Show older at the end of Done paging back from the newest.
- **Folding**: each group folds to its title (a level below the section's: caption, regular, in every style, as iOS and the web) and count; Done's start folded except Just now and Today; what the user flipped is kept (`InboxPreferences.folds`, "section/key"). Group headers stay pinned at the top while their rows scroll under them (a background only while pinned).
- **Selection**: a long-press on a row starts it (the Android convention); the bar becomes "N selected · Select all · Mark as done" with ← (or Back) to end it. Each group header gets a tick (none / some / all, `JTriCheckbox`) that picks the whole group, folded or not, and says how many it has picked; a section without groups has the tick on its own header. Agents' cards, done items and ones Persona is working on can't be picked (faded). Mark as done is one `POST /api/v1/inbox/bulk` per manager (`markAll`, with Undo). With one row picked the bar's **⋯** has what its long-press used to: Hand to AI, mutes and rules.
- **Code**: the rules in `core/domain` (`InboxGroups`: time keys, source order, folds, ticks, Done's counts and spans; `InboxGroupsTest`), the rows' groups and titles and the history in `feature/inbox` (`InboxSections.kt`), the page in `InboxScreen`; `MessageItem` `checked` / `dimmed`, `JSectionHeader` `leading` / `sub`, `JTriCheckbox` / `JCheckMark` in the design system. Checked by `InboxGroupsShotsTest` (mock data: by source, by time, Done with two pages of history and Jump to, a selection marked done; every style, light and dark).
- **Not yet**: reopening an item of a non-default manager's history that the list no longer has goes to the default manager (the repository finds an item's manager in the list; #2974).

## Inbox, Lark chats and connectors: iOS parity (#3045)

The and-inbox batch of #2919; iOS is the reference throughout.

- **Mute** (G119, `InboxMutes.kt`): item `mutes` becomes `InboxRecord.mutes`; below Done / Hand to AI, the long-press menu lists "Mute this repository / conversation / sender · name" (repository name without owner). Tapping first marks matching unhandled items—same source, tag + value—done (`mutedBy`), then `POST /api/v1/inbox/mutes`; failure rolls back, success says "Muted “X”; it won't enter Inbox again". Inbox ⋯ gains **Muted** sheet (`GET /inbox/mutes`, newest first); each **Unmute** removes the row immediately (`DELETE /inbox/mutes/{id}`), rolling back on failure (`InboxMutesModel`). Separate from Agent muting (`/api/v1/mutes`). With several managers, **Muted** and **Rules** read the current assistant's alone (the manager named on the request, `listOwner`); while it is offline the sheet says they show once it is back. Entries are stamped with their manager (`InboxMute.manager`, `InboxPolicy.manager`), so Unmute, Edit and Remove go to it (`InboxPolicyTarget.manager`; tests `InboxMutesModelTest`, `InboxPoliciesTest`, `InboxRepositoryTest`; shots `InboxManagersShotsTest`).
- **Rules** (#2140, `InboxPolicies.kt`, [inbox.md](../assistant/inbox.md) "Policies"): after the mute entries the long-press menu lists "Rule for this repository / conversation / sender · name"; the sheet (`InboxPolicySheet`) reads `GET /inbox/policies` to prefill the item's rule, offers the tier and the AI twin as option rows, Save (`POST` for a new rule, `PATCH /{id}` for one there, off until something is chosen for a new rule) and **Remove** for an existing one. Saving moves the matching open, triaged, not-working items of that source to the new tier at once (`applyPolicyTier`, under the repository's refresh lock), restored on failure. Inbox ⋯ gains **Rules** after **Muted** (`InboxRulesSheet`, newest first; a tap edits, **Remove** sends `DELETE /inbox/policies/{id}` and rolls back on failure). Tests: `InboxPoliciesTest`, `ParityInboxTransportTest`, `InboxRepositoryTest`; shots in `ParityInboxShotsTest` (row menu, ⋯, new / existing rule, Rules, empty Rules).
- **Pinned messages** (G124, `LarkPinsSheet` in `LarkChatExtras.kt`): chat ⋯ **Pinned messages**, fetched only on opening (reading the list marks it read upstream), with **Load more** if `next_cursor` exists. Tapping an item first asks the hub where the original is (`pin-messages/{id}`, with chat `topic` so topic groups can find the thread); thread replies open the topic sheet (using the same message-display settings), otherwise jump near that message in chat. ⋯ / long-press **Unpin** confirms first. The message long-press menu offers **Pin** when `can_pin`, sending after confirmation (account from the Pin list's `account_id`). Account changed, unclear result and old hub (404 / 405 without code) each get a sentence. Account change clears and disables the list (only "Lark account changed; reopen the chat" remains); after removal or unclear result, no further removal before the list reloads. Previews show retained content only according to that Pin-list account's message-display settings; an account mismatch treats all options as off.
- **Read receipts** (G133): only query your own messages already in Lark (`LarkReadReceipts`: up to 100 per request, once per 3 seconds per chat, no more queries once all read; 404 / 405 stops queries for this visit). Left of the time: direct chat **Read / Unread**; groups (from Inbox `lark.is_group`, including two-person groups) "N unread" or **All read**; without the chat in Inbox, group status is inferred from counts. List changes do not cancel requests (rapid changes would prevent any receipt arriving); the next interval queries the updated list. iOS uses a progress ring for groups; Android uses text.
- **Message display** (G131): Settings › Lark › Message display (`LarkDisplaySettings.kt`, `GET / POST /lark/message-display`, with `expected`), **Keep recalled messages**, **Edit history**. Failure, 409, 403 and 404 each explain and offer **Reload**. Read once on chat opening (failure means all off): recalled `retained` messages display normally with **Recalled** below if keeping recalled messages is on, but take no reactions (no emoji bar in the long-press menu, existing reactions hidden), no thread and no card buttons (#5306, as on iOS, `docs/protocol/protocol.md`); **Edited** opens prior versions when edit history is on and `edit_history` exists. Both preferences form one `LarkMessageDisplay`, shared through `LocalLarkMessageDisplay` by chat, topic and Pin sheets.
- **Outlook** (G128, `OutlookSignIn.kt`): an Outlook option for adding mail. Without a hub client ID it shows registration steps, copyable redirect address and input (`PUT /mail/oauth/microsoft/config`); with one it offers **Sign in with Microsoft**, using the same browser + callback as Google (`GoogleSignIn.signInMicrosoft`; `OAuthRedirectActivity` also accepts `persona-mail-oauth://outlook`); errors and `error_description` go to the hub for recovery wording. If manager.json changes the redirect address this package cannot receive it, and the page explains. Reading hub config first shows loading; failure offers Retry; no missing-redirect registration form before the read finishes.
- **Messages (iMessage)** (G137, `MessagesConnector.kt`): `connector/imessage` no longer uses the generic connector page: status line (which Mac, readable or not), step-by-step permission guidance and **Check again**, **Hand to Persona** toggle (`PUT /imessage/settings`), **Delete messages stored on the hub** (toggle must be off; confirmation explains what is deleted and that the Mac is unaffected), explanation based on toggle state; **Chats** (`/imessage/chats`, search name or number, page at bottom) and chat detail (`/imessage/chats/{id}/messages`, **Earlier messages**, timestamps after an hour's gap or day change, sender shown in groups, iMessage / SMS marked when service changes).
- **Mark all read** (G215, `feature:agents`): under the Unread filter the row shows "N unread" at right; tapping confirms "Mark N unread sessions as read?". Both count and targets use list `unreadShown` (like iOS, regardless of grouping); the space menu's **Mark all read** also confirms first.
- **Tests**: `ParityInboxTest` (one each in `feature:inbox`, `feature:settings`), `ParityInboxTransportTest` in `core:hub` (MockWebServer path and request-body checks); screenshots `ParityInboxShotsTest` (both modules) and `MarkAllReadShotsTest`, three styles × light / dark, PNGs in each module's `build/parity-shots/`.
- **Topic groups** (G134, #576, iOS `LarkTopicCard`): a chat the inbox row marks `is_topic`, or whose page the hub answers with `topic: true`, is read as its topics (`InboxAssistantTransport.topics`, `…/messages?topic=1`, backward paging only). Each topic is its own card (`LarkTopicCard.kt`): avatar, name (Bot tag), day and time, the topic folded past 320 dp behind Expand / Collapse, the newest two replies under "View N earlier replies" (read from its thread a moment after the card is drawn, again when its reply count changes; "Couldn't load replies" with Retry), reactions, React and Reply; a tap or Reply opens the thread sheet, a long press the chat's menu. System lines stay lines and my messages still on their way stay bubbles. A recalled reply is left out of the preview. Tests `LarkTopicCardTest`, `LarkConversationTest.aTopicGroupIsReadAsItsTopics`, shots `LarkTopicCardShotsTest` (`build/lark-topic-shots/`); mock `MOCK_LARK_TOPIC=1` (版本发布).
- **Not done**: sending Lark images / files (G121) and group @mentions (G122) require changing the reply outbox (persistent request and `reply` API).

## Lark through extensions: display by hub features (#1530)

Lark access through a Lark gateway is provided by a private extension. The hub has `/api/v1/lark/…` only when the extension is installed; it adds `lark`, `lark_moments` (plus `lark_attachments`, `lark_native_encryption`) to hello features.
Android uses one check (`LarkFeatures.of(features)` in `core/contracts`, same rules as iOS `LarkFeatures` and web `larkOn`): empty features (hello not yet received), or only `lark_attachments` without `lark` (hubs with direct Lark access before extensions), keep showing everything; otherwise `chats` requires `lark` and `moments` also requires `lark_moments`.
The shell derives it from the selected hub's hello and provides `LocalLarkFeatures` (`core/designsystem`) to pages; demo mode always shows everything (its data includes Lark).

- The **Lark** row in Settings › Connectors (reads `/api/v1/lark/status`) appears, and status is queried, only when `chats` is true (`connectorRows`).
- Chat links (`ChatLinkHost`) and Inbox / Lark chat links (`LarkLinkHost`) use `larkNativeLink(url, lark)`: documents need `chats` for the native reader; Moments posts need `moments` for the post sheet and Moments chat cards. Otherwise open as ordinary web pages.
- Chat link cards (#3064, aligned with iOS LinkSheet / web LinkPanel): native reader for documents, post and comments for Moments (`LarkMomentsLink`), with **Retry** or **Open web page** on failure. Header offers **Add this document as context** (link only; Agent reads it) / **Add this post as context** (post body); ⋯ **Reload** reloads. When text can be selected (document permits copying), the selection menu adds **AI edit / Use as context** after Copy; without a chat it is **Hand to Persona**. Compose cannot read the selection, so the menu's own Copy writes to a clipboard intercepted for this operation (`LarkSelectable` / `PickingClipboard`), leaving the system clipboard untouched.
- Native reader (#3064, aligned with iOS FeishuDocReader): below title, author · "updated by (person)", then read / seen / like / comment counts; Lark's existing AI summary in an expandable card; **Contents** when there are headings (sheet, tap scrolls to section); **Open in Lark** at the body end. These read `/api/v1/lark/raw/documents/reader/info`, `header`, `ai-summary` (`InboxAssistantTransport.documentExtra`) alongside the body; failed extras are hidden and never block the body.
- Document comments (#2964, aligned with iOS `FeishuDocsComments` / web `DocComments`): **Comments** in the native reader title bar and below the title opens a sheet (`LarkDocCommentsSheet.kt`, model `LarkDocCommentsModel` in `LarkDocComments.kt`), with "Unresolved N / Resolved N" tabs. Threads show the quoted passage (**Whole-document comment** for full-document comments), resolved / partially visible tags, replies (@mentions and links accented, edited marker, N images). Reads `GET /api/v1/lark/raw/documents/comments`; writing requires declared `lark_doc_comments` (`LarkFeatures.docComments`, together with `lark`, not assumed before hello). Under threads: Reply, Resolve / Reopen; bottom composer defaults to whole-document comment; replying shows "Reply to name" (× returns to whole document); @ searches names (`/api/v1/lark/documents/people`), and outgoing `mentions` only retain mentions still in the body. Every write carries `client_id`, reused for retries of the same text to the same destination; failure keeps draft and says "Not sent: <hub reason>"; after writing use the response thread, or reload if absent. Commenting on a passage (aligned with iOS #1729, also requires `lark_doc_comments`): if copying is allowed, select text by long press and choose **Comment** after Copy, commenting on the selected words (block unknown; plugin locates by quotation); if copying is disallowed, long-press a paragraph or heading for **Comment on this passage**, carrying the whole passage and block id (`attrs.id` in JSON tree). Both open the comments sheet with **Comment on this passage** and quotation above the composer; × returns to whole-document comment. Sends `quote` (and `block_id`). API: [protocol.md](../protocol/protocol.md) ("Lark API"). Screenshots: `./gradlew :feature:inbox:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*LarkDocCommentsShotsTest*' --tests '*LarkDocPassageCommentTest*'`.
- Document cache and preload (#3064, aligned with web `loadDoc` / iOS skipping Lark links during web preload): the last 12 successfully read document bodies per pairing remain in memory (`LarkDocumentCache.shared`), displayed immediately on reopening; owner, counts and AI summary are not cached and reload on every open / refresh (like web and iOS), and preload only fetches the body. Native Lark links skip WebView pool preload and do not load web pages on opening; only **Open web page** (or automatic fallback after read failure) obtains a WebView. Thereafter refresh, progress and "Web page quit unexpectedly / Reload" follow web behavior. Lark documents now preload into this cache.
- Inbox items with `lark` / `doc` / `moment` only come from the extension, render normally and navigate to Lark chats / cloud-document item pages / Moments posts (see "Inbox: cloud document items"). Android has no Messages page or background Lark-chat fetching, and Knowledge does not show archived Moments posts; corresponding iOS / web toggles do not exist here.
- The company's branded social feed is always called **Moments**. Unit tests `LarkFeaturesTest` (`core:contracts`), `LarkNativeLinkTest` (`feature:inbox`), `SettingsConnectorsTest.feishuRowOnlyWhileTheHubHasFeishu`; screenshot comparison uses mock `MOCK_NO_LARK=1` on Settings.

## Hub links in assistant answers (#2680)

When an http(s) link is tapped in chat, `ChatLinkHost` first asks the shell (`LocalChatHubLinks`, `openHubPage` in `app`'s `ShellLinks.kt`): if host:port belongs to the selected hub group
(pairing address, alternate routes, hub-provided endpoints including `public_url`, other group hubs; `OkHttpHubConnection.knownAddresses`; reverse-proxy subpath prefixes stripped),
and the path matches an App page, navigate directly instead of opening a link card; otherwise open the card as before. Path parsing uses `HubLinks.page` in `core/navigation` (same paths as web `web/src/lib/router.ts`):

| Link | In the App |
| --- | --- |
| `/s/<machine>/<session>`, `/t/<machine>/<space>/<slot>/<session>` | That Agent chat (a short session id is used only if it matches one session); `/t/…` without a session opens Agents |
| `/persona/<chat>` | That assistant chat, opened in the Persona tab; `/`, `/persona`, `/persona/new` open Persona |
| `/todos?id=<to-do>`, `/todos/<to-do>`, `/todos` | To-do detail, to-do list |
| `/inbox/<id>`, `/inbox`, `/agents`, `/settings`, `/machines[/<machine>]` | Corresponding page |

Machine ids are the hub's raw ids plus group suffix (`GroupKeys.machine`). Queries and anchors (`?view=`, `#…`) do not affect recognition.

**Jumping to a message** (`?at=<item id>`, #2921, aligned with iOS #2762): `at` on `/s/…`, `/t/…/<session>`, `/persona/<chat>` becomes
`itemId` in `HubPage.Agent` / `HubPage.Conversation`. The route remains that chat's `ChatRoute` (one stack entry per chat; assistant chats display in place in the Persona tab).
`openHubPage` stores the target in `MessageJumps` in `core/navigation`; that chat page, newly opened or already open, consumes it once.
`ChatMessageJump` (`feature/chat`) uses in-chat search navigation: wait for initial positioning (last reading point / latest), expand its work group if loaded,
otherwise page backward (up to 20 pages); once found, scroll to top and outline for 3 seconds like a search hit. Not found leaves position unchanged and says "The message in this link is no longer in this chat".
Demo mode has no hub addresses and intercepts nothing. Opening hub links from other Apps / browsers into the App (App Links) is not implemented yet (second half of #2194 step 4).
Unit tests `HubLinksTest` (`core:navigation`), `ShellHubLinksTest` (`app`), `ChatMessageJumpTest` (`feature:chat`).

## Sharing to Persona (the share sheet)

Share › Persona in another App opens `ShareActivity`: the shared text, link or files, a note, **As context**, and where it goes: the main chat or one of the running Agents (the Agents in the list's order). It is queued at once (`ShareQueue`) and sent when the hub is reachable.

- Recent targets (#2395): a sent share remembers its target (the main chat or the session) with the time, at most 8, in the pairing's list settings (`ShareTargetStore`, `ScopedListPreferences`, so the keys are the ones the sheet lists and they go when the pairing does). The Agents shared to most recently that are still listed (at most 3, latest first) come before the others. The sheet starts on the last target when it was used within 24 hours and is still listed; otherwise on the main chat. A pick in the sheet wins (and survives rotation); sending is always the user's tap. The rules are pure (`ShareTargets.kt`), tests `ShareTargetsTest`.

## Ongoing notification and home-screen entries (ported from the old main-line App 2026-11-02)

Android counterparts of iPhone Live Activities, widgets and shortcuts. All four say the same thing: copy and selection rules in `FleetSummary` / `FleetText` in `core:domain` (matching iOS `FleetText` and hub `buildFleet`), all data from `FleetFeed` in `:platform:notify`.

- **Data**: `FleetFeed` receives WS `fleet`, also `GET /api/v1/fleet` after each connection (hub pushes only changes; with multiple groups, fetch each connected group concurrently so a stalled hub cannot delay others, translate to merged ids like pushed data, then sum). Latest snapshot stored per pairing in SharedPreferences `fleet`, allowing widgets and tiles to render with no process running; unpairing clears it and new pairings never show the former hub. HTTP and pairing identity supplied by App `FleetModule` (`FleetSource`).
- **Ongoing summary notification** (foreground-service notification, `OngoingStatus` / `Notifier.ongoing`): title "N need you / working / finished", subtitle other counts. With **Show lock-screen details** on, body is the first Agent's "name · work", or the permission/plan the buttons operate on (like iOS, not an older question above another Agent's Allow); expanded, up to three rows with "machine · space › tab" and "+N". Working-only uses a timer. First permission Deny / Allow, first plan View / Approve (`acceptEdits`; allow/approve require unlock; buttons through `ActionReceiver` + `NotificationActionExecutor`). Tap opens the sole Agent, otherwise Agents. Nothing, only unread completions for 5 minutes, or disconnected falls back to the connection row. With details off (default), body and expanded list also use generic text ("4 need you", "A permission is waiting", "2 permissions are waiting", "1 working"), no Agent names, locations or request detail—even if system lock screen is set to show all content. Buttons remain. With details on, names appear but the notification remains private, with a count-only public version, so hiding sensitive content at system level still shows only counts. See `platform/notify/README.md`.
- **Home-screen widget** (`PersonaWidget`, Glance 1.2.0): Persona logo row, state-colored title dot, other counts, then as many Agents as fit (max 3, dot + name + "work · machine · space") and "+N"; empty "No Agents need you", unpaired "Not paired / Open Persona and scan the hub QR code". Tap Agent opens its chat; other areas behave like tile (sole Agent or Agents). Navigation only, no widget approval. Uses actual launcher size (`SizeMode.Exact`, portrait and landscape): Agent count depends only on height (`WidgetLayout`, row heights measured in launcher, room reserved for "+N", fewer with larger system font; phone one-row height shows 0, two rows 2, three rows 3); if even logo cannot fit, only counts remain. Former Responsive sizes (110×100 / 250×150 / 250×200 / 250×260) all had 250 dp width except the smallest, so phone 2×2 widgets always chose smallest and showed only the title regardless of height; 150 dp also clipped "+N" (found on emulator 2026-11-02). Minimum 110×100 dp, default 3×2 cells, resizable both ways, no periodic refresh (`updatePeriodMillis=0`); `SurfacesRuntime` redraws on summary change. Design-system color/type (`SystemSurfaces`: selected style palette and type, light / dark pair passed to Glance to follow system); corners use system widget radii.
- **Quick Settings tile** (`PersonaTileService`): Persona label, summary subtitle ("All handled", "Not paired"); active when needs-you or working, unavailable if unpaired; tap opens like widget.
- **Icon long-press shortcuts** (`ShortcutPublisher`, published dynamically because static shortcuts require hard-coded application id): **Ask Persona** opens Persona, focuses composer and keyboard (`persona://open?target=tab&tab=persona&focus=composer`, cold or already open; the main chat's composer, inside the shared chat page (`ComposerFocusAsk` in `feature/chat`), as iOS's Ask Persona sends to the main chat; with no main chat yet, the composer of the page that starts it); **Approve pending**; then the two most recently active online Agents (not Persona itself, by update time; not a subtask the Agents list hangs under an online session above it, #4769 `RecentAgents`), opening their chats. Republish only on change; remove on unpairing.
- **Approve pending** (`ApproveFirstActivity`, not exported, transparent, only a design-system dialog over `palette.scrim`; without the scrim white dark-mode status icons disappear on light wallpaper): fetch pending requests fresh from hub, take earliest. Permission asks "Allow <summary>?"; plan "Approve this plan?", explaining "Edits will be accepted automatically; commands will still ask first" (`ApprovalPolicy` shortcut: plan only `acceptEdits`, unlock required), then "session · machine". Questions/terminal dialogs say to open Persona and offer **Open Persona** (that chat at that request; a card outside any session, e.g. `personad ask` in a plain terminal, opens Agents with that card, as its notification does, #5719); also loading, nothing pending, unpaired, error states. Buttons disabled during send; unclear outcome says "Result awaiting confirmation", not retry. Success Toast "Allowed Bash" / "Plan approved; starting".

Differences from old App: connection row and summary share **Live status** channel (old connection had a separate lowest-priority channel); with lock-screen details off even body omits names (old App relied only on public version and leaked names when system showed all content); data is hub-computed `fleet`, not phone-derived; shortcut plan approval uses `acceptEdits` (old App and iOS used `bypassPermissions`, changed per shared spec); recent-Agent shortcuts use terminal prompt icons.

Debug (debug package only):

```sh
# Approve pending states: loading, nothing, unpaired, question, screen, permission, plan, sending, error
adb shell am start -n <package>/persona.platform.shortcuts.ApproveFirstPreviewActivity --es debug.approveState plan --es debug.style board --es debug.theme dark
# Widget sizes (minimum, heights for 1 / 2 / 3 Agents; the 2-Agent one is two launcher cells wide), hosted over wallpaper like a launcher; grant binding once
adb shell appwidget grantbind --package <package> --user 0
adb shell am start -n <package>/persona.platform.shortcuts.WidgetPreviewActivity
# Add tile to Quick Settings
adb shell cmd statusbar add-tile <package>/persona.platform.shortcuts.PersonaTileService
# Ask Persona
adb shell "am start -a android.intent.action.VIEW -d 'persona://open?target=tab&tab=persona&focus=composer' <package>"
```

Mock hub: `MOCK_FLEET=1` (or `all`) pushes `fleet` on connect and after answers; `GET /api/v1/fleet` always available. `MOCK_FLEET=permission` only permissions (first request Deny / Allow), `working` working and unread completions only (timer), `done` unread completions only, `one` one working Agent (tap directly), `empty` nothing (connection row). Default first request is a plan (View / Approve). Change at runtime without restart (restart changes `HUB_ID` and needs re-pairing): `GET /mock/fleet?mode=<value above>` changes and pushes; `GET /mock/notify?interaction=<id>` sends a needs-you `notify` to Apps declaring `ws_notify`, like the hub; `GET /mock/renew?minutes=30` renews pending expiry to N minutes from now (deadlines normally derive from mock startup; after long uptime notification / shortcut answers only get Expired).

Verification status: unit tests `FleetSummaryTest`, `OngoingStatusTest`, `ShortcutTextTest`, `ApproveFirstModelTest`, `ComposerFocusRequestsTest`, `RouterTest`, `UnlockSettleTest` passed. Unified verification 2026-11-02 on an API 35 emulator (Pixel 8, Pixel Launcher), both system light and dark: ongoing notification collapsed / expanded, View / Approve, Deny / Allow, timer, unread completion falling back after 5 minutes, sole-Agent direct tap, connection row, branded status-bar icon; lock-screen Approve required unlock, then mock received `{"approve":true,"mode":"acceptEdits"}`, while Deny required none; lock-screen privacy (details on/off × system show-all/hide-sensitive); widgets (four preview sizes × unpaired/empty/Agents, actual launcher 2 × 1, 2 × 2, 2 × 3, 3 × 2); tile (unpaired/empty/active, tap to Agents); icon long-press menu; nine Approve pending states × three styles × light / dark, actual launcher approval of a permission and a plan both produced Toasts; Ask Persona from cold start and while on Agents both opened Persona home and keyboard. Fixed during verification: unlock after lock-screen button still treated as locked (see Lessons), foregrounding restored an already-fallen-back notification to "N finished", unpairing left an undismissable "Waiting for network", details-on made the entire notification public, two-cell-wide widget drew no Agents. Note: Pixel defaults to hiding silent lock-screen notifications; the ongoing notification (low-importance Live status channel) then does not appear. Live Update capsule requires Android 16+, not verified.

## Phone data tools: iOS parity (2026-11-02)

When Claude calls `phone_*`, the hub merges same-name tools into one definition (the longer description), and may route the call to any phone declaring it, so Android definitions match iOS byte for byte: names, English descriptions, parameters (types, descriptions, ranges), required fields all follow `apple/Persona/PhoneTools/*.swift`. Out-of-range numbers are clamped; extra parameters ignored; return structures use iOS keys. Only a tap on Deny (or dismissing the dialog) returns `The user declined on the phone.` (#1875). A confirmation that cannot be shown (Persona in the background with its notifications or the Phone action confirmations channel off, or the app leaving the dialog when no notification can be posted) says so and how to fix it; one withdrawn because the phone tools closed returns the usual interrupted sentence. Swiping the confirmation notification away (or "Clear all") ends it at once and returns that it was dismissed and nothing was saved (#3861). None of these reads as a refusal (`PhoneConfirmation`, `PhoneToolText`). `phone_notifications_list` remains Android-only. Fixtures are exported from iOS source by `python3 scripts/assets/export_ios_phone_tools.py` (`--check` detects staleness); `PhoneToolCatalogTest` compares each. Deliberate differences (clipboard and location foreground-only, video uses preview frames, creates a local calendar if none writable) and all rules: `platform/phonetools/README.md`.

**Concurrent reads, FIFO confirmations, no app-side countdown (2026-11-12, parity with iOS `PhoneConfirmations` and `ToolResultOutbox`).** `PhoneToolRegistry` no longer serialises calls behind one mutex: a confirmed write waits for the user inside `PhoneConfirmations`, which keeps exactly one visible card and queues the rest FIFO, while reads and other writes run concurrently. A second delivery of the same `call_id` either replays the cached terminal result or shares the in-flight leader's outcome via `PhoneToolCallGate`; it never re-executes the tool, and a cancelled follower does not cancel the leader. The hub owns how long a confirmed write may wait (`phoneConfirmWait`); there is no Android-side 90-second countdown that withdraws the card. The local `execute` timeout still applies, but on a confirmed write it returns an *uncertain* result ("may already have been saved, do not retry automatically") instead of an ordinary timeout, and the same `call_id` later replays that cached uncertain result. Once a confirmed write has entered its local execute (tracked in `PhoneToolRegistry.writesInFlight`), the enabled / permission / visibility watcher stops trying to cancel it: the local operation has to finish or fail on its own so the result can be returned, possibly as uncertain. A result whose socket send failed (`PhoneToolsTransport.result` returns false) is held in `PhoneToolResultOutbox`, scoped to the pairing runtime, bounded and in-memory only; the outbox re-sends *results* on reconnect, never re-executes a tool, and nothing is replayed after process death. `queue_if_offline` stays a hub-side feature: the hub adds it to the schema and strips it before forwarding, so the Android app does not implement a second write queue.

Photo image blocks previously used `mimeType`; the hub only recognizes `mime_type`, so images never reached Claude. Fixed, guarded by `PhotoContentTest`.

Mock hub: `GET /mock/tool_call?name=phone_device_info&args=<JSON>` calls a phone declaring that tool like the hub; `GET /mock/phone` shows registrations and responses. Unit tests passed. Verified 2026-11-02 on API 35 emulator: calendar permission prompt, confirmation dialog (three styles × light / dark), Deny returns `The user declined on the phone.`, Allow writes to local Persona calendar; background App's lock-screen confirmation Allow followed by unlock also writes (fixed, see Lessons); `phone_photos_recent` and `phone_photo_get` (image block `mime_type: image/jpeg`, `max_size` applied).

## Viewing and marking up images

Chat images open full-screen; marked-up images become composer photos (port of old main-line App d2d484ef, 2026-11-02). Viewer/editor are design-system components (`ImageViewer.kt`, `ImageEditor.kt` in `core:designsystem`), reusable later by Inbox/files; chat assembly is in `feature:chat`'s `media/` (`ChatImageViewer`, `MediaShare`).

- Entry: tap a chat image (Agent image, tool screenshot, sent photo, attachment thumbnail) to open `ChatImageViewer(images, initialIndex, onDismiss, onEdited, photoLimitReached)`, wired by the chat page, in chat order. Components are `ImageViewer(images, initialIndex, onDismiss, actions)` and `ImageEditor(source, onCancel, onDone)`. Image: `ViewerImage(key, description, width, height, load)`; `load` returns encoded bytes, exception/empty means failure. Action: `ViewerAction(label, icon, onClick)`; handler receives index and original bytes, returns viewer notice (null means none).
- Appearance: full-page (`JFullScreen`), always dark with light system-bar icons; top ✕ and "n / m" only for multiple images; bottom action row, floating capsule sampling the image in Glass, bottom bar in Brief/Board. Single-line labels; if crowded, only first (Save) retains text, then all icons if still crowded. Loading spinner (known dimensions first show a proportional placeholder), failure **Image failed to load / Retry**.
- Gestures: swipe between images; pinch 1–6× centered between fingers; double-tap zooms 2.5× at finger, again resets; one-finger pan when zoomed, edges cannot be pulled into view; tap toggles controls; at 1× vertical drag dismisses with fading background, release beyond 120 dp closes, otherwise springs back. Back and ✕ close.
- Decode: background `ImageDecoder` (EXIF orientation corrected, unlike `BitmapFactory`), power-of-two sampling to at most twice screen long edge (1024–4096); original bytes retained for actions; last 8 images' bytes cached in memory to avoid rereads.
- Actions (`ChatImageViewer`): Save (MediaStore `Pictures/Persona`, no permission; Saved to photos / Save failed), Edit (only if chat consumes result), Share (bytes to `cache/shared/`, FileProvider `<package>.chat.files` to system share; Share failed; opportunistically removes files older than one day), Copy (same file's content URI to clipboard; Image copied / Copy failed). Results use the viewer's own notice strip: `JFullScreen` is a dialog window, covering the App's `LocalAppFeedback` in the window below.
- Edit (#4992, iOS `ImageEdits` / `ImageEditor` from #4963): Box / Pen / Crop. Box and Pen draw in six fixed red/yellow/green/blue/white/black colors (`MarkupColors`, independent of style) and three widths (thin / medium / thick, medium the default and the old width). Crop shows the whole turned picture with a frame whose corners and edges drag (inside moves it; a minimum size), a shape (Freeform, Original, Square, 4:3, 3:4, 16:9, 9:16), **Rotate** (a quarter turn left; the crop and its shape turn along) and **Reset**. Undo and Redo step through every change (a mark, a crop let go, a turn, a shape, a reset, Clear). The edits are a pure value (`ImageEdits`: turns, crop in fractions, shape, marks in source pixels; `EditHistory`), drawn on screen through one matrix and burned in by `ImageEdits.render` (turned, drawn on, cropped, white under transparency); tests `ImageEditsTest` (geometry and pixels ported from iOS `ImageEditsTests`), shots `ImageEditorShotsTest` (`build/image-editor-shots/`). Done draws onto original resolution, converting visible stroke width to image pixels (`ImageMath.strokeInImage`), so sent image matches the preview. Output long edge 1600, JPEG 75, `cache/chat-captures/` (transparent areas filled white), then `ComposerAttachment(image = true)` to chat `onEdited`; `LocalAppFeedback` says "Added to the composer; add a message to send", viewer closes. Already 4 photos says "Up to 4 at once" only. Editing decodes at least a 1600 long edge (output at most 1600).
- Composer photos (iOS `ComposerImages`, web `AttachmentTray`): tapping an uploaded photo with a local original opens the same editor (1600 long-edge decode). An edited photo keeps its source, edits and history beside its attachment (#4992, iOS `EditedImage`; `ChatComposerState.photoEdits`, in memory, never sent): a tap opens the editor on the untouched source with its crop, turn and marks, all still undoable (`ImageEditor(start =, onEdited =)`); every edit undone sends the source again and keeps nothing; the edits go with the attachment, and a camera photo's capture stays in `cache/chat-captures/` while the photo made from it is in the draft (`leftCaptures` with the kept sources). Its source unreadable, it opens on itself, fresh. Done writes JPEG 1600/75 and uploads via `uploadAttachment` to that session's computer, replacing the original in place only after success; message carries only new path. Failure keeps original and says "Edited image couldn't upload; original kept". Uploading/failed/invalid/computer-only photos do not edit (failed still retries); Done without marks does not re-upload. Logic `ComposerPhotoEdit.kt`.
- Pure calculations in `ImageMath` (fit, screen-to-image coordinates, stroke width, sampling, scale, scale/pan, drag-dismiss opacity, types PNG / JPEG / GIF / WebP / HEIC / AVIF), unit test `ImageMathTest` in `core:designsystem`; action order/text/photo limit in `ChatImageViewerTest` in `feature:chat`.
- Unified-verification screenshots (three styles × light / dark, `debug.style`, `debug.theme`):
  - Component catalog (`debug.openCatalog true`), last **Image viewer** section. **View images (3)** opens viewer: each waits 0.6 seconds for loading spinner/proportional placeholder; first landscape, second portrait (n/m), third deliberately unreadable (failure). Tap hides controls, double-tap zooms, drag halfway (background fades, controls hide; hold with `adb shell input motionevent DOWN / MOVE` before screenshot, since `input swipe` releases). Save/share/copy show local notices (samples do not really execute); Edit opens editor over viewer. **Markup editor** opens directly: rectangle, pen, color changes, undo, Done. Estimated four labels fit 411 dp; narrower (`adb shell wm density` increased), only Save retains text (estimate, not checked on a device); capture both.
  - Chat (after wiring): mock `MOCK_CHAT_IMAGES=1`, "博客迁移" ("Blog migration") has two images (tool screenshot and pre-reply image); inspect multi-image viewing, then edited attachment row and notice in composer.

## Lessons

- Never read response bodies on the main thread: pairing starts requests in a main-thread coroutine; reading body triggers StrictMode `NetworkOnMainThreadException`,
  with only generic Connection failed / Connection incomplete in the UI (`PersonaPairing` logcat has the reason). JVM unit tests miss it; it also depends on whether body arrives with headers:
  `adb reverse` via 127.0.0.1 often arrives in two chunks and fails, while small responses over the emulator's documented host alias sometimes pass by luck.
  `awaitResponse()` now buffers the body on OkHttp's thread by default; only APK downloads use `buffered = false`, streaming on `Dispatchers.IO`.
- Emulator screenshots (both light / dark required, `docs/operations/testing.md` ("UI checklist")):
  - Dedicated AVD, fixed port (`emulator -avd <name> -port 5600`), all adb commands `-s emulator-5600`; leave others alone.
  - Delete the AVD you created when the lane is merged or abandoned (`avdmanager delete avd -n <name>`); each one keeps 2–4 GB of user data on disk.
  - Data from `scripts/mock/mock_hub.py`, deep-link pairing. Device shell truncates URL `&`; wrap the whole command in another quote layer:
    `adb shell "am start -a android.intent.action.VIEW -d 'persona://pair?url=...&token=mock' <package>"`, then tap Connect on confirmation.
  - Before every screenshot `am force-stop`, then cold start; otherwise each tab's stack retains the prior detail. Chat direct entry `persona://open?machine=&session=`.
  - Find text nodes' coordinates with `uiautomator dump`. Duplicate labels (Settings is both bottom tab and Agents gear description) require coordinate taps.
- `adb shell input swipe` downward from the very top opens system Quick Settings; start historical scrolling from screen center.
- A newly created AVD occasionally has no network on first boot (`eth0` DOWN in `ip addr`, no NetworkAgent in `dumpsys connectivity`); App stays on "Showing local cache" after pairing. Restart emulator once.
- A key input (TAB, BACK, `input text`) leaves touch mode; later cold starts focus the first focusable control (phone Agents top-bar New session, extra focus outline in screenshots). Before cold start, the screenshot script taps the launcher's status bar to return to touch mode.
- `HubService` starts with `startForegroundService`. Stopping it before `startForeground` (`stopSelf` or external `stopService`) crashes with `ForegroundServiceDidNotStartInTimeException` (logcat first: "Bringing down service while still waiting for start foreground"). This caused frequent pairing crashes on heavily loaded screenshot machines (confirmed 2026-11-02). Three former paths: `onStartCommand` immediately `stopSelf` if unpaired/user stopped; `NotificationRuntime` `stopService` on `isPaired` changing true/false before our start foregrounded; system start (new-process service restart, `am start-foreground-service`) stopped in `Application.onCreate` before foregrounding. Now service always enters foreground before deciding, uses `stopSelf(startId)` to avoid stopping newer starts; `HubService.stop()` calls `stopService` only if already foreground, records pending stop for our starts not yet foreground (`ForegroundStarts`), otherwise leaves the service to decide. Repro: after emulator `adb root`, run `am start-foreground-service -n <package>/persona.platform.notify.HubService` with an unpaired App; always crashed before, now foregrounds then stops itself.
- herdr server inherits its launching shell environment: starting it with proxy variables also routes claude through that proxy. Use a clean login shell for integration tests.
- Tools allowed by Claude Code allow rules/bypass do not trigger PermissionRequest; test permission cards in a session without those allowances.
- `JsonCodec` `encodeDefaults = false` omits default-valued fields, while hub treats missing as empty/false: scheduled task `enabled = true`,
  `time = "09:00"` were omitted and saves disabled tasks or returned 400. Fields sent to a hub that treats omission as empty need `@EncodeDefault`, or a hand-built body like scheduled tasks (`ScheduledTask.editable()`, `AssistantManagementTest` checks exact requests).
- OkHttp does not retry WebSocket upgrades on a dead connection: after forwarding (adb reverse, ssh -L) breaks once, reconnects return `unexpected end of stream` until App restart.
  Evict connection pool on WebSocket failure (`connectionPool.evictAll()`).
- Inverted chat lists (`reverseLayout`) preserve first-visible position on insertion at the front: growing/new replies leave even latest-position readers stationary. Explicitly scroll to 0 when already at latest or just sent a message.
- Drag reorder (diagnostics candidates, `EndpointDrag.kt`): do not change `JGroupItem.separate` when lifting a row; Board's separate uses another `Column` branch, recreates the row and kills the gesture (only change group position). For long-press drag, set `JRow.onLongClick` null and detect in the slot, a child of the row click that gets down first. Handle consumes down, so handle and long-press don't both activate. Never attach two gestures to one drag (double distance).
- Changing opacity must not use `Modifier.alpha` (root cause confirmed 2026-11-02 of `JButton` losing everything but text after disabled→enabled): `alpha(1f)` adds no layer, other values add graphicsLayer, so 0.4→1 removes a layer mid-chain. Preceded by `clip` and `background` (button fill, composer fill/border, decision option fill), Compose UI 1.12.1 then stops drawing that background until it changes again; child text still draws. Tested emulator/Robolectric: removing `clip`, or keeping layer (`graphicsLayer { alpha = … }`, or 0.999) fixes it; removing `clickable` doesn't; press `scale` layer at chain start is unaffected. All disabled fading in design system uses `Modifier.stableAlpha` (persistent layer, only alpha changes): `JButton`, `JIconButton`, `JTextField`, `JSwitch`, `JCheckbox`, `JRadio`, `JRow`, `DecisionOption`, `JActionSheet` rows. Pages no longer need `key(enabled) { JButton(…) }`. `EnabledStateTest` in `core:designsystem` checks background/border pixels with native Robolectric and fails on old code. Muted `AgentRow` also uses stableAlpha (Board formerly had `alpha(if (muted) 0.75f else 1f)` after clip/background). `AgentRowTest` checks restored fill, color block, hairline and pixel identity after unmute. But old AgentRow never reproduced missing fill: emulator 2026-11-02 mute/unmute via list menu (Board/Glass, light / dark) remained pixel-identical; Robolectric, even with only opacity changing and state color unchanged, too. Thus that test passes old code as well; it only guards restored appearance.
- Two-pane left selection never highlighted (clicks, deep links, debug entries): shell compared `paneMetadata(key) == ListDetailSceneStrategy.detailPane()`, but `detailPane()` creates `DetailMetadata` without `equals` each time, always unequal, so `LocalDetailKey` stayed null. Now route-based (`isDetailDestination` / `detailOf` in `app/…/navigation/ShellPanes.kt`; `PairingRoute`, `PhoneToolsRoute` map to corresponding settings pages). `ShellPanesTest` covers deep links, clicks, back, other tabs. Don't compare Navigation 3 / adaptive metadata for equality. Brief rows have no side margins; selected panel and left ink line previously covered icons, so Brief `selectedRow` extends panel 12 dp each side.
- New-session node detects claude readiness by composer footer text (`inputBoxVisible` in `server/internal/node/claudeready.go`). Some Claude Code versions use
  `⏸ manual mode on · ← for agents`; node misses it and waits a minute before "did not become ready", though usable. App finds the pane absent before launch but now in the target directory and opens it.
- Xiaomi / Redmi: `adb install` requires Developer options USB installation (Xiaomi account login), plus a phone USB-install confirmation that auto-denies after seconds
  (`INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED`). Injected screen input additionally needs USB debugging (Security settings), otherwise `INJECT_EVENTS`.
- When phone cannot reach hub directly (hub local network, phone mobile data), USB integration works: forward computer localhost 18660 to hub, then `adb reverse tcp:18660 tcp:18660`,
  pair phone with `http://127.0.0.1:18660`.
- Two-pane `ThreePaneScaffold` (material3-adaptive) defaults to moving focus to current pane (`PaneScaffoldDirective.shouldAutoFocusCurrentDestination`), taking the first focusable element: Agents/Inbox search. Tablets/foldables with hardware keyboards open an input-method bar immediately, first Back only hides it (even touch mode; single-pane phone unaffected). `Root.kt` disables via full constructor; `PaneScaffoldDirective.copy()` lacks this parameter and restores default true. To trace focus: attach `onFocusChanged` to field and log `Throwable()` stack; synchronous focus stack contains initiator.

### Lessons from the old main-line App

Imported when merging main line 2026-11-02: pitfalls from the old single-module App; class/file names here refer to that App (original text via `git show origin/master:docs/android/android.md`, code in pre-merge main-line history). Use as reference when implementing similar features in the new architecture.

- Folding changes `screenSize` / `smallestScreenSize`; without `configChanges`, Activity rebuilds. Compose `rememberSaveable` restores,
  Activity fields (`MainActivity.openSession`, notification-opened chat) do not, so folding closes chat; recreation also reprocesses launch intent,
  reopening a notification chat the user closed. Now declares `configChanges`, saves open chat in `onSaveInstanceState`, handles launch intent only on first `onCreate`.
- Custom panels (two-pane right side, placeholders, Persona left side) need `Surface(color = …)`, not `Modifier.background`: only Surface sets `LocalContentColor`;
  default `Text` is black, making dark-mode titles/body invisible on black (old bottom sheets/Scaffold had Surface, so single-pane unaffected).
- Same page at different Compose-tree positions across layouts loses position-based `rememberSaveable` (drafts, viewed Persona chat): wrap each with `SaveableStateHolder.SaveableStateProvider(key)`;
  `removeState` for pages no longer drawn, or closing/reopening returns to old scroll position instead of latest.
- Don't put full-screen images in Compose `Dialog`: window stays between status/navigation bars (tried `decorFitsSystemWindows = false`, `FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS`, underlying page still visible at edges).
  Draw over chat directly (`ImageViewerOverlay`), set status icons light while open, restore on close.
- `Markdown` defaults to `SelectionContainer`; long press opens system text selection instead of message menu. Chat replies use `selectable = false`; menu Select puts the message's words in a `SelectionContainer` where they are (`SelectableWords`, #4210 T5) and selects them all.
- Inverted list `verticalArrangement = spacedBy(x)` packs at top with few messages; use `spacedBy(x, Alignment.Bottom)`.
- Chat verification uses `scripts/mock/mock_hub.py`: `MOCK_CHAT_IMAGES=1` adds two screenshots to "博客迁移" ("Blog migration") for viewer/editor; files/Git use fabricated `~/code/weather`.
- Xiaomi / HyperOS `adb shell input` requires Developer options › USB debugging (Security settings), then `adb reconnect` to apply;
  occasional consecutive `SecurityException: Injecting input events requires … INJECT_EVENTS` recover after seconds; test clicks wait 3 seconds and retry on denial.
- Terminal typing: Android IMEs compose even English words for suggestions; waiting for commit delays a word until space. Send only each change's delta, including composing text
  (new characters or backspaces). Pinyin appears first, then backspaces replace it with Chinese on selection (emulator has no Chinese IME, not device-tested yet). Android emoji fonts render ⏺ ⏸ ⏵ as two-cell colored icons;
  U+FE0E does not help, so draw these shapes ourselves.
- After forwarding breaks, hub may still consider node online but every call returns `rpc: connection closed` (including /screen). Restart persistent node
  (`launchctl kickstart -k gui/$UID/com.persona.node`), reconnect machine from hub; no App change.
- `adb shell am start` with an intent identical to current task root only foregrounds it ("Activity not started"), no onNewIntent;
  verify shortcuts through launcher long-press, or leave App on another page first.
- Application-context WebView always stays light: application context lacks App theme (`isLightTheme` true); WebView only supplies
  `prefers-color-scheme: dark` / algorithmic darkening when outer theme is dark. Pool WebViews use `ContextThemeWrapper(app, Theme.Persona)`; `values-night` `Theme.Persona` is dark with `isLightTheme=false`.
- Adding WebView selection actions: Chromium fills menu in `onPrepareActionMode` on every selection change, all its items demand button display, which floating toolbar prioritizes;
  custom items also need `SHOW_AS_ACTION_ALWAYS` and lower order to precede Copy. Read selection with `evaluateJavascript` before `mode.finish()`; finishing first clears it and returns empty.
- `scripts/mock/mock_hub.py` with `MOCK_RICH=1 MOCK_LINKS=1` adds links to chat, Persona main chat and Inbox.
- Paparazzi renders one frame: synchronous state changes in `LaunchedEffect` appear, network/delay-dependent ones don't. Pages must preload data: hub status in `Hub`
  StateFlow (test `refresh()` first), chat in `hub.chat(key)` (wait `loading = false`), assistant in `AssistantModel` (test `reload()` before `ShellRoot`).
  Leaving chat calls `hub.release(key)`; preload again before second dark shot. Page network responses may arrive before screenshot and overwrite injected data:
  injected data must match mock results. Paparazzi reports retain old runs; delete before copying by name (`android.sh shots` does).
- Paparazzi/layoutlib WebView is a stub: class exists, `getSettings()` doesn't, causing `NoSuchMethodError`; no `LocalInspectionMode` / `isInEditMode` either.
  Screenshot WebViews (mail) reflectively check for `getSettings`; otherwise draw substitute (`MailWebView` uses `MailHtml.toText`).
- No Activity in Paparazzi: `rememberLauncherForActivityResult` needs `LocalActivityResultRegistryOwner`, `BackHandler` needs `LocalOnBackPressedDispatcherOwner`,
  faked by `ShotHost`; no `PersonaApp.instance` (composition reads need `runCatching`); services like `PowerManager.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations` throw `Unsupported Service`.
- Persistent two-page shell: top page root needs `pointerInput`, or blank-area touches fall through; hidden page uses `clearAndSetSemantics` to hide from accessibility.
  Later children in Box are above earlier ones unless `zIndex` changes it; page `zIndex(1)` requires top bar `zIndex(2)` or hides it (the initial bug).
- Two weights divide remaining width: title `weight(1f, fill = false)` plus `Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))` limits title to half and truncates on short screens.
  Put title and adjacent small elements in one `weight(1f)` Row, time outside.
- Chinese lines are taller (Noto CJK line spacing); same-size bullet "•" floats above Chinese. Both marker/text use `alignByBaseline()`.
- `WindowInsets.statusBars.asPaddingValues()` returns raw values regardless of outer consumption: pages computing top padding (`AgentsScreen`, shell chat)
  must be in a pane starting at window top; `statusBarsPadding()` pages (`PageHeader`, chat top bar) go in a pane offset only by shell bar height, adding status-bar space themselves.
- Moving pages in composition (single ↔ two panes) loses `remember` / `rememberSaveable`; cross-layout state like drafts belongs outside the page.

- Hub stores one phone_* list (`setPhoneTools`), replaced by every `register_phone`; calls go to last registered `phoneClient`.
  Android tools must exactly match iOS, no extras or parameter changes. Since Android 10 only foreground Apps can read clipboard; background `phone_clipboard_get` gets empty and explains in result.
- With two connected phones, phone_* goes to last registered: close Persona on physical phone when testing emulator tools, or calls reach that phone and time out if its data source is off.
  Claude Code reads tool list only on startup; newly added tools need a new session.
- Emulator has no writable calendar account by default; `phone_calendar_create_event` says "no writable calendar". Create a LOCAL calendar with `content insert` for tests.
- Opening a pairing link while paired: clear `pendingPair` only after pairing completes. Earlier clearing makes Root switch back immediately, disposes pairing page/coroutine, and loses the link.
- Headless (`-no-window`) emulator `adb exec-out screencap` sometimes returns previous frame: immediate shot looks unresponsive although navigation occurred. Capture twice, one second apart.
- Read-only real-hub UI inspection: pairing adds a device with `POST /api/v1/auth/tokens`. Debug can `run-as` write `shared_prefs/pairing.xml` (`url`, `token`) directly, launch, then `pm clear`; don't tap hub-writing switches (Claude Code settings, notifications, phone data).
- Byte-based chart y-axis rounds in 1024 (`ChartMath.yTicks(binary = true)`); decimal rounding shows "1.91 MB/s", "3.81 MB/s". Measure label width from widest label; fixed width clips.
- Mock hub (`scripts/mock/mock_hub.py`) does not push pending interactions on WS connect; pull to refresh once after opening.
- Lark screenshots (`LarkShots`) need `MOCK_LARK=1` (message types, emoji library, images). Images decode in background and miss first frame. Capture once with same name to trigger loads, wait `LarkMedia.settle()` (no downloads), then actual shot; nested-image link previews may need 2–3 cycles. Default mock f2/f3 are separate data (iOS screenshots, TranslationTests, fully rendered f3); `MOCK_LARK=1` replaces these chats with this fixture; sets do not affect each other.
- Inbox details use `JSheet` (ModalBottomSheet, separate window), above link cards/image viewer. Chats needing links/images should be pushed pages over list, not sheets; profile/topic/Moments sheets close themselves before opening links/images (`LarkActions.closing`).

## App shell and direct page entry

The composition root uses `PersonaTheme` from `:core:designsystem`, style from `AppStyleStore` (Settings › Interface style). Four fixed bottom tabs: Persona / Agents / Inbox / Settings, identical order and text in all styles, styled independently (Glass floating dock, Brief/Board bottom bar). Dock belongs to each tab's first page; pushed pages (chat, documents, nested settings) cover it. Visited tabs retain composition and do not rebuild on return (`docs/android/android-design.md` section 3). Tablet/foldable layout follows the window: a regular window (at least 600dp wide and 480dp tall) has the sidebar column beside the Assistant page and two-pane list/detail for Agents, Inbox and Settings; tabletop puts chat above and composer below (`docs/android/android-design.md` section 3.1). Each tab retains its back stack. Agents badge counts ordinary-Agent pending interactions, filtered by hub session/space mute and exception rules; Persona badge counts assistant interactions, each request counted separately even in one session. Inbox badge counts unhandled Needs-you items and unmuted requests. Current tab, Agents machine filter and each tab's open chat are stored in pairing directory and restored on normal cold start; sign-out, revoke and re-pair delete together, and retired writers cannot recreate position files. Legacy global shell preferences/checklist records are cleared on unpairing.

Connection state occupies each page's title (Agents, Inbox, Persona, chat); shell no longer adds a separate banner, avoiding duplication and top whitespace. Lightweight refresh failures show no extra notice; user-action failures go through one outlet, with a shared 4-second dedupe window for offline notices. This is notice-policy configuration, not measured performance. Unclear results retain in-chat checking.

Second-batch module entries:

- An Agent's chat opens its terminal as a cover over the chat (`TerminalCover` in `feature/terminal`, iOS `TerminalCover`, #4339 C7): ⋯'s first item **Terminal** in every style, the mark at the end of the live work line on Glass, a request's or a refused message's **Open terminal**, and on Brief and Board the header's button they keep (Glass's header has none, as iOS's: owner decision 11 of the Android alignment plan).
