It comes up under the status bar with no title; its bar has a handle, the read-only / control chip, what it shows ("Updates every second"), Select text, Display and **Close terminal**. Dragging the bar (or the not-running state) moves it down; let go past 140dp, or flung, it closes, otherwise it springs back; Back closes it. The chat stays mounted under it and counts as covered (no read receipts). Not for assistant chats. Notification fallback (`persona://open?target=terminal&machine=…&session=…`), Terminal's pane list and the Agents page's request cards still open the terminal as its own page over the original chat.
- Inbox item/Lark deep links (`target=inbox&item=…`, `target=lark&chat=…`) wait for list refresh, then open the same Lark/mail page as list taps; missing items show explanation.
- Agents List settings › Machine monitoring opens machine list; gauge button at each computer header opens that computer's detail.
- Standalone terminal (#2972, `docs/integrations/terminal.md` section 19): with hub `terminal_panes`, List settings gains Terminal below Machine monitoring (like web sidebar Terminal below Machines), listing online computer → space → tab → pane (`GET …/machines/{mid}/panes`, every 8 seconds in foreground). Agent panes show title/state and open by session id; shell panes open same terminal with `pane:<pane>` (`TerminalAddress`; `/keys` doesn't recognize addresses and read-only typing reports not sent). New tab beside space sends `POST …/panes {workspace_id, kind}` then opens new pane. Multiple hubs route by machine suffix (`MergedShellServices.panes`); suffixed-machine terminal uses that group's connection/features (`groupTerminal`, strip suffix for subscription/input, restore on events). Keys use "host type/space id", preventing herdr/orca `w1` collisions on one computer. Close/split/rename not implemented.
- Confirmed Agent closure from list/chat removes its chat/request/terminal pages from every tab; unknown result keeps pages for checking. Sharing targets use Agents `agentRank`, then time and stable identity within state.
- Chat ⋯ opens Files; once confirmed repository, shows Git and change count. Files/Git switch within original chat; path insertion preserves draft/valid selection and returns to composer.
- Persona drawer Knowledge opens profiles/search/details; links open in App.
- Settings › Phone data opens permissions/toggles. Foreground state plus actual foreground-service state determine availability; location/clipboard still require foreground. Old pairings without device identity require re-pairing.
- Settings › Assistant (`SettingsPage.Assistant`) opens Persona settings, also Persona title menu Details and top-right avatar; see above.
- Unpaired users can choose "No hub? Try it first". Demo mode has isolated data/cache/outbox and can be exited in Settings; terminal/monitoring/Knowledge without samples explain and never call real hub.

Debug entry and launch parsing adapters exist only in `src/debug/`, reuse `:core:navigation` debug parser; Release registers no development entry. Replace example package with actual Application ID:

```sh
adb -s emulator-5600 shell am start -W -n com.example.persona/persona.android.debug.DebugEntryActivity --es debug.openTab Agents
adb -s emulator-5600 shell am start -W -n com.example.persona/persona.android.debug.DebugEntryActivity --es debug.openSession machine/session --es debug.conversationKind agent
adb -s emulator-5600 shell am start -W -n com.example.persona/persona.android.debug.DebugEntryActivity --es debug.openRequest request --es debug.session machine/session --es debug.conversationKind agent
adb -s emulator-5600 shell am start -W -n com.example.persona/persona.android.debug.DebugEntryActivity --es debug.openPairing true
adb -s emulator-5600 shell am start -W -n com.example.persona/persona.android.debug.DebugEntryActivity --es debug.openDiagnostics true
```

Pass only one `debug.open…` target at a time. `debug.openTab` compatibility values: `Persona` (Persona assistant), `Agents`, `Inbox`, `Settings`; `debug.conversationKind` agent/assistant; `debug.openCatalog true` opens component catalog; `debug.openLink '<persona:// link>'` opens any deep link by MainActivity rules (terminal fallback, notification tab, Inbox item, Settings subpage); `debug.openPage` machines/knowledge/phone/catalog for pages without public deep links. Links with `&` need another single-quote layer because adb shell hands the command to device shell. `debug.theme` light / dark affects App only (including per-page PersonaTheme), not emulator system. `debug.style` glass/brief/board changes and saves style like Settings. `debug.posture` tabletop/book simulates half-fold hinge at window center for screenshots (android-35 Pixel Fold emulator reports no FoldingFeature).

State previews use `debug.state`, parsed only in debug. Use an unpaired dedicated test package; entry starts local demo and skips checklist without real-hub access. Paired packages are not automatically unpaired/replaced; use a separate test package. State stays fixed despite refresh; restart without argument returns to normal demo. Existing target/theme arguments such as `debug.openTab`, `debug.openSession`, `debug.theme` can accompany it, still only one target; cannot combine with `debug.mockAddress`.

| `debug.state` | Default target | Screenshot state |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `offline` | Agents | Waiting for network, demo list retained |
| `connecting` | Agents | Connecting |
| `updating` | Agents | Updating |
| `pairing-expired` | Agents | Invalid pairing notice, cached samples retained |
| `certificate-mismatch` | Agents | Certificate-change notice |
| `local-permission` | Agents | Local network permission notice |
| `empty` | Agents | Empty session list; specify Inbox for empty Inbox |
| `loading` | Inbox | Persistent loading; chat target also supported |
| `load-failed` | Inbox | Load failure and Retry; chat target also supported |

```sh
adb -s emulator-5600 shell am force-stop com.example.persona
adb -s emulator-5600 shell am start -W -n com.example.persona/persona.android.debug.DebugEntryActivity --es debug.state offline --es debug.theme dark
adb -s emulator-5600 exec-out screencap -p > /tmp/persona-state-offline-dark.png
```

State fixtures/argument names are in `app/src/debug/`; production registers none. Wait for target page before capture; fixed states do not measure connection/loading performance, which is unmeasured.

This round: `./gradlew --no-daemon -q :app:assembleDebug :app:testDebugUnitTest` passed, 14 App tests. In-process Kotlin compilation recheck and separate-package build also passed. Dedicated-emulator screenshots of all 9 states × light / dark, 18 images, `/tmp/persona-p5-root-shots/`; spot-checked offline, empty, invalid pairing, load failure. Release artifact checks, real session closure and unpairing await integration acceptance.

Debug Settings footer also has Component catalog (debug), hidden during `debug.automation`. Catalog route/page compile only in `src/debug/`, absent in Release. Legacy `--es openSession machine/session` supported. Notifications, public deep links and debug arguments all converge on one router; old notifications wait for session metadata before routing.

## To-dos: iOS parity (#3034)

Code in `feature/agents/` (`TodosScreen`, `TodoDetailScreen`, `TodoPickerSheet`) and `TasksStore`, `sort/AgentList.kt` in `core/domain`.

- Local cache (#4533, aligned with iOS `TodoStore` StateCache): every hub read (old-hub 404 means empty) writes the pairing list snapshot (`todos` in `lists`). Cold start/offline first show prior to-dos, then hub response wins. Cache does not count as a hub response; attaching to a to-do still waits for first real read.
- List: incomplete rows show one dynamic line below title, ordered like iOS `TodoRow`: auto-completion reason, most urgent Agent's latest line (red when waiting), one-line description, otherwise "Not handed to an Agent yet". Completed rows swipe right to reopen, left to delete, with auto-completion reason. Empty non-search state has **New to-do**. Urgent summary matches iOS `mostUrgentSummary`: failed start with reason gives "Start failed: reason", otherwise latest line (Markdown stripped), session title, Agent title, then description.
- Detail: after status tag "N Agents · Created by X"; Projects lists attached workspaces (project · computer; "No Agent now" if none alive; Remove uses `DELETE /api/v1/todos/{id}/spaces/{mid}/{space}`). Each Agent shows computer · space · tab, origin (with project / dispatched by X / derived / same group, like iOS `TodosLogic.agentSource`) and latest line. Old hub with no `spaces` on any to-do hides projects and space-menu entry.
- Attach: space ⋯ offers Attach to to-do… / Move to another to-do… / Remove from to-do (`PUT` / `DELETE …/spaces/…`; name from hub `space_name`, otherwise id); attached header shows "To-do: title". Shared session/space picker has New to-do… → title → Create and attach, and Remove when attached. `TasksStore.setTodoSpace` optimistically moves project locally (one to-do per project, removing project-following Agents from old to-do), rolls back on failure, then reloads. Only available after a hub to-do read with project support (`spaces`). Route-level 404 on attach (no code, or `not_found` + "Not Found", like iOS `TodosLogic.isLegacySpacesRoute`) means legacy hub, then hides entries.
- By to-do: manually opened herdr sessions without to-do (`space_kind == "herdr"`, not Persona-opened) get expanded **Manually opened** section before **No to-do**. No to-do defaults collapsed if there are to-do sections above and none need you (web `buildTodoAgentGroups`); manual expansion remembered as `expandedIdle` `todo-none`.
- Three-level to-do tree (#3156, web #2698): project › to-do › session. Project inferred from repositories of to-do sessions (`repoOf`, merged across computers; most sessions wins, alphabetical tie-break, no cwd → Other). Needs-you projects first, then working, Other last; project header "N to-dos", to-do indented, trailing arrow opens detail; session retains computer/space. Manually opened/No to-do last, with another project level and unchanged collapse rules. Every level collapsible (`todo-project/<repo>`, `todo-loose/<section id>/<repo>`). Derived-Agent indentation and branches completed a day ago collapsing by default are not yet implemented on Android. Screenshots `AgentsTodoTreeShotsTest`.
- Launcher shortcut: New to-do (`ShortcutPublisher`, `persona://open?target=new-todo` → `NewTodoRoute`, list opens with create sheet already up), aligned with iOS `AddTodoIntent`. Every use brings the sheet up, also when the list from an earlier use is still on the stack: `restore` raises a one-shot `NewTodoOpen` request that the list takes, like `AssistantComposerFocus` (#5718; tests `NewTodoRouteTest`, `NewTodoSheetTest`).
- Calendar (#2046, the month; #4200, the day and the week; rules in `docs/assistant/todos.md` "Calendar"): List | Calendar at the top of the list, and in the calendar Day | Week | Month beside it (on a narrow phone on a line of its own; `TodosViewBar`, kept on the device with Show done, `TodoCalendarPrefs`, the month until another is picked). The month (`TodoCalendarMonth`) shows the to-dos with a due date from the list the page already has: Today, previous / next and a sideways swipe; weeks from the app language's region (`weekStart`); a day's to-dos in up to three lines, or two and "+N", each on a thin edge in its state's color on a neutral ground (`docs/android/android-design.md` 2.3.1), red once overdue, faded and struck through when done; days of the months around faded; a tap on a day lists its to-dos in a sheet, where one opens; "N without a due date · See list" under it goes back to the list. A long press on a to-do in a day picks it up and a drop on another day moves it there at the same time (#2045; `TasksStore.setTodoDue`, `PATCH` with `due_at` or `due_date`), with the shell's "Moved to …" and Undo; TalkBack's "Change Due Date…" on a day and on a row of its sheet does the same without dragging (rules in `docs/assistant/todos.md` "Moving one"). Logic in `core/domain/.../tasks/TodoCalendar.kt` (`TodoCalendarTest`), sizes in `CalendarTokens`; `TodoCalendarDragTest` (drop, Undo, a drop in place, a failed save, a swipe, "Change Due Date…"); shots `TodoCalendarShotsTest` (month, a day's sheet, a to-do before / while / after it is moved, the "Change Due Date" sheet; three styles × light / dark, `feature/agents/build/i18n-shots/todo-calendar-*.png`). The day and the week (`TodoCalendarTimeline.kt`, as iOS `TodoCalendarTimeline` / `TodoCalendarWeekStrip`): the day view has its week's strip (a dot per open to-do, up to three), the week a header of its days; both an All Day row above an hour axis that opens an hour before the first timed to-do or an hour and a half before now, whichever is earlier (08:00 with neither), with today's red line and the time in the gutter; every block is `CalendarTokens.block` high at its due moment, overlapping ones side by side in lanes. A long press moves a block to another quarter hour, day or All Day row with the month's Undo; a tap on the empty axis or All Day row brings up `TodoQuickAddSheet` (title only, due at the half hour tapped or that day), as does the page's "+" in the calendar (due on the day shown, all day); a failed save keeps the sheet and what was typed. With larger text the hours' column and the dates' circles grow with it, up to `CalendarTokens.textScaleMaximum`; on a phone's week the blocks then show only "…" (TalkBack still reads each). `TodoCalendarAxisTest`; shots `dayview`, `week`, `weekdrag`, `new`, and `-large` (the largest text) of the day and the week.
- List filter and sort (#3740, the rules of iOS, `docs/assistant/todos.md`): beside List | Calendar, a filter button (filled while the filter or the sort is not the default) opens a menu, Show (All / No due date / Today / This week / This month, each with how many it would show) and Sort by (Recent activity / Due date / Priority); an active filter shows as a "This week ×" chip beside it. Today, This week and This month also take open to-dos already overdue; the week is the calendar's (`weekStart`). A search, then the filter, pick the to-dos and the sections place what is left; with a filter, Other open unfolds and "N more waiting on you, not in This week · Show all" says what it leaves out of Needs you; a sort orders the rows inside each section. Kept on the device (`TodoListPrefs`), worked out on it (`core/domain/.../tasks/TodoListFilter.kt`, `TodoListFilterTest`); no request. The list has no "N open · N shelved · N done" line (#3712): each section's title carries its count in a capsule of its state's tone (`JSectionHeader` `countTone`). Shots `TodoListFilterShotsTest` (after, the menu, each filter, both sorts, an empty filter; three styles × light / dark). Searching doesn't unfold the folded sections on Android (iOS does). From a hub with the direction tree (#4352) the menu starts with **Group by**: Status (the default) | Pinned (常驻), kept as `TodoListPrefs.grouping`; by direction the list has one section per direction of the tree in its order (groups with no to-do left out; **Secretary** for the secretary group), its header the name (an unnamed lead reads as an untitled chat, as in the drawer) and what it has going as the drawer's row shows it (`JDirectionCounts`, #6626: the working spinner while the tree's `running`, then "N need you"; nothing when neither), folding like the status sections; the rows are the usual to-do rows for the tree's to-do ids in the tree's order (what the search and filter leave), and every to-do the tree does not list (shelved ones, Completed, any other) keeps its status section after the directions, so none goes missing (`DirectionTree.todoRows`, `TriageResult.without`, `DirectionTreeTest`). An older hub has no Group by.
- Screenshots `TodosParityShotsTest` (list, empty, detail, create-and-attach, three styles × light / dark, `feature/agents/build/i18n-shots/todos-*.png`); tests `TodoDataTest` (manual section/default collapse), `TaskDockTest` (project attachment), `HttpTasksApiTest`, `RouterTest`, `ShortcutTextTest`.

## Unified main-chat entry (#930)

Same design as iOS/web ([unified-entry.md](../assistant/unified-entry.md)); code stays in new files where possible, with only a few hooks in chat:

| Part | Location |
| --- | --- |
| Wire types: plans, chat activity, to-dos, `todos` / `assistant_feed` / `task_plan` events, question `task_title` | `Tasks.kt`, `ServerEvent.kt`, `Models.kt` in `core/contracts` |
| API | `core/contracts/api/TasksApi.kt`, `core/hub/HttpTasksApi.kt` |
| State (to-dos, plans, per-chat activity, routing, sending) and pure logic (`AssistantLines`: activity at end of its turn, quick results after work rows, daily date row; `TaskState`; `QuickResult`) | `core/domain/tasks/Tasks.kt`, test `AssistantLinesTest` |
| New chat row `ChatLine.Extra(id, payload)`, rendered by chat host | `core/domain/lines/LineModels.kt` |
| Cards: plan, task (older-than-today collapsed to one line), receipt, answered question, routing bubble, Today, scheduled Brief, message, quick result, date; task dock and route tag above composer | `feature/chat/AssistantTasks.kt`, `feature/chat/BriefCard.kt`; lossy Brief body decoding in `core/contracts/Brief.kt` |
| Hooks: `ChatChromeBindings.tasks` (only assistant chats in `ChatDestination`), row merge in `ChatPage`, task-Agent questions merged into existing `requests` panel, routing checked before sending, bottom task dock | `feature/chat/ChatScreen.kt`, `ChatEntries.kt`, `app/di/ChatWiringModule.kt` |

- A scheduled **Brief** keeps its `brief` kind through the Wire decoder and draws waiting, done/failed, acceptance, later questions and progress in the same feed position. Open follows the row's own chat or to-do; Accept / Got It / Take Recommendation sends the displayed `asked_at`, Complete Directly completes only that still-current decision round and shares the ordinary task completion's local Todo/cache merge, and Handled posts to `/api/v1/later/{id}/handled`. All writes retain the conversation's manager. Known code stages use the localized `StepProgress`; general tasks omit the code-stage bar and unknown stage names retain the hub's words. Missing optional sections stay empty and one malformed row does not discard the rest. The first briefing of a week ends with the hub's recommended-setup lines ("Rule suggestions, held decisions, recommended setup and purpose descriptions (#5802)" above). `BriefCardShotsTest` covers the real feed, actions, three styles in light/dark and 200% text; the fake hub's `feed_brief` supplies the emulator fixture.
- Task details (#3034, aligned with iOS `TaskDetailSheet`; the web has none since #5096 and opens the Agent's page): task cards/rows/dock, receipt View, tasks in Today/message cards all raise details from bottom (`feature/chat/AssistantTaskSheets.kt`) instead of leaving main chat: title, state, Remove task in ⋯ when not running, close. Multiple Agents have top chips. Agent section shows computer · directory · type, current work, sub-Agents, then Open chat (full Agent page) and Say something (route to it). Before Agent exists: Starting Agent; failure shows reason, Retry, Remove. Difference: iOS embeds full Agent chat and underlying chat stays usable at 0.6 height; Android uses modal `JSheet`, with chat behind Open chat. Details render at chat-page level (`ChatScreen`, independent of composer), so read-only assistant chats can open them too; failed-start Remove confirms first.
- A receipt's **Look** opens task details from elsewhere. Inside that same task's sheet it dismisses the sheet and opens the task's lead Agent chat; Back returns to the Assistant without reopening details. The durable task binding remains navigable when its computer is offline or its session is outside the current list. Without a valid Agent binding, an inline notice stays visible in the sheet. This navigation does not retry, complete or dismiss a task. `TaskReceiptNavigationTest` covers the receipt separately from the footer's **Open chat**, including interrupted receipts, invalid bindings, multiple Agents and read-only state.
- Task dock (iOS `TaskDock`): fixed left **All N** opens Tasks sheet (Needs you / Working / Finished / Stopped / Completed / Failed to start sections, long-press removes), then horizontally scrolling task capsules; non-running tasks removable by long press (`JChip.onLongClick`). Main chat also shows manually opened working/needs-you Agent capsules (`dockAgents` in `core/domain`, iOS `DockAgent.shown`: not an Agent of any incomplete task; hub-tagged to-dos qualify only if manually created; no subtask while the dock shows the agent above it, see **Subtasks** in "Aligning the Agents list and the Machines page (#3038)"), opening Agent sheet ("Agent · Not opened from chat"). `TasksStore.mainConversation` records main id; if opened chat differs, requery hub to follow new main, computer changes or another device's change (#4690). Tasks sheet task rows show computer · directory · Agent · current work (failure reason instead if start failed); Agent rows show computer · directory · Agent · latest line, like iOS `TaskBoardSheet`.
- Plan **Edit** (iOS `PlanEditSheet`): before start each task can edit title, computer (hub computer chips), directory, model (empty default), permissions (Ask every step / Allow file edits / Plan first / Allow all), first message. Start sends `tasks` via `POST /api/v1/task-plans/{id}/start` (`TasksApi.startPlan(id, remember, tasks)`). With edits, pulling down, outside tap or Back asks "Discard these changes?" (`JSheet.onBlockedDismiss`), like iOS `.sheetCancel(unsaved:)`. The sheet closes only once the start succeeds; while it is on its way Start spins, and a failed start (e.g. `task_no_machine`) keeps the sheet and its edits (#5312); test `PlanEditSheetTest`.
- Screenshots `TaskSheetsShotsTest` (dock, two-Agent detail, starting, all, Agent sheet, edit; three styles × light / dark, `feature/chat/build/i18n-shots/tasks-*.png`); test `TaskDockTest` in `core/domain`.
- Quick results have no Undo on Android (reminders/events are on iPhone), labeled "On iPhone".
- Multiple hubs: only current-group tasks (unsuffixed machine ids) match sessions; other-group cards show Starting, questions aren't merged; supporting them requires suffixing to-do machine ids in `GroupTranslation`.
- Screenshots: export `MOCK_TASKS=1` (optionally `MOCK_TASKS_AT`, `MOCK_TASKS_NO_ASKS`) then `./scripts/app/android.sh shots`; assistant-chat shot is main chat.

## Assistant cards: actions, live cards, HTML (#2022)

Specification in [assistant-views.md](../assistant/assistant-views.md). Uses the extension renderer (`ExtensionViewColumn`), not separate components; each state only changes the page passed to it (`AssistantCardPresentation`).

| Part | Location |
| --- | --- |
| Feed `views` / `view_actions` / `view_blocks` (raw per entry, a bad card doesn't affect others), `assistant_view` / `assistant_view_action` / `assistant_view_block` events, `view` activity | `Tasks.kt`, `ServerEvent.kt` in `core/contracts` |
| Action/HTML bridge requests (generated `ExtensionViewActionInput` / `ExtensionAssistantHTMLActionInput`), two 409 results (`view_changed` reads version/card from `ExtensionViewChangedError`; `already_answered` reads existing answer) | `core/contracts/api/AssistantViewActions.kt`, `core/hub/HttpTasksApi.kt` |
| Fences/keys (matching `sdk/go/wire/assistantblocks.go`, `assistant-text/fences.json`), card validation (matching `CheckAssistantView`, generated `ExtensionWire`), `open` targets, trailing `<persona-action>` | `core/domain/views/` |
| Per-hub state (`TasksStore.cards`), separated by chat: live versions and answer revisions only advance, snapshots fill gaps but never regress; `AssistantCardActions` sends actions | `core/domain/views/AssistantViewStore.kt` |
| Card: page stack, fields, confirmation, selected / sending / failed, HTML | `feature/settings/AssistantViewCard.kt`, `AssistantCardPresentation.kt`, `AssistantHtml.kt` |
| Hooks: body split by fence (`ChatMarkdownCards`, keys numbered across text item), `view` activity renders live card, `LocalAssistantCards` provided only to assistant chats | `feature/chat/ChatContent.kt`, `StructuredContent.kt`, `AssistantTasks.kt`, `ChatScreen.kt` |

- Body-card key `<item id>/<n>` counts `persona-view` blocks across whole text item (bad blocks count, `persona-card` and examples inside other code fences don't), version fixed 1;
  answering requires hub registration (`view_blocks` or event). Streaming, unregistered, read-only device/chat only render. Live key `view_…`, version from hub.
- Answer once: reply carries visible `expected_version`; `confirm` shows native confirmation with wording and raw outgoing text; version changes during confirmation close dialog and ask to confirm again.
  409 `view_changed`: fetch feed once, redraw in place preserving filled fields/page, never auto-resend new version; 409 `already_answered`: fetch feed to show existing answer.
  Retries reuse `client_msg_id`, changing only when content/version changes. After answer selected option marked Selected, fields become read-only rows, status "Selected, sending / Sent / Send failed: reason"; open/push/pop remain usable.
- `open`: https opens App link card; `persona://session/…` converts to App link; task/kb/file currently say "This target isn't available on Android yet", never hand to another App.
- Exact hub-format trailing `<persona-action view=… action=…>{"values":{…}}</persona-action>` in user message is hidden and bubble labeled From card; other positions, extra attributes or non-string values are not recognized.
- Answers require hello `assistant_views` (`HttpTasksApi.features` follows same connection's `serverFeatures`): reply, submit, search, filter, load more;
  HTML bridge also needs `assistant_html_bridge`. An old hub (one with its `persona_view` switch off included, from before #5509), or no hello (unknown capabilities) means render-only, no requests (`AssistantCardActions` also blocks with `Unsupported`);
  open/push/pop local actions still work. A later hello adding capability makes existing card actionable in place.
- Search/filter/load more are card replies. Extension `ExtensionSearchBar` records `q` while typing, no debounce auto-send; adjacent Search (`SearchSubmit`, absent if extension supplies none, preserving extension behavior) sends search action + `q`; selecting filter sends `q` and filter value.
  Submit includes all page fields, `q`, filters per hub rules (`AssistantCard.fields`). Load more is a tap-only button (extension load-more auto-requests when visible, not allowed here).
  Answered cards hide search/filters.
- 403 with code fails only that request, not pairing (`forbidden`: read-only device/disabled card; `permission_denied`; `declined`; expired install-preview, or a used one once its install's operation is gone, `this_machine_only`; credential restrictions `master_token_local_only`, etc., #4692). `tls_required` means TLS required, not revocation (`HubFailure.from`, `HubFailureResponseTest`, `HubConnectionLivenessTest.aRefusedRequestDoesNotRevokeThePairing`, `HubHttpRoutingTest.aRefusedCardDoesNotRevokeThePairing`).
  Only 401 and code-less 403 (legacy hub) revoke pairing; `ApiClient` interceptor revokes only on 401. Composer Interrupt still treats any 403 as expired pairing, not changed here.
- **HTML**: `AssistantHtmlSandbox.Accepted = false`; all html components show "This page doesn't run on Android yet" and fallback. Sandbox code exists pending acceptance:
  separate WebView (separate Profile when supported), data: document (opaque origin), CSP before page content, `blockNetworkLoads`, empty responses for every `shouldInterceptRequest`, all navigation refused,
  no windows/dialogs/permissions, third-party cookies denied only for this WebView (no global changes); bridge only `addWebMessageListener`, current-instance main-frame messages only, one confirmation at a time, stop asking after two denials;
  at most 2 concurrent, destroyed off-screen, Stop/Retry, renderer exit stops only this page. **Pending acceptance** (no emulator on this development machine): observe real-WebView request counts for fetch, XHR, images, CSS, iframe,
  WebSocket, beacon, navigation, meta refresh, popup, forms, cookies/storage, forged bridge messages, confirmation floods (with unprotected control); whether `addWebMessageListener` `*` injects for opaque origins;
  infinite loops/large allocations: a page loop occupies the App's shared renderer and stalls link cards; Stop only destroys this WebView, not a hard CPU/memory limit. All must pass before `Accepted` becomes true.
- Screenshots `./gradlew :feature:settings:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*AssistantCardsShotsTest*'`, three styles × light / dark: pending (decision/form), answered (sent/failed/live-updated),
  fallback (HTML, streaming block, comparison table), PNGs `build/assistant-card-shots/`. Same class tests outgoing tap requests, confirmation/reconfirmation after version change, no send for unregistered blocks.
- Tests `AssistantFencesTest`, `AssistantCardsTest`, `AssistantViewStoreTest`, `PersonaActionTagTest` (`core:domain`), `TasksContractTest.theCardsSnapshotDecodes`,
  three card tests in `HttpTasksApiTest`, `AssistantHtmlTest` (checks configuration/input only; Robolectric WebView does not run pages and is not acceptance evidence).

## Chat content: photos, files, long-press menu, tool details, error cards

Ported from old main-line App chat code (d2d484ef, 4588285d) on 2026-11-02, redrawn with the new design system; behavior aligned with iOS. Image viewing/markup is above; files below in "Files and Git in chat".

- Sent photos: paths in `<attached_images>` (and older trailing "相关图片：…", "Related images: …", or "（我从手机发来了图片，请用 Read 查看：…）", "I sent images from my phone; use Read: …") render as square thumbnails inside bubbles (one large, multiple small, wrapping). These annotations and Claude Code `[Image #N]` are stripped from visible text; photo-only bubbles contain only photos. `AttachedPhotos` reads from the session computer (`GET /api/v1/machines/{mid}/file`, 40 encoded images and 40 thumbnails cached in memory), spinner while loading, Load failed on missing/non-image/>2 MB truncation, tap retry; viewer pages through this message's photos. Previously `cleanText` replaced whole tags with "[图片]" ("[image]"), so history lost photos; now only one-line previews (`cleanText`) still do. Persona chats also show them (`ChatAppBindings.files`); no session directory uses `~` because paths are absolute.
- Sent video / audio / files (#2981, iOS `AttachedFileCards`, web `FileCards`): `<persona-file kind path name size duration/>` parsed by `parseAttachedFiles` in `core/domain`, stripped from bubble text, shown above as design-system `FileAttachmentCard` (play / waveform / file icon, name, size · duration). Tap opens file page (video/audio playback there), long-press copies path (TalkBack Copy path); no empty bubble for file-only message. Duration rounds to seconds like iOS. Edit/resend restores tags unchanged, retaining files.
- Documents mentioned in answers (#2981, iOS `DocumentCards` #1543): Word / Excel / PowerPoint / iWork / RTF / PDF that really exist (`PathPresence`), max 6, horizontal cards below answer: hub `file/thumb` first-page thumbnail (`DocumentCardThumbnail`) left, name/type (Word document…) right; tap file page, long-press copy path; other files remain chips. Screenshots `ChatFileCardsShotsTest`, three styles × light / dark, `build/file-card-shots/`.
- Code blocks, inline code and quotes in replies (#2984, as Telegram draws them; `feature/chat/ReplyBlocks.kt`, iOS and web the same): a fence is one block whose title bar (the language, none when the fence names none, and Copy) is inside its box in every style (`JCodeBlock`'s `header`); Copy copies the whole block and says "Copied" with the success haptic (tool output's Copy says so too now). Inline code is the code face in the body's color on the well, and a tap copies it (`copyingCode`, a `LinkAnnotation.Clickable` through the renderer's annotator); inside a link (a path that opens) it stays the link's. A quote is the accent bar on an `accentSoft` card with a quote mark at its top end (`ReplyQuote`; the renderer's own bar is off); one longer than about four lines folds with its end faded, a tap opens it and its chevron folds it again, and a reply still being written (`LocalReplyStreaming`) never folds. Shots: `ReplyBlocksShotsTest` (`build/reply-block-shots/`).
- Paths (#1694, web #1669, iOS `FilePresence`): **link only paths that exist on that computer.** Path-like text in messages/replies (`PathRefs`: `~/x`, `/x/y` with at least two components, relative paths with directory and letter-starting extension; suffix line references `:12`, `:12:5`, `:12-20`, `#L12`; whole backtick spans can be paths with Chinese/spaces; excludes links, model names like `deepseek/v4.1-flash`, final punctuation) is batch-checked with `GET /api/v1/machines/{mid}/stat` (`PathPresence`, relative to session directory, simultaneous rows coalesced). Only file / dir / denied render linked: user messages via `messageLinkAnnotations`, Markdown rewritten to `[path](persona-path:…)` (`PathLinks.markdown`, leaving fences/indented code/existing links alone, whole-path inline code becomes link). `PathLinks.handler` intercepts without web card. Before results or unavailable (old node/offline), plain text. Cache: exists 30 minutes, missing 2 (Agent may create soon), failed 1. File tags below (`FilePaths`, max 6, filename/type icon) also only existing. Tap opens file directly (`ChatChromeBindings.onOpenPath` → `SessionFilesScreen(initialPath)`), completing relative path against session directory. Line references (`a.kt:12`, `a.kt#L12`, `a.kt:12:3`) open source, scroll line to upper third, accent-highlight (iOS `FileViewer.goTo(line)`; link `persona-path:…#L12`, `onOpenPath(path, line)` → `SessionFilesScreen(initialLine)` → `FileSelection.File.line` → `FileText(focusLine)`). Whole file read, no paging; >2 MB truncated, out-of-range line stops at last. Tool file headers above edits and Read paths also clickable without check (tool just used them). Only chats with file opening: Agent chat with directory; Persona chats lack file page and show no tags/links.
- Agent goal (#2888, iOS #2420, `docs/integrations/agent-goal.md`): session `goal` (Claude Code `/goal`, Codex thread goal) adds a strip below top bar after sibling tabs/ports: target icon, goal first line, state Active / Achieved / Paused / Blocked / Usage limit / Budget exhausted; working blue, achieved green, others warning. Tap bottom panel: state, tokens/time used, full goal, last-check reason ("Why achieved" when done). No goal, no strip. `GoalBarShotsTest`, three styles × light / dark, `build/goal-shots/`.
- While-away recap: Claude Code writes a recap each time idle is checked, rendered "While you were away · 21 minutes ago ›"; consecutive recaps fold to one ("3 recaps · …", `foldRecaps`, like iOS). Bottom panel newest first, written-at time and selectable Markdown body. On Glass the newest recap of an Agent chat, while it is the chat's last line, is open in full as iOS's `RecapStrip` (`ChatRecapStrip.kt`: the accent bar, the title, its time and a close mark over up to eight lines of plain words; the earlier recaps of its run stay the one-line marker above it) until it is tapped or typing starts; which recap was closed is kept per chat on the device (`ClosedRecaps`, the 200 most recent once past 300, iOS `closedRecaps`). Brief and Board keep the marker.
- Truncated answers: hub truncates text over 24 KB (`full_size`); show partial body without hub's "…（已截断" ("…(truncated") line and **Full text (N KB)**, fetching `GET …/items/{id}/full` to replace in place; Loading…, failure Load failed, tap retry.
- Long-press menu: long-press/right-click your message, Agent reply (including intermediate work reply), or message from another session dims it and opens `JActionSheet`: Copy; Select (the message's words selected where they are, in the chat: handles, a long press picks a word, the system menu with Copy and Quote of what is picked; it ends when nothing is selected any more, on a tap outside the message, on Back or when the message has left the screen; `MessageSelection.kt`, `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` section 4.2); Quote (prefix each line `> ` at start of composer before existing draft); Share; Hand to Persona (message as `<persona-context>` plus "看看这条消息，告诉我该怎么处理。", "Look at this message and tell me how to handle it", to main chat, switch Persona, open once target known; absent in Persona chats); Use as context (above this composer, sent next time); Edit (your messages only, restores text and uploaded photo attachments). Message bodies no longer `SelectionContainer` so long press reaches menu; thinking/recaps remain directly selectable. Read-only sub-Agent/history chats have no Quote/context/edit (`messageMenuWrites`; non-sendable sessions disabled here once batch B capabilities merge).
- Tool details: Codex FileChange mapped by hub to `Edit` with only `diff` renders unified diff with old/new line numbers, dim `@@` and `\ No newline at end of file`; created/deleted files arrive as full contents without `@@`, all added/deleted. File header above has filename, directory, +N −N, opens file if possible. ExitPlanMode Markdown, AskUserQuestion each heading/multi-select/options, Task/Agent type and first 8 description lines (Full text for long); others key:value. File-edit tools show output only on error; success "Updated" adds nothing.
- Images: Agent images, screenshots, sent photos preserve ratio, max 280 dp (`ChatPictureFrame`) so tall screenshots don't span several screens; tap viewer, swipe all chat images; failure Load failed, tap retry.
- Images a turn's work looked at (#4339 C9, iOS `ViewedImagesRow`, docs/protocol/protocol.md "Images viewed this turn"): on Glass a strip between the turn's work line and its answer, also while it runs (`ChatLine.Viewed` from `ViewedImages` in core/domain: what tool results carried, a picture file Read opened whose result carried none, and a fold's `images`, each once in the order they came; `ViewedImagesRow.kt`): thumbnails `box64` tall in a row that scrolls sideways (`FileCardRow`), one alone `box80`, each as wide as its picture between ¾ and 1.6 of its height, a screenshot's or a web page's mark in the corner; a tap opens the viewer on the turn's images, the label is its name or what took it and the machine or its size, a long press on a file's copies its path. A file's picture is its `file/thumb`. Brief and Board leave the line out. Tests `ViewedImagesTest` (core/domain), the `tool_images_belong_to_call` lines fixture; shots `ChatDetailsMoreShotsTest` (`viewed`; Robolectric draws no decoded picture, so the tiles show their marks).
- API error cards (D6): Claude Code's final "API Error: …" replies (including preceding "Failed to authenticate. API Error: 401 …") and top-level hub "API 错误：…" ("API error: …") notices (inside work remain steps) become `ErrorCard`: plain reason (same iOS cases: GPU, overload, expired login, rate limit, timeout, connectivity, invalid parameters, 5xx…), status code, Temporary failure / Needs attention. Same errors separated only by "继续 / 重试 / continue…" ("continue / retry / continue…") merge as "N in a row" (`foldApiErrors`). Details: copyable request ID/session, time, selectable/copyable raw text. Retry only on final chat row, Agent not working, no pending messages, not a needs-attention error (expired login/invalid parameters); sends "继续" ("continue") through outbox. Both Agent/Persona chats.
- Refusal fallback (#4488, the Android half of #2528; web `ModelFallbackCard`, `RefusalNotice`): a notice with `model_refusal` is a line of its own after the exchange (`ChatLines.buildBlocks` puts it with the turn's trailing rows), saying the upstream refused, which model Claude Code switched from and to (or that no switch is recorded), "later turns may keep using it" for scope `session`, the category, the explanation and the refusal's own words. While the session has `model_fallback`, a card above the composer (`ModelFallback.kt`) names both models and the error since: a terminal session gets only the "type /model in Claude Code" step; a Persona conversation (`assistant`) gets **Back to configured model** (`ChatActions.restoreModel`, `POST …/restore-model`, no body), and the main conversation also **New main chat** (the assistant page's own dialog, through `LocalNewMainChat`, where the hub has `assistant_main_new`). While `model_restore` is pending the card says only that the next start will use the configured model and names it. The 409 codes are said in words (`restoreErrorText`); "Retry" is not offered on an API error card while `turn_error_code` is `model_fallback_unavailable`. The item cache is versioned for it: `ItemsDatabase` 4 (`MIGRATION_3_4`) drops what an older build cached, which was decoded without `model_refusal` (the chat keeps a cached item over the hub's copy, so such a notice stayed a work step); the cache keeps a refusal notice with the refusal's words as its text, never an older hub's whole line (`publicRefusalItem` in `SessionItemsCache`, as the web's). Strings in en, zh and ja (`strings_model_fallback.xml`, the web's words). Tests: `ModelRefusalContractTest`, `RefusalNoticeLineTest`, `SessionItemsCacheTest`, `ModelFallbackShotsTest` (three styles × light / dark into `build/model-fallback-shots/`). Mock: `MOCK_MODEL_FALLBACK=1 MOCK_MAIN_ACTIONS=1`.
- Current turn (#2602): every chat type, including Persona's main conversation and Agent chats, starts with one collapsed execution summary in Glass, Brief and Board. No running-step preview or tool-backed card sits outside it: tools (including Workflow, Agent/Task and every TodoWrite update), thinking and intermediate narration are inside the expandable trace. The latest assistant text is the only text outside it; each new segment replaces its predecessor in the same composition slot (`ChatLine.contentKey`), while message IDs stay unchanged for menus, search and receipts. On completion the final result stays visible and the running summary folds. Each genuine user message starts a separate visible exchange, even when queued messages share an engine turn ID: keep user-message order and one summary/latest-text slot per exchange rather than moving calls across user inputs.
  Manual expand/collapse works during the run and after completion. Historical expansion choices are stored with the chat's reading position; restored open lines fetch hub folds on demand (`openFoldsToFetch`). A running line's completion resets only that line, not historical choices (`WorkOpenState`, `toggleWork`, `settleWorkOpen` in `WorkLineOpen.kt`). Expanded traces retain usable Workflow, sub-Agent and plan cards; pending question, permission and plan-approval panels remain directly actionable outside the transcript fold. Wake notices stay in the same exchange's normal trace, including trailing calls before any new text arrives; they never divert calls into an inaccessible wake drawer. Compaction dividers retain their existing presentation. Native JVM screenshots and transition regressions: `AssistantLiveWorkShotsTest`, both conversation types × three styles × light/dark, in `feature/chat/build/assistant-live-work-shots/`.
  While the session waits on you, an Agent chat's running summary reads Waiting for you, as the header does, with no elapsed time or spinner (`workSummary(waiting = true)`, #2483); the turn stays the live one and folds and opens as before. It waits on you when the hub's unified state says needs you (a question, plan or permission a hook holds while herdr's status stays `working`), the status says `blocked`, or one of the session's own requests is pending on the phone; the header goes by the same state (`turnWaitsOnYou`, `needsYou` in `ChatState.kt`). A task agent's request alone puts Waiting for you in the header but leaves this turn working.
  Codex commentary is assistant text as in `docs/integrations/codex.md` (#5853). `ItemsDatabase` 5 (`MIGRATION_4_5`, registered in both cache builders) drops cache rows written when those items were thinking; no schema or user data is migrated. `ContinuityNarrationTest` covers expanded narration being replaced by a folded page without losing the final answer outside its seq + block range.
- Tool naming follows iOS `ToolViews.swift` / `PersonaToolNames.swift` (#2753): Persona tools, `conversation_*`, `computer_*` / `browser_*`, `phone_*`, `mail_*` use names stripped of `mcp__x__`, localized verbs (Rename chat, Control computer, Phone, Read Agent chat…), icon and subject. Work row "Control computer macbook"; no app on `computer_state` gives "macbook · Whole screen" localized; `ask_user` first question; `Monitor` description or command first line. Unknown Persona-server tools "Call Persona · github read", others raw name (`namedToolVerb` in `core/domain`, `ToolVerbTest`, `ToolPresentationTest` in `feature/chat`).
- TodoWrite plan uses `PlanCard`: title completed/total and current item (activeForm while in progress), right Step N/M or checkmark when done;
  tap title collapses to progress hairline. Completed dim/check, running current spinner, pending hollow circle; failed update shows Update failed even collapsed (`planProgress`).
  Like web `planSteps`, the latest plan with 2+ items per work row (`latestPlan`) gets the full plan card; older updates, clearing empty lists and single-item lists remain inspectable as ordinary tool steps. Absent content uses activeForm.
- iOS/web parity batch (#2967):
  - Plan off-screen yields centered Step N/M capsule above composer (`PlanPill`, progress ring/checkmark, like web), tap scrolls to latest card (`latestPlanLine`: expanded work plan only). In an Agent's chat on Glass the tap opens the whole plan in a sheet instead, as iOS's `PlanSheet` (`ChatPlanSheet.kt`: "Step 3 of 4 · <the step it is on>", a bar of the steps done, the steps with their state; it follows the plan while open).
  - Fork/rewind menu (web MessageActions): transcript Agent reply id `<uuid>#<n>` (not `line…`) with `can_fork` offers Fork to new chat (`POST …/fork {at_item_id}`, success notice/open; assistant forks open as assistant chats, like `forkAt`). Your message offers Rewind here only in sendable Agent chat, disabled with reason if not Claude/not running/pending answer/working (failed sending or waiting message isn't working, web `outgoing`); `capabilities.rewind` decides instead of "Claude only" where the node says it (#6294: a runtime's session or a Codex thread rewinds, false says Unavailable, web `rewindBlock`); then choose Chat only / Code and chat, latter confirms again, the second only where `capabilities.rewind_code` says so or nothing is declared (a Claude terminal; `Rewind.restoresCode`, web `rewindCode`). `POST …/rewind` (`prompt`, `occurrence`, `prompts_after`, `restore`; web `rewindTarget`, `Rewind` in `core:domain`), wait 110 seconds; success restores message to composer (only Claude's own rewind in its terminal, where the node declares no `capabilities.rewind`, says Claude's input on the computer still holds it, as on iOS and the web); hub 404 Unsupported rewind, 403 Full-permission device required, 409 node reason.
  - Swipe left to quote (iOS #1058): reply icon appears right, at threshold 44 pt × 1.5 haptic/fill, release quotes; damping beyond threshold. Accessibility treats message as one focus with Quote action (`swipeToReply`, links/buttons still separate). Only where menu has Quote.
  - Dragging a conversation puts the keyboard away (#4210 T2, iOS `ChatView`'s `.interacting` scroll phase; every style): `ChatListEffects`, which the Agent chat and Persona's conversation (`ChatScreen`) and the Lark chats (`LarkScreen`) all run, clears the focus and hides the keyboard on the list's `DragInteraction.Start`, taking the focus from a request card's field too. A tap on blank space still does so (`dismissKeyboardOnBlankTap`); a scroll the page starts (following the newest message, a jump) is not a drag and leaves the keyboard; the keyboard does not follow the finger.
  - Persona tool names use #3100 `namedToolVerb` (`Rows.kt` in `core:domain`); adds `computer_run` steps/script subject "MacBook · Tap AC → 6 → ×" (iOS `ComputerSteps`, `PersonaTools.kt`), dedicated ask_user/Agent/Knowledge/card/reminder icons; Board no longer repeats verb tag when title is only tool name, no object.
  - Expanded ask_user lists questions/options, marks answers from hub "The user answered: - <question> → <answer>", free-text on own row, No answer if none (iOS `AskUserDetail`); answered output not repeated, only errors shown.
  - Task/Agent steps have View sub-Agent when session `subagents` matches `tool_use_id`, including finished; opens read-only chat. Brief and Board only: Glass draws the task card below.
  - Subagent task cards in Glass (#5045, `SubagentCards.kt`, `Subagents.kt` in `core:domain`; `docs/design/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` section 4.7, "Task cards"): every Task / Agent call is one full-width card where it was launched, running or done, inside the work summary like every tool call (super rule 2). First line: the subagent avatar, **Subagent**, the title (the call's description, else the listing's, else its prompt) and the status word with its mark (Running, Completed, Failed in red, Stopped); second line: Claude Code (not in a Persona conversation), the model chip (`Subagent.model`, "Haiku 4.5"), "23.4k tokens", "12 tool uses" and how long it took, or "Running for 1m 5s" each second. Status as the web's `agentStatus`: the deliveries' word first, a launch receipt never a result, a background one the list's to tell. The head opens it in place, remembered per call while the chat is open (`SubagentFolds`): the prompt, the latest result (four lines until **Show full result**; a cut report loads whole first), **Last tool** and **Output**. A subagent a subagent launched (`parent_id`) is a flat card of its own after the step that started before it (`Subagents.withNested`). No "View Subagent", no recent steps, no grid. Checked by `SubagentsTest`, `SubagentCardTextTest` and `SubagentCardsShotsTest` (`build/subagent-cards-shots/`).
  - Language-tagged reply fences (```kotlin) highlight via `CodeSyntax` (comments/strings/numbers/common keywords, palette roles); `//`, `/* */` only comments for C-family (not bash URLs/Python `//`); untagged, text/log/output/diff/markdown, tool outputs plain, only known programming languages highlighted like web.
  - Plan approval adds Approve, confirm each step (`mode: default`, web), alongside Approve and run automatically below two primary buttons, wrapping if needed.
  Expanded work shows the latest plan at its original position; collapsed work has no plan preview.
  Android's Step N/M pill only targets an expanded plan, not a plan inside collapsed work because there is no visible card to return to;
  web current title uses content, Android activeForm with content fallback.
- Claude next-message `suggestion`: idle, empty composer, no attached context yields a chip above composer, tap fills (X1).
- **Reply Markdown parsing** (#3050; `ChatMarkdownCache` in `ChatContent.kt`, `ChatMarkdownStream.kt`, `MarkdownPrewarm` in `ContentPresentation.kt`): a row draws its text plain until a parse of exactly that text is ready (#2602). The page parses ahead in one cache (`LocalChatMarkdownCache`, one entry per `<item id>/<part index>`): the latest turn's replies first, which an unread conversation waits for before it is placed (`markdownReadyFor`), then the earlier ones newest first, 80 replies in all.
  - One parse per id and text: a caller that asks while that parse is under way (the prewarm and the row it warms) waits for it. It runs in the cache's own scope, so a row scrolled away or a restarted prewarm does not cancel it for the others; nothing cancels it, even once every caller has left. A delta's tail parse that ends after the stream's whole parse of the same text does not replace it.
  - The prewarm parses what the row parses (`MarkdownText.source`: with the paths `PathPresence` knows as links, as `PathLinks.markdown`, when the page opens paths). One key, one source; otherwise the two replace each other's entry on every update.
  - What is kept: the ids the prewarm last asked for (`keep`), and the 32 most recently used other ids (thinking, plans, recaps, older replies scrolled to). At about 19 bytes of parse per byte of reply (JVM, `retainedHeapPerEntry`), a full cache is about 4 MB of 2 KB replies, 33 MB of 16 KB ones.
  - While the last reply streams, the prewarm splits only that reply's parts again; the others are as the previous split left them.
  - A streaming reply (`update.streaming`, passed from `MessageContent` only) parses from its last settled block on: a paragraph, a heading, a rule or a closed code fence followed by a blank line is kept, and the rest is parsed at its offsets after it. Lists, block quotes, tables and HTML never settle (later lines can continue them). The whole text is parsed instead when it is not the previous text plus more, when it holds a reference-link definition (for the rest of the stream), when a tail fails to parse, and for a reply that is not streaming; once the stream ends, its final text is parsed whole once. `ChatMarkdownStreamTest` checks the tree (each node's type and offsets), the text and the links against a whole parse at every prefix of a corpus, and after a cut at each line's end, its first six places and every fifth character.
  - Tests: `ChatMarkdownCacheTest` (one shared parse, a caller that leaves, the kept set after 1 and 40 other ids, the row finding the prewarm's source, a stream then its whole parse), `MarkdownPrewarmTest` (the kept split equals splitting afresh over random updates, and a streaming update keeps every other reply's parts as they were), `ChatMarkdownStreamTest` (also the characters a long reply's steps parse again, which a boundary that stops moving would multiply).
  - Measured with `PERSONA_BENCH=1 ./gradlew :feature:chat:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*ChatMarkdownBench*' -i` (CPU and allocation over all threads, the old cache and this one in 11 alternating rounds; skipped without the variable, never in CI). On a desktop JVM (2026-11-10), a 16 KB reply streamed in 400 deltas took 767 ms of CPU and 1,144 MB of allocation before, 167 ms and 73 MB after; the prewarm's cycle over 80 replies after one other text went from 80 parses to none.
- Code: `MessageText.kt` in `core/domain` (`messageText`, `imagePaths`, `FilePaths`), `lines/ApiErrors.kt` (`ApiErrorInfo`, `foldApiErrors`, `foldRecaps`; `ChatLine.ApiError` / `Recaps` in `LineModels.kt`, applied last in `chatLines`); `ChatRowActions.kt` in `feature/chat` (per-row full text/path/photos/viewer/menu/retry via `LocalChatRowActions`), `AttachedPhotos.kt`, `MessageMenu.kt`, `MessageHandoff.kt` (`handToPersona`, `rememberHandToPersona`), `ToolDetails.kt`, `ApiErrorCard.kt` (errors/recaps), `MessageExtras.kt` (full text/relative time/suggestions), `ContentPresentation.kt` (`messageModel`, `patchDiff`, `unifiedDiffLines`); design `ChatParts.kt` (`ChatPictureFrame`, `PhotoThumbnail`, `PhotoRow`, `PathChip`, `ChatNoteChip`, `ErrorCard`, `DetailField`, catalog Chat attachments and errors). App only passes `files`, `onOpenPath`, `onHandToPersona` in `ShellChat.kt`, and `ChatAppBindings.files` in `ChatWiringModule.kt`.
- Tests: `MessageTextTest`, `PathRefsTest` (web `path-refs.test.ts` cases), `lines/ApiErrorsTest` (old `ApiErrorTest`), `LinesFixtureTest` (two new rows) in `core/domain`; `MessageContentTest` (photos/files/Codex diff/questions/timer/collapsed execution/plan/truncation), `WorkTraceShotsTest` (active/plan/done, styles × light / dark, `feature/chat/build/work-trace-shots/`), `MessageMenuTest` (items/quote/handoff/retry/error tags/time/suggestions), `ChatPicturesTest` (computer photos/viewer list), `PathLinksTest` (Markdown/batch/cache/failure), `PathLinksShotsTest` (JVM user/reply links, three styles × light / dark, `feature/chat/build/path-link-shots/`, link tap) in `feature/chat`.
- Mock: `MOCK_CHAT_CONTENT=1` adds "看图、文件和计划" ("Images, files and plans", MacBook, `/Users/me/code/weather`): two photos (one fails), photo-only message, path reply, plan/questions/subtask/truncated answer; running Codex "排查 /orders 接口变慢" ("Investigate slow /orders") adds patch/new-file changes; Persona "帮我约周五和韩梅梅吃饭" ("Arrange Friday dinner with a friend") adds photo (server photo readable only without `MOCK_FILES`). `MOCK_API_ERRORS=1`: Persona home three repeated 502 with retry, "检查 PR #42" ("Check PR #42") ends expired login; `MOCK_RECAPS=1`, `MOCK_SUGGESTION=1` (Check PR #42), `MOCK_CHAT_IMAGES=1`.
- Unified screenshots (three styles × light / dark, `debug.style`, `debug.theme`, `debug.openSession <machine>/<session>`): two photos including failure, photo-only, file tags/open page, plan/questions/subtask details, Codex patch/new file, before/after full text, recap row/panel, your/Agent/intermediate reply menus, Select text page, context card above composer, handoff to Persona landing, error cards (three repeated/retry, expired-login/no retry, details), active elapsed timer, suggestion chip; catalog Chat attachments and errors.

## Batch screenshots with a mock hub

Boot a dedicated Android emulator first, then specify serial and output:

```sh
./scripts/app/android.sh shots -s emulator-5600 --out /tmp/persona-android-shots
```

Builds/installs separate `com.example.persona.shots`, clears only that package. Starts `scripts/mock/mock_hub.py` on an allocated local port, pairs through target device `adb reverse`, cold-starts each page and outputs light / dark PNGs. Covers four tabs, Agent chat, assistant chat (embedded in the Persona tab), request, terminal, Lark chat, mail detail, pairing, diagnostics, notification/checklist settings, monitoring, Knowledge, phone data, catalog, model configuration and new provider (manual/extension). Mock supplies Lark windows/topics/documents, mail body, Knowledge, session files/Git. Automation starts tab roots, never restores prior position or shows checklist; script waits for pairing `shell-position.json` to detect shell startup. Sheets/menus/forms (status, search, tasks, full text/request, files/Git, filters, compact list, space, long-press, new, history, Lark topic/document, devices, machine/Knowledge detail, demo) still need manual opening and screenshots. Sample contacts are anonymized before mock launch; captures use fake data.

`SHOTS_PLUGINS=1` adds `MOCK_EXTENSIONS` and Settings › Extensions plus three extension pages (`example-notes` needs setup with all field types, `voice` read-only from the manager's manager.json, `example-weather` unavailable on this hub). Off by default because running voice extension adds a Persona voice button and changes other baseline shots.

Tests never read user pairing or connect to user hub; automation declares no phone tools. On success/failure stops this mock and test package, removes forwarding. Output includes mock logs/fixture identity for missing-page debugging. Wait durations are script settings, not measured load performance. Device permissions, Chinese-vendor background survival and actual terminal IMEs still require real devices.

### Pixel-exact before/after comparison

For move-only refactors that must not change appearance (e.g. moving Agents tree components into designsystem 2026-11-02), capture before/after packages on same emulator with same steps. Freeze changing inputs first:

- Animation: `settings put global animator_duration_scale 0` (App reduces motion, static spinner/breathing glow).
- Time: same fixed mock/emulator clock (`datetime.now()` fixed; emulator `adb root`, `settings put global auto_time 0`, `date -u` at cold start and before each shot). Brief greeting, loop next HH:MM, minutes-ago otherwise change between builds.
- Status bar: reenter SystemUI demo each round (exit then enter), clock/battery only; repeated enter without exit retains prior icons (observed two Wi-Fi icons then one).
- Comparison: don't use Pillow `ImageChops.difference(a, b).getbbox()` for equality. RGBA `getbbox()` only checks alpha; screenshots alpha all 255, so it always says equal (encountered). Compare `tobytes()` directly, differences per channel.

`android.sh shots` can do all of this (added for multi-hub 2026-11-02; unset variables preserve behavior):

```sh
SHOTS_STYLE=glass SHOTS_MOCK_PORT=<port> SHOTS_FROZEN=2026-11-02T04:00:00+00:00 SHOTS_SETTLE=3 \
  ./scripts/app/android.sh shots -s <emulator> --out /tmp/shots-before-glass
python3 scripts/app/android_shots_diff.py /tmp/shots-before-glass /tmp/shots-after-glass --diff /tmp/shots-diff-glass
```

- `SHOTS_STYLE`: `debug.style` each start; `SHOTS_MOCK_PORT`: fixed mock port (saved in pairing, must match before/after); `SHOTS_SETTLE`: seconds after cold start (then waits for 3 identical frames, max 20 seconds).
- `SHOTS_FROZEN`: replaces mock datetime/time with fixed clock, seeds `uuid4` (stable hub_id), in `android.sh` wrapper without changing `mock_hub.py`; emulator root, auto-time off, clock set every 0.1 seconds throughout (machine chart uses seconds). Animation zero, status demo; restored afterward.
- Diff script compares pixels, reports changed count/bounding rectangle, `--diff` images; `--tolerance` / `--ignore '<filename glob>=x0,y0,x1,y1'` off by default.
- Two runs of same package differ only in diagnostics "Identity verified, N ms" (measured single probe) and Glass light Lark bottom 49 rows with channel difference 1 (unknown reason). Exclude with `--tolerance 1 --ignore '*-diagnostics.png=0,Y0,1079,Y1'`; style Y: Glass 1385–1425, Brief 1360–1410, Board 1305–1355.

## Live adaptive screenshots

`shots-live` proves state continuity in one App process. It reuses the batch command's dedicated application id, APK installation, mock hub, fixture discovery, `adb reverse`, permissions, frozen clock and cleanup, but does not cold-start each capture:

```sh
SHOTS_STYLE=glass SHOTS_MOCK_PORT=<mock port> \
  ./scripts/app/android.sh shots-live -s <emulator> --theme both \
  --scenario scripts/app/android_adaptive_scenes/window-selftest.json \
  --out /tmp/persona-adaptive-window
```

`-s` must be an already running, dedicated emulator. `--theme` accepts `light`, `dark` or `both` (the default); `--out` has the same default rule as `shots`. Each theme runs the complete ordered scene with its own initial PID. Package clearing is allowed only for that theme's initial pairing; steps within a scene never use `force-stop`, `am start -S` or `pm clear`. An `open` step establishes the initial scene and cannot reopen the same destination after a window transformation. Opening a different destination is allowed for a continuous multi-page scene, so navigation to a new page is not mistaken for recovering a lost route. The old `shots` command and its cold-start captures are unchanged.

### Scene format

A scene is one JSON object with `id`, `description`, `requires` and ordered `steps`. An optional `manifestId` maps the scene to an existing paired-screenshot manifest entry without rewriting that manifest. `requires.page` selects an exact probe page or one page with the given prefix; `requires.fields` lists page-specific evidence that must be present. PID, window width/height and route are always required. A missing field, stale or malformed probe, ambiguous page, changed PID or unreadable navigation checkpoint fails the run rather than writing a null value and passing.

For a scene with several live pages, `requires.pages` instead maps aliases to probe pages and their required local fields. `capture` and `assert` may provide a step-local `requires` object, replacing the scene-level page selection for that evidence point. Existing single-page scenes need no change. Assertions address aliased evidence through `probePages.<alias>.<field>`. When a list field is required without an explicit page, the runner selects the unique probe page whose title is visible in the current UI XML; ambiguity fails. `selectedKey=null` is valid evidence for an unselected root, and `detailAgentsExpanded=null` means the current page has no detail-agent control; use explicit `equals` assertions on pages where those states apply.

The canonical `{"step": "open", "page": "machines"}` spelling and the compact `{"open": {"page": "machines"}}` spelling are equivalent. Compact `wm-size` width/height, `posture.state`, `rotate.direction`, direct assert-check maps and capture names are normalized before execution. `requires.mockEnv` accepts single-line `MOCK_*` values and exports them before starting this run's mock hub; it never restarts the mock during a continuous scene.

```json
{
  "requires": {
    "fields": [],
    "pages": {
      "settings": {"page": "Settings", "fields": ["firstVisibleKey", "contentBounds", "hingeBounds"]},
      "composer": {"page": "chat", "fields": ["draftHmac", "selection"]}
    }
  }
}
```

For machine grids, `columns` is derived from the number of stable machine IDs in the first visible `machine-row|…` Compose key. `detailAgentsExpanded` reads the current detail page's Show all / Show less control in English, Chinese or Japanese; it is false while the collapsed control is visible and true while the expanded control is visible. These values never infer a session key from a visible title. `firstVisibleOffset` is accepted as an alias of `firstVisibleOffsetPx`.

| Step | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| `launch` | Starts the DEBUG entry with the selected style/theme and records the initial PID. Later launches are rejected. `session`, `tab`, `page`, `link`, `conversationKind` and `windowingMode` use the existing DEBUG arguments. `session: "chat"` and `link: "terminal"` can reference `fixture.tsv`. |
| `open` | Reuses `debug.openTab`, `debug.openSession`, `debug.openPage` or `debug.openLink` without clearing the package. A different destination may open after a transformation; reopening the same destination is rejected. Secondary-page route/selected IDs supplement the two-tab `shell-position.json`, which does not persist every child route. |
| `tap` | Reads fresh UI XML, selects exactly one node by `resource-id`, `content-desc` or exact `text`, and taps the center of its bounds. Zero or multiple matches fail. |
| `text` | Waits for `focused=true`, then injects printable ASCII with `adb input text`. Spaces become `%s`. Shift, Ctrl, Alt and Meta are reset before and after input. Newlines use `keycombination: ["SHIFT_LEFT", "ENTER"]`. Chinese or other IME composing text is deliberately unsupported here and requires instrumentation with `InputConnection`; ASCII input is not evidence of composing behavior. |
| `key` | Injects `key` through `input keyevent` or `keycombination` through `input keyboard keycombination`, records the keys, and resets modifiers around the action. |
| `rotate` | Runs `adb emu rotate`, then polls the actual window dp and orientation. No fixed sleep substitutes for readback. |
| `posture` | Runs `adb emu posture 1|2|3` and polls `dumpsys device_state`. Emulator posture evidence is labeled `folding-feature`; `fake: true` with `posture: "book"` or `"tabletop"` uses `debug.posture` and is labeled `fake`, never real hinge evidence. |
| `wm-size` | Sets `size: "WxH"` and optional `density`, then reads back the actual dp. Before a compact size it explicitly unfolds and waits for `OPENED`. `reset: true` restores the size; `densityReset: true` also restores density. `resize-display` is not used. |
| `freeform-resize` | Finds the currently resumed Persona task by application id, runs `am task resize <id> 0 0 <right> <bottom>`, and polls the same task's dp bounds. At 320 dpi, 599/600, 759/760 and 839/840 dp therefore use 1198/1200, 1518/1520 and 1678/1680 px respectively. A missing or changed task fails. |
| `assert` | Evaluates a `checks` object using `equals`, `unchanged`, `matches` or `notOverlaps`. Dotted paths address fields such as `window.widthDp`, `selection.start`, `firstVisibleKey` and `attachments`; scalar shorthand means `equals`. `unchanged` compares with the previous capture/assert evidence, and `pid: "unchanged"` always compares with the initial PID. For hinge safety use `"contentBounds": {"notOverlaps": "hingeBounds"}`, or the corresponding `probePages.<alias>` paths. Rectangles must be present, numeric and non-empty; touching edges do not overlap, intersecting areas fail. |
| `capture` | Waits for three identical frames with a 20-second bound, validates the PNG, collects fresh evidence, and writes the PNG and sidecar. A stability timeout or bad frame fails instead of saving the last frame as a success. |

`window-selftest.json` keeps an Agent chat's multiline draft, full selection, focus and attachment list through expanded → rotated → real book half-open → unfolded compact size → expanded. Generic Fold's compact simulation explicitly unfolds first. Freeform boundary cases belong in tablet scenes using the same DSL.

### Probe and sidecar

`DebugEntryActivity` enables `AdaptiveProbe` only for a debuggable build launched with `debug.automation=true`. The probe is disabled by default: no snapshot listener, file write or additional recomposition is installed. `AdaptiveListProbe(page, LazyListState)` publishes the first non-chrome Compose item key and its pixel offset, plus the list viewport and current hinge rectangle. `AdaptiveBoundsProbe(page, contentBounds, hingeBounds)` provides the same bounds evidence for `JPage` and other non-list containers; callers supply page-local rectangles in one shared coordinate space. `AdaptiveComposerProbe(page, TextFieldValue, attachmentStates)` publishes an HMAC-SHA-256 draft digest, selection, focus and attachment `id:uploadState` evidence. Focus is reported from the existing `onFocusChanged` callback because `TextFieldValue` does not contain it. Each enable session generates a new random HMAC key; neither the key nor draft plaintext is written to the probe or sidecar. Attachment names, URIs and paths are omitted too.

Evidence is atomically written to the App-private `no_backup/hub-data/adaptive-probe.json`, with field version, PID, session, monotonic update time and per-page timestamps. Active pages heartbeat without recomposing; a stale snapshot is not accepted. Route and selected session key come from the pairing-scoped `shell-position.json`, never from a visible row title. UI XML supplements node bounds and focus only. UI dumps delete the old device file and retry at most five times, 0.5 seconds apart; a null root, old file or empty hierarchy fails.

Every capture has two files:

```text
<out>/<scenario>/<step>-<state>-<style>-<theme>.png
<out>/<scenario>/<step>-<state>-<style>-<theme>.json
```

The sidecar includes scenario/manifest id, step/state, serial, initial/before/after PID, window px/dp/density/orientation/task evidence, DeviceState and posture evidence type, style, theme, locale, route, selected key, first visible key/offset, draft HMAC, selection, focused node, attachment states, content/hinge bounds, all aliased `probePages`, assertion results, input/modifier records, timestamp and PNG SHA-256. A multi-display warning before the PNG signature is stripped; an empty file or missing signature is an error.

On exit, the Python runner removes only its UI/probe files, resets input modifiers, and restores window size, density and posture it changed. The shell cleanup then stops only its own mock/clock processes, removes its own reverse forwarding and test package state, and restores frozen-clock/demo settings. It does not use `pkill`, stop another emulator or touch another lane's ports. Cleanup failure also makes the command fail.

Current environment limits remain explicit: hover, secondary-click and cross-App drag/drop are not implemented by this driver; IME composing needs instrumentation; fake tabletop is visual evidence only; a Generic Fold compact `wm-size` is not a physical outer display; and emulator results are not physical-device performance measurements.

## Proactive work (Settings › Assistant › Proactive work)

Requested 2026-11-02: "There is no setting for where Persona chats, memory and the rest run." Assistant settings split into two; this part manages Persona's scheduled tasks, self-arranged follow-ups and run history (iOS `apple/Persona/Views/Settings/AssistantProactiveViews.swift`). Code `feature/assistant/…/proactive/`, public `AssistantProactiveScreen(onBack, onOpenConversation)` only; `onOpenConversation` opens assistant chats created/resumed/run-now. API/coroutine scope default to current pairing in Persona host `LocalAssistantHost`. Internal child pages (task, create, all follow-ups, history) own navigation/back, pushing from right like App pages.

- Entry: Assistant settings Proactive work section opens in place (chats route `ChatRoute(kind = Assistant)` to Persona); Persona drawer Scheduled tasks opens same page in place, chats directly in the Persona tab.
- Home: Persona sentence with enabled count/next time; Next timeline (large time left, all enabled tasks mixed by next run, max 4); recurring tasks daily/every N minutes (human rule "Weekdays 9:00 AM", "Every 30 minutes", next/last result, non-default permission, toggle; `created_by: "assistant"` avatar spark and "Created by Persona in chat", failed-last-run red warning); one-off follow-ups (time, resume original / same chat / new chat, tap edit, ⋯ Run now/Edit/Open chat/Delete with confirmation); latest 3 runs. Top-right + templates (meeting prep, weekly report, end-of-day review, same iOS) or blank; long-press recurring task Run now/Edit/Open chat/Delete.
- Task editor (iOS `AssistantTaskEditor`, #3034): name (optional one-off), enabled; When with Once/Daily/Interval. Once: today through 14 days date chips + hour/minute wheels, no past save; Daily: 24-hour wheels, TalkBack slider adjustment, weekdays Mon–Sun with Every day/Weekdays/Weekends shortcuts, cannot disable last day; Interval: chips 5 minutes–24 hours. Switching Once defaults next whole hour after one hour; Interval defaults 30 minutes. Prompt; execution: task model opens Settings › Models `task.<id>` (`schedule` on creation, hidden without host navigation), permission Default / Auto-edit files / Plan only / Auto / Allow all (confirm Allow all, red row selected). Chat: conversation-arranged has Resume original toggle, only can turn off; otherwise Run in same chat, existing chat openable, Next run in new chat via `POST …/tasks/{id}/fresh`. Existing task also Run now (save edits first), Last result, history latest 5 with More, confirmed Delete. Back with unsaved edits confirms.
- History: newest first by day, Ran / Failed to start / Skipped, on time or lateness, manual-run marker, reason (known hub English errors localized, others verbatim), opens created chat. Task history filters `ref_id`; `Run` gains `ref_id`, `due` already returned by hub. Last run comes from latest history, because immediate runs don't update `last_run`; task fields alone mix old scheduled time with current manual result.
- Writes: unified `/api/v1/assistant/tasks` (no legacy `routines`/`followups` since #3034): create POST, edit PUT `…/tasks/{id}`, DELETE. Only editable fields (`ScheduledTask.editable()`: title, prompt, `repeat`, `at`, `time`, `weekdays`, `every_minutes`, `enabled`, `session_id`, `permission_mode`), no `push` (hub retains); hub no longer reads `launcher`/`effort` here, model uses config. Edit sends only user-changed fields, preserving concurrent Persona edits such as time. Before toggle or editor writes, reload the task from the hub: the page does not listen to `assistant_schedule`, so this is how it sees changes elsewhere (#5075). Only if that read fails does it use the already-loaded task; repeat change sends new-rule fields. Serial queued writes, started writes survive leaving; toggle optimistic, restore hub value on failure. Failed write/delete first rereads; desired hub state means no failure. Concurrently deleted edited task says not saved, never recreates. Create id assigned by hub, lost response cannot identify it and reports failure.
- Run now: `POST …/tasks/{id}/run`, hub waits for chat readiness up to 2 minutes, so read timeout 130 seconds (`HttpAssistantManagement`, same new-Agent creation; others 15). Success opens chat; one-off removed then reread. Pairing scope owns run, leaving page doesn't cancel. New task also has Run now: create, run, open (iOS `runNow`).
- Failure notices: user actions through `LocalAppFeedback.notice`, otherwise debug footer. Transport cancellation on pairing/route switch counts as failed request ("Connection just changed; try again"), no stuck Saving. Read errors stay in section with Retry, preserving content. Relative times refresh every 30 seconds; closest item triggers reread 5 seconds after due (based on last-read time so clock jumps don't cancel).
- Time: hub `next_run` uses user timezone with offset (`+08:00`); Android 12/13 `Instant.parse` only accepts Z, so `instantOf` tries Instant then OffsetDateTime. `core:contracts.parseTime` still Instant-only; other pages fail offset times on these versions. Hub accepts `9:00`; editor shows/sends `09:00`. Numbers formatted with `Locale.ROOT`.
- Debug-only `app/src/debug/…/ProactiveDebugActivity.kt`: direct mock, no App pairing, loopback only:

  ```sh
  PORT=0 python3 scripts/mock/mock_hub.py        # prints mock hub on <loopback address>:<port>
  adb -s <emulator> reverse tcp:<port> tcp:<port>
  adb -s <emulator> shell am start -n <package>/persona.android.debug.ProactiveDebugActivity \
    --es debug.mockAddress http://<loopback address>:<port> --es debug.theme dark --es debug.style board --es debug.page routine:morning
  ```

  `debug.page`: `routine:<id>`, `new`, `new:<template index>`, `followups`, `runs`, `runs:<task id>`, absent home; theme/style as App. Chat-opening only explains destination in debug dialog. Real shell after mock pairing: `--es debug.openLink 'persona://open?target=settings&page=assistant' --es debug.assistantSettings proactive`.
- Mock: sample rules UTC+8 (last run previous scheduled occurrence; emulator uses this timezone), `next_run` offset +08:00 like hub. `MOCK_SCHEDULE=empty`; PUT validates name/prompt/HH:MM, preserves hub fields by id, computes next run; run/cancel missing id 404, each immediate run logs manual run and updates last status but not `last_run`; `GET /mock/schedule` lists immediate runs, `/mock/schedule/reset` restores.
- Verification (rewritten for tasks #3034, 2026-11-02): `ProactiveStoreTest` (two-section split, task-only toggle write, failure rollback, lost response not failure, changed-only edits, repeat switch, disable original-chat resume, no resurrection, delete/lost response, run-now one-off removal, fresh chat, stale-data retention, transport cancellation failure), `ProactiveTextTest` (rules/interval/time-offset/countdown/lateness/errors/last run/destination/mode/timeline/sentence/page keys/draft comparison), `AssistantManagementTest` (POST/PUT/fresh/run paths/bodies); `ProactiveShotsTest` home, daily/interval/once edit, create, follow-ups/history (three languages, Glass light / dark, all styles home). Prior 2026-11-02 emulator round tapped every old operation; #3034 on Linux had no emulator, only Robolectric shots; no physical-device/real-hub test.
- Design system lacks time/weekday pickers; these live in `proactive/ProactiveComponents.kt`, token-only (palette, type, `shapes.chipShape` / `controlShape`, `Tokens.Space`), no style branches; replace when matching design-system components exist.

## Terminal and links (old main-line App port, 2026-11-02)
