Old single-module terminal/link features filled in with new architecture, iOS interactions/trade-offs. Code `feature/terminal` (`README.md`), `core/terminal`, `feature/chat` (`LINKS.md`), shell `app/…/ui/ShellLinks.kt`.

Terminal:

- Key row adds ⇧Tab (Claude Code mode, one key, not ⇧ then Tab). Design-system `JKeyCap`: Ctrl/Alt/⇧ tap arms next key (color/border), hold solid, quick double-tap locks (solid/underline), like iOS.
- Control/read-only stored locally SharedPreferences `persona-terminal`, not pairing, reused next opening; read-only in Take over terminal? dialog applies only this visit.
- Read-only/screen-poll fallback can also type (decided 2026-11-02): key row/keyboard/paste `POST /api/v1/sessions/{m}/{s}/keys`, one request at a time, in-flight typing accumulated next (text before keys), reread screen immediately after. Failure drops queued keys, says Keys not sent. Wheel/click still control-only.
- Control scrollback accumulates off-top rows locally (max 3000, `ScrollbackGrid`), visible in Select text; finger scrolls locally if no node `terminal_scroll`. Program-redrawn lines after keyboard hides/pane grows are removed from scrollback. Read-only anchors last row (formerly rendered from row 0, showing oldest mirrored content first).
- Glyphs: box drawing including rounded/dashed/half-lines per cell; ⏺ ⏸ ⏵ ⏹ ⏴ ● ■ ▶ ◀ drawn as shapes (bundled JetBrains Mono lacks them, fallback emoji two-cell colored); ✔ ✳ etc. add U+FE0E; OSC 8 links have translucent underline (tap remains terminal click; no iOS link opening yet).
- Select text: top Copy button, full-page monospace unwrapped text, both-axis scroll, Copy all. No iOS grid selection.
- Display panel top-right: font 7–20, step 0.5, default 15 dp, not system-scaled to keep columns stable, stored locally; control font changes resize grid. Wrap toggle moved here from top bar.
- Controlling/read-only explanation below title (Taking over…, Computer 172×45, Reads screen once per second), not a card above terminal; card appearance/disappearance changed height and caused two extra pane relayouts.

Links:

- Preload first 3 links from Agent replies within 15 minutes among last 40 items, requires hostname dot, excludes native Lark links (documents preload separately, see Lark); pool max 4.
- Renderer crash/kill drops only that page (Reload in card), third-party cookies on, memory pressure destroys off-screen pages, viewed pages prefer disk cache; ⋯ Share; selection actions added in `onPrepareActionMode`, before Copy.
- Web outside chat (shell cards from Knowledge/non-Persona-home pages, Inbox web) offers only Hand to Persona for selected text: optional instruction, send main chat, switch Persona. Inbox uses shell-provided `LocalInboxWebSelection` in `feature/inbox`.
- Chat `persona://session|tab|assistant|inbox` navigates locally (`LocalChatAppLinks` to shell); not `persona://pair` / `persona://open`. `mailto:` / `tel:` / other App links confirm before system handoff, `intent:` sanitized per legacy rules; unsafe schemes refused.
- `<persona-context>` exactly matches iOS (body unescaped, only strips closing tag, one newline between contexts). Context-only body "参考上面的内容。" ("Refer to the content above."); AI edit without instruction "按上面选中的内容修改。" ("Edit according to the selected content above."); handoff without instruction "看看上面这段。" ("Look at the passage above.").

Tests: `BoxDrawingTest`, `ScrollbackGridTest` in `core:terminal` (iOS recorded `apple/PersonaTests/Fixtures/terminal-control-scroll.jsonl`); `TerminalPortTest`, `TerminalCriticalPathTest` in `feature:terminal`; `HubTerminalTransportTest` in `core:hub`; `ChatLinksTest` in `feature:chat`. Mock:

```sh
# Control, scrollback (one line every 0.7 seconds), keyboard; read-only /keys logged at GET /mock/keys
MOCK_TERMINAL_STREAM=1 MOCK_TERMINAL_CONTROL=1 MOCK_TERMINAL_SCROLL=1 MOCK_TERMINAL_INTERVAL=0.7 PORT=<port> python3 scripts/mock/mock_hub.py
# Read-only mirrors real Claude Code frames (rounded composer, ⏺, ⏵⏵)
MOCK_TERMINAL_STREAM=1 MOCK_TERMINAL_CONTROL=1 MOCK_TERMINAL_FIXTURE=apple/PersonaTests/Fixtures/terminal-observe-permission.jsonl MOCK_TERMINAL_INTERVAL=0.2 PORT=<port> python3 scripts/mock/mock_hub.py
# Various link schemes in first Agent's last reply, Persona main chat and Inbox
MOCK_LINK_SCHEMES=1 MOCK_LINKS=1 PORT=<port> python3 scripts/mock/mock_hub.py
```

Direct terminal: `--es debug.openLink 'persona://open?target=terminal&machine=macbook&session=<session id>'`. Emulator 2026-11-02 inspected above switches: control/read-only, three modifier states, text selection, display, font 7/15/20, read-only keyboard `/keys`, three styles × light / dark. Links were finished after lanes stopped building, not emulator-checked; real menu order, renderer-crash recovery, pinyin need device confirmation.

Port forwarding (#2144, `docs/operations/ports.md`, iOS `PortForwarder` / `PortLinks`):

- Recognition: `core/domain/ports/PortLinks` recognizes `http(s)://localhost|127.0.0.1|0.0.0.0|[::1]|*.localhost|this-machine-hostname(with/without .local):port/path`, and schemeless `localhost:3000/x`; excludes no-port, LAN addresses, other hosts. `Session.ports` (`ListeningPort`) arrives with sessions.
- Opening: `ChatLinkHost` with session machine via `LocalChatPorts` checks first; if port is in an Agent's `ports` on that computer, forwards directly; otherwise `POST …/ports/{port}/open`, 404 says Port :N no longer listening, leaves link unchanged. Card subtitle machine :port, only Reload menu (phone-loopback address unusable by other Apps).
- Forwarder `core/hub/PortForwarder`: bind same port on phone 127.0.0.1, random if occupied, then same number on ::1, loopback-only. Each connection gets `…/ports/{port}/connect` WebSocket (device token, pairing-pinned certificate, current route). Hub bytes written to browser directly on read thread; outbound >512 KB pauses browser reads, no unbounded buffers either direction. Group routing `PortOpener.inGroup`.
- No port preloading/disk cache; reopen reloads same address because dev server changed (iOS #1934).
- Session port strip (`ChatPortChips.kt`, iOS `SessionPortChips`): below sibling tabs, `PathChip` per port, globe + ":port page title", tap forwarded like link; hidden with no ports, Persona, or no opener.
- Badges below replies/Bash output (`PortBadges`, iOS `PortChips`): each recognized link ":port/path page title", forwarded on tap. Ports absent from Agent `ports` queried once via `GET …/ports/{port}` (`PortPresence`, cache 1 minute, not-listening 30 seconds, failed no badge); stopped dims/labels Stopped, opening 404 also marks stopped.
- Not supported: https dev servers with self-signed certificates not trusted by system CA.

## Machines, engines, model configuration and new Agents

Behavior ported 2026-11-02 from old single-module App (batch E: machine, engine, Claude settings, models, async creation, prefilled creation, revoked-device section).

- **Machine page** (Agents › List settings › Machine monitoring › machine, or header gauge; `MachinesRoute` / `MachineDetailRoute`):
  - The overview puts computers that need action first: pending confirmation, offline, critical alerts, warnings, agents needing you, then a visible deploy/version mismatch; ordinary computers follow. `MachineConcern.kt` is the single projection for this order. Alerts come from the existing `alerts(current, cores)`, and agents come from `machineSessions` (including its nested-subtask and background-state rules). Equal concerns retain the hub's original index, so a heartbeat or a sample that stays at the same severity cannot reorder cards. Metrics-only and manager-scoped inputs keep their ids. The summary separately reports online / total computers, computers with any non-normal concern, and the sum of the cards' Needs You tallies.
  - A detail shows its agents before Now / History, in the existing `machineSessions` priority. Five are shown initially; **Show all N** reveals the rest and **Show less** folds them again without another sessions request. `rememberSaveable(machineId)` retains expansion across configuration changes but does not share it with another computer. The old post-monitoring agents section is not repeated.
  - Deployment row when `node_mismatch`, `deploying` or a `deploy_phase`; Redeploy `POST /api/v1/machines/{id}/deploy`, offered only online and when the hub doesn't say `can_deploy: false` (then `deploy_reason`, or a plain "can't be updated from here yet", says why; the self-check's old-node Redeploy follows the same rule; older hubs without the field keep Redeploy). A redeploy under way names its `deploy_phase` (waiting for idle / transferring / restarting / confirming; none: installing), as the web does (#1868). Progress/last failure `deploy_error` follow machine pushes; request failure Deployment failed: reason (409 raw reason). Same card in list/detail.
  - Engine and connection: Engine opens `EngineRoute`; Reconnect `POST …/reconnect`, waits up to 15 seconds for online, polling machines every 3 seconds if push lost, Connected / Not connected yet: machine error / Reconnect failed: reason. Footnote connection type (hub machine / hub through ssh alias).
  - Below Engine: Show botmux sessions too (#2899, iOS `BotmuxSwitchModel`, web #2575/#2842), `GET / PUT /api/v1/machines/{id}/botmux {"on"}`, read on opening; 404/failure hides row. Toggle optimistic and immediate save independently of Engine Save; rollback with botmux setting not saved: reason. Footnote explains botmux installation. On machine page beside engine per web #2842, visible and one-tap.
  - Launchers N: `GET …/launchers`, monospace names, model final path component; no offline query, reload on online, failure reason.
  - History disk chart (used bytes converted using latest total, hidden if unknown); system info adds boot time, herdr/Claude Code/Persona node versions. Missing machine on hub explained.
  - Code `MachineActions.kt` (reconnect/deploy/launchers/botmux, tested), `MachineManagementSections.kt` in `feature/machines`; interface `MachineManagement` in `core:contracts`, `HttpMachineManagement` in `core:hub` via paired connection request; `app/…/di/MachineManagementModule.kt` routes each call by machine suffix to group (#634). No demo actions.
- **Engine** ([engines.md](../integrations/engines.md)):
  - Required step between pairing and the checklist (`PairingScreen`) if the hub machine (`kind: local`, else first) reports `engines`. Back cannot skip it. The picker preselects the current engine (herdr if never chosen) and follows updates until the user edits. A failed save keeps the selection and shows the reason next to **Retry**.
  - Survives process death: machine saved in `ReadinessRecords` `engine-pending`; restart `OnboardingScreen` shows before checklist. Missing machine allows Retry/Continue for now.
  - Machine Engine opens the same `EngineSelectionScreen` via `EngineRoute` in `settingsDetailEntries`. While saving, the toolbar Back is disabled and Android Back stays on the page. On success, the returned Machine immediately updates the list via `SessionsRepository.applyMachine`. Rules/options/detection/footnotes are in `core:domain.engines/Engines.kt`.
- **Claude Code settings** (Settings › <name>'s settings › Claude Code settings, `SettingsPage.ClaudeCode`, `page=claudecode`): select online machine (first hub machine, fallback first online), Apply to all online machines default on, both local. Switch/choice/text rows; unset Default, clear text sends JSON null. Multi-machine sequential writes, all success Applied to N machines, failures per machine then reread.
- **Codex settings** (same Computers, `SettingsPage.Codex`, `page=codex`, #2754): same `ClaudeSettingsPage(kind = AgentSettingsKind.Codex)`, iOS `AgentSettingsKind.codex`, `GET/PUT /api/v1/machines/{mid}/codex-settings`, allowlisted settings in machine `~/.codex/config.toml`; machine/apply-all shared with Claude settings.
- **Model configuration** (Settings › <name>'s settings › Model configuration, `SettingsPage.ModelConfig`, `page=modelconfig`): grouped hub purposes, search name/group/id/model; effective provider/model, state/inheritance. Purpose page `ModelPurposeRoute(purposeId)` (voice can open `plugin.<id>`): effective config, provider (hidden marked Hidden, unavailable current reference marked; changing clears model/effort), model, reasoning effort (hidden for Messages API providers), restore inheritance (when the purpose has a runtime selection, clear the runtime set by iOS / web too with `runtime: null`, #5077; normal saves omit `runtime` so the hub retains it). Revision PATCH, empty null; 409 preserves edit, says Changed elsewhere, offers only Reload (discard edits). Legacy route 404 without JSON says update server; known config missing purpose says absent. Extension model still text field; replace with catalog when voice batch G integrates.
- **New Agent**:
  - Claude always async (`async: true`): immediate `starting` session adopted (`SessionsRepository.adopt`), chat opens. First message no longer prompt; `name_hint` names tab, message enters ordinary outbox, id and start-operation id stored in `AgentCreationStore`; lost receipt/restart reconciliation queues same id once. Codex remains synchronous with required `prompt`.
  - Hub `agent.new` purpose defaults Follow model configuration (empty launcher), no last-launcher automatic override; manual Launcher (this time only) overrides once. Old hub selects launcher as before; unreadable purpose explains, require select or reload.
  - Above composer `ChatStartingNote`: Starting Claude; message sends when ready / Didn't start: reason.
  - `NewAgentRoute(machineId, cwd, prompt)` page version of same form for Knowledge Start Agent: online machine/directory preselected editable, offline fallback explained; success closes form and opens chat in Agents.
  - **Recent** (#2134, rules and fixtures in `spec/fixtures/recents/`): up to 3 cards above the machines, each a setup that started an Agent from this form (folder · machine, then launcher or the follow wording · model when not the default · Claude's mode and effort; Allow everything in the caution colour with the warning icon). A tap fills machine, folder, launcher, model, mode and effort, marks the card and scrolls to the first message with the cursor in it; it never starts. An offline machine's card is dimmed with an Offline tag and picks nothing; a long press on any card offers **Remove from Recent** / **Clear Recent Setups**. Hidden in a space's form and while a start waits to be confirmed. Recorded on `Created` next to `saveDefaults`, a tab in a space too (its folder), as on iOS, the Mac and the web; not a resume with the agent type and the model sent; kept as one JSON string under `$scope/recent` in the `agent_creation` DataStore (`RecentSetups.kt`, `AgentCreationPreferences`). Picking a machine also brings back its model: that of its latest recent setup with the same launcher. A card whose launcher the machine no longer has (or that follows a configuration the hub can't give) keeps the machine's launcher and the default model; a Codex card keeps the form's mode and effort.
  - To-do **Hand to Agent › Start a new one** starts from the same memory: the last machine New Agent started on if it is online (else the first online one), with that machine's folder, launcher (following the configuration when that is what it remembers) and mode; a machine with nothing remembered still gets `~`, claude and the default mode. Picking another machine loads its memory.
  - **ultracode** (#582, also #4211 F2.1; iOS `UltracodeStartRow`): for Claude Code whose effort can be set, on a hub with the `ultracode` feature, an **ultracode** switch under Thinking effort, off each time the form opens and never saved. A known Claude Code older than 2.1.284 on the computer (`Machine.claudeVersion`) says so and can't be switched on (`NewAgentUltracode`). On, the start sends `ultracode: true`; a 409 `node_outdated` / `ultracode_unsupported` turns it off and says "Couldn't start with ultracode: … Retry starts without it". The same quota lines go under it, read from the hub of the computer picked (`MachineManagement.usageWindows(machineId)`), without the warning under a Claude Code too old for it. Tests `NewAgentUltracodeTest`, `UltracodeQuotaTest`; mock `MOCK_ULTRACODE=1` (Mac Studio's Claude Code is 2.1.250), `MOCK_USAGE=stale` / `none`.
  - **ultracode on a list row** (#582, iOS `Ultracode.rowMark` / `UltracodeMark`): a Claude Code agent of the user's whose node saw ultracode on, and whose row isn't offline, has the workflow mark (`PersonaIcons.workflow`, in the AI color: `RowFlag.ai`) right after its title, read "ultracode on" (`ultracodeMark`).
  - **ultracode off after a resume** (#582, iOS `UltracodeLostNote`): `Session.ultracodeLostAt` (`ultracode_lost_at`). While it is set, ultracode isn't on and the chat offers the switch (`Ultracode.lostShown`), one line over the composer says "ultracode off after resume" with **Turn back on** (`POST …/modes {"ultracode": true}`, "Applies next turn" while queued) and × (`{"ultracode_dismiss_lost": true}`, `ChatAction.Modes.dismissUltracodeLost`, verified by the notice being gone): hidden at once, back if the hub says no. On Glass the line is iOS's card (`floatingSurface`, #4339 C9), without the retry mark iOS puts before **Turn back on**, which cut the note's words on a phone's width; Brief and Board keep the plain line. Mock: `MOCK_ULTRACODE=1`'s PR review agent.
  - **Attachments** (#2321, iOS `NewAgentAttachments`, `NewAgentAttachments.kt`): under the first message, **Add Images or Files** opens the composer's sources (**Photos**: pictures only, up to 4; **Take photo**; **Files**: any type, each up to 200 MB, refused at once in a toast, iOS's words, when over). Each is uploaded to the computer picked as soon as it is picked, through the chat's upload service of that computer's hub group (`chatWiringSource().bindingsFor(SessionKey(machine, ""))`, plain machine ids); photos show as the composer's thumbnails, files one row each with Uploading… / size and ✕. An upload that fails goes, with a toast saying why (iOS `FileUploads.message`: "Couldn't send “name”: why", or the too-large / too-old words alone). Start waits with "Waiting for the files to finish uploading" under it. The first message is written as the composer writes its messages (`ComposerMessage` in core:domain, the same as `ChatComposerState.message`): the words and the files' paths, then the photos in an `<attached_images>` block, drawn as thumbnails above it; photos alone say "Please look at the photos". iOS and the web send the photos as image data instead. The tab is still named from what was typed. A launcher that takes its first message with the start (Codex and other CLIs, `agentType != "claude"`) has no add row (iOS and the web refuse only Codex; on Android only Claude starts in the background with its first message sent on its own, other launchers carry it in the start request, so only Claude takes attachments for now); with attachments already there, Start is off with "Codex takes no attachments" and iOS's line on picking another launcher. Picking another computer takes the files out with "The files uploaded to “…” were removed…" and uploads the photos again to the new one (iOS keeps its photos, which are not on a computer). Closing the form without starting takes them out (uploads stop, photos taken for it leave the cache), as iOS's sheet does; so does the form's view model going. Agents' New session, a space's new tab (which now also starts Claude in the background, as Agents does) and the knowledge base's page all offer them; previews, tests and the demo (no hub services) do not. Tests `NewAgentAttachmentsTest`, `NewAgentAttachmentsShotsTest` (`build/new-agent-attachments-shots/`), `ComposerMessageTest`; mock `MOCK_UPLOAD_KBPS=64` (uploading), `MOCK_UPLOAD_OLD_NODE=1` (refused: the toast, the file gone).
  - **A new worktree** (#4532, iOS `NewAgentWorktreeSection`, `docs/integrations/terminal.md` "A new agent in a worktree"): a moment after the folder stops changing the form asks `POST …/worktree/status` (`AgentCreationService.worktreeStatus`; null from an older node or offline: nothing shown). For a folder in a repository (and not a tab in an existing space) a Worktree section has **In a new worktree** and, when on, a Branch field whose placeholder is the node's own pick (`AgentWorktree.branch`: persona/<slug of the first message, else the folder's name>) and the path it checks out to. Start makes it first (`…/worktree/add`); a refusal says why in the node's words (git's for `worktree_failed`) and nothing starts; the agent then starts in the returned path, while recents and the machine's default folder keep the folder picked (`AgentStartOperation.folder`). Tests `AgentWorktreeTest`, `AgentCreationModelTest.worktreesAreAskedForMadeAndRemoved`; mock `MOCK_AGENT_WORKTREE=1` (folders under /Users/me/code/<repo>; a branch with "taken" is refused).
  - **Workspace** (#7259, the same rule as iOS and the web, `NewAgentWorkspaces.kt`): when a computer is picked the form reads the hub's workspace catalog (`GET /api/v1/workspaces`, `AgentCreationService.workspaces`, that computer's rows; an older hub, a failure or no rows: no section, no error) and lists its Orca and Paseo workspaces that can take an agent (`available` and `online` not false) under **Workspace**, by project (the project's name, else its folder's name; sorted, "Other workspaces" last), each project's main checkout first, then its worktrees, then its folders. A row is drawn as a recent folder is: the title (else the branch, else the folder's name), then "Main checkout", "Worktree" or "Folder" with " · branch" when a Git row's branch is not its title, then the folder. Nothing is picked at first; a pick takes the Directory row's place with **Choose by folder** (back to the folder picked before), and the start sends `workspace_id` (the row's `source_id`), `host` (its `source`) and its folder, never replacing the computer's remembered folder. A recent folder, a folder from the browser, a Recent card or another computer clear it; while one is picked **In a new worktree** is not offered. Other hub groups' rows come back with their machine ids translated (`GroupTranslation.kt`). Tests `NewAgentWorkspacesTest`, `AgentCreationServiceTest.workspacesAreTheComputersCatalogRowsAndNothingOnFailure`.
  - **Closing an agent in its worktree** (#4532, iOS `WorktreeCleanupSection`): Close Agent and Delete Chat (the chat's menu, `ChatChrome`; the Agents list's long press and swipe, `AgentOperationDialog`) ask `worktree/status` for the session's folder when its computer is online; for one Persona made (`ours`) the dialog has **Keep worktree** (the default) / **Remove**, then **Delete the branch … too**, what removing loses in red (changed files; with the branch, its commits main lacks) and **I understand these changes will be lost**, without which the button stays off. After the close or delete went through, `WorktreeCleanups.cleanUp` removes it (`force` only when losses were confirmed), trying again up to 6 times a second apart while the node still lists the agent (`worktree_in_use`), and says how it went app-wide (`LocalAppFeedback`). The chat's binding reaches the group's hub through `ChatAppBindings.worktrees` (plain machine ids). Tests `WorktreeCleanupTest`; mock `MOCK_AGENT_WORKTREE=1 MOCK_WORKTREES=1` (the widget worktree has 2 changed files and 3 unmerged commits).
  - **To-do detail: due date, reminder, priority** (#4636, iOS `TodoPlanSection`): under the title and notes a card with **Due date** (and **All day**, **Date**, **Time**), **Remind me** (**Date**, **Time**, "Reminded …" once pushed) and **Priority** (High / Normal / Low). A change is saved 400 ms after the last step (`TasksStore.setTodoPlan`, one `PATCH /api/v1/todos/{id}` with all three; a switch turned off sends `null`); on failure the app says why and the controls go back to the to-do. New dates start tomorrow at 18:00 (all day) and at the next full hour. Tests `TodoPlanDraftTest`, `TodoPlanBodyTest`.
  - **To-do detail: stage bar** (#4636, iOS `TodoStageBar`): `Todo.stage` (plan, develop, review, merge, deploy, verify; merged by the hub from `assistant.todo_stages`) draws a six-step bar under the title card: passed steps ticked green, the current one in the accent color with a ring, the rest hollow; read as "Stage: Develop (step 2 of 6)". No stage, or a name this build doesn't know, draws nothing (`TodoStage.of`). Test `TodoStageTest`.
  - **To-do detail: dictated notes** (#4636, iOS): while the notes are edited, a mic at the end of the field (`PersonaIcons.dictate`, "Dictate notes") opens the phone's own voice input (`rememberDictation` from feature/chat, now public, with the prompt "Go ahead; what you say goes into the notes"); what was heard goes at the end of the notes (`withDictation`), saved with Save as before. No recognizer on the phone: no mic.
  - **Blocked launchers** (#4530, iOS `LauncherAvailability` / `LauncherBlockedHelp`, `docs/server/launcher-health.md`): `AgentLauncher.health` is the hub's record for that computer. Only `blocked` grays a row (`LauncherAvailability.kt` `block`): listed at half opacity with one line per failure class (not in the terminal, key or sign-in rejected, gateway can't use the model, CLI too old, no answer, failed its last check); failing and repairing stay pickable. Choosing a grayed row opens `LauncherBlockedSheet` instead of picking it: why, How to fix, the hub's record under Details, **Add provider** for `not_in_terminal` on a primary-group computer (opens Settings › Model providers, whose + adds one; iOS opens the wizard itself), **Try anyway** picks it, and the row keeps its line. The form starts on a launcher that isn't blocked (`initialLauncher`, as `LauncherChoices.initial`), also when the agent type changes; when Follow model settings resolves to a blocked launcher, its line is under that row. Tests `LauncherAvailabilityTest`; mock `MOCK_LAUNCHER_HEALTH=1` (`work_gw` not in the terminal, `team_gw` auth).
- **Devices** (Settings › Devices): revoked credentials separate dim section Revoked at…; unused devices Added at…, explains credentials. Unsupported 404/no database 503 explained directly.

## Extensions (Settings › Extensions, #1206)

Same iOS specification ([extensions.md](../extensions/extensions.md) ("Hub API", "App: Settings › Extensions")). With hello `extension_manager` (`plugin_manager` before #1844), Settings gains an Extensions row in its Extensions section, counts total/needs-attention (`ExtensionText.listSummary`, reload on returning home/reconnect); no demo entry.

- **New/old APIs** (#1846, [extensions.md](../extensions/extensions.md) ("Naming and compatibility")): features `extensions` uses `/api/v1/extensions/…` (list `extensions`, UI place `extension` / `extension_name`, market count `extensions`, preview `extension`); otherwise pre-#1844/no hello uses `/api/v1/plugins/…` and legacy keys. `HttpExtensionManagement(legacy = …)` / `HttpVoiceExtensions(legacy = …)` choose by selected hub at call time; contracts accept both keys (`@JsonNames`, `WireSerializer` aliases). WS `extension_ui` / `plugin_ui` decode to `ExtensionUiEvent` (new hub sends both, setting same badge twice unchanged). UI slots `extension_ui_slots` or `plugin_ui_slots`. Route `@SerialName`, debug names (`page=plugins`, `plugin:<id>`, `plugin-ui:…`), screenshots/test tags unchanged.
- **Market signatures** (#1848): `signature: {state, key_id?, detail?}` adds Signed / Unsigned; other states (signature mismatch/key change) show danger icon and reason (`CatalogText.signatureProblem`, no `detail` → Confirm on hub machine). Legacy missing field shows nothing.

- Data: `ExtensionManagement` in `core:contracts` (`available` follows selected features, list/detail/toggle/full config/set-clear secrets), `HubExtension` shared voice (version/origin/enabled/description/provides/missing/warnings), `ExtensionDetail` adds form/config/secrets/permissions… (`decode` reads same object twice); `HttpExtensionManagement` in `core:hub` (hub-owned APIs under reserved `-`; validate ids/secret names against path injection; 400 `invalid_config` → `ExtensionConfigRejected(field, message)`), injected per current pairing in `MachineManagementModule`.
- Pages `ExtensionsScreen.kt` (`ExtensionsPage`, `ExtensionPage`, `ExtensionRoute(extensionId)`, wide-screen right pane), `ExtensionsState.kt` (`ExtensionText`, `ExtensionDraft`, page models) in `feature:settings`.
  - List: name/state (Enabled / Starting / Needs setup / Disabled / Unavailable on this hub / Error; `running`/`stopped` both Enabled, small detail says running or not), style state dot or danger warning; footer hub-machine-only installation.
  - Detail: state/version/origin, description, Missing…, errors; Enabled; Settings from `form.fields`: text (uri/email keyboards), numeric (raw typing, inline integer/number error disables save), switch, select (sheet), text list (remove/add), multi-select switches, objects separate sections. Save submits full config (empty strings/lists omitted for defaults); rejected-field reason inline, draft retained, unrenderable-field errors at top. Unsaved edits survive toggle/secret changes. Secrets only Set / Not set (required), sheet hidden input, clear existing; Provides / Capabilities / About (version/previous/origin/publisher/installation location).. manager.json entries read-only, no switches/form/secrets.
  - One machine (`format: machine`, by name, of the field's `x-os`) and one launcher (`format: launcher`, the hub's `GET /api/v1/launchers` read once per open page, hidden ones left out, a display name before the name) are choice sheets like a select, **Not set** first, a value naming none of them first and marked; a launcher is typed until the list is in or when it can't be read. An `integer` has a − / + stepper in its field, held to `minimum` / `maximum` (#2448, as iOS and the web). Tests `ExtensionPickersTest`; shots `ExtensionPickerShotsTest`, three styles × light / dark, `feature/settings/build/extension-picker-shots/`.
- Debug `--es debug.openLink 'persona://open?target=settings&page=plugins'`, `--es debug.openPage plugin:<id>`; batch `SHOTS_PLUGINS=1`.
- Tests `HttpExtensionManagementTest` (`core:hub`, MockWebServer list/legacy/detail/hub.json/toggle-config-secret paths/bodies/rejected fields/invalid ids and secret names/malformed responses), `ExtensionsStateTest` (`feature:settings`, copy/path-edit-cleanup/numbers/full-save/rejections/unsaved retention/failure/read-only/list).

### Extension UI and marketplace (#1329)

Specification [extensions.md](../extensions/extensions.md) ("Declarative UI", "Extension marketplace"). `ExtensionManagement` also implements `ExtensionUi` (`uiPlaces`, `renderUi`, `uiAction`) and `ExtensionMarkets` (markets/catalog/preview/install/upgrade/uninstall); `listing()` includes `can_install`, `markets`; `ExtensionDetail.ui` retains only `plugin_page`. `HttpExtensionManagement` maps 404 `{code: "unsupported"}` (extension says launcher's gateway isn't its own, #1339) to `ExtensionUiUnsupported`.

- **Renderer** (`ExtensionViewContent.kt`, `LazyListScope.extensionViewItems` + `rememberExtensionView`): eight items, design-system only. text uses body/secondary/caption/`JCodeBlock` per style; metric label/number-size value/detail; progress `JLinearProgress(tone)` + percent/detail; row `JRow` with `JTag(tone)` badge, clickable only with action; button full-width `JButton` (submit primary, destructive danger, others secondary, single-line); link external-link row, https only, in-App; five fields text/secret (no echo/prefill)/numeric/switch/select sheet; divider `JDivider`. Adjacent rows/links/switches/selects grouped. New `Tone` / `PersonaPalette.tone()` maps positive green, caution amber, danger red, neutral gray. Unknown items skipped. `confirm` uses `JDialog`; submit includes all page field values; result view replaces page, toast App feedback, `open_url` https only, empty response rerenders; extension `state` passed back. Logic `ExtensionUiState.kt` (`ExtensionViewModel`, `ExtensionPlacesModel`, `ExtensionViewText`).
- **Extension pages**: detail Pages section when `ui`, opens `ExtensionUiRoute(extensionId, uiId, title)` using View title; failure Retry, empty state, pull refresh.
- **Launcher page**: previously no detail. With `plugin_manager`, machine launcher rows clickable; `LauncherRoute(machineId, name)` shows model/gateway host/type, then quota (#1748 below), then `launcher_section`s (`context` launcher name/raw hub machine id). Unsupported sections omitted without error; title appears after View, no flash.
- **Marketplace** (`ExtensionMarketScreen.kt` / `ExtensionMarketState.kt`): Extensions list with `markets: true` gets Extension marketplace (`ExtensionMarketRoute`) above installed extensions. The official market is named **Official extensions** in lists, filter chips and the catalog (`OfficialMarket.named`, #5017), covered by `ExtensionOfficialMarketShotsTest`. Catalog search 0.3-second debounce, stale responses discarded; market chips when multiple; name/description/version/installed/update/unavailable reason/tags, failed-market red text above. `ExtensionCatalogRoute(marketId, extensionId)` detail badges/description/tags/Capabilities/Provides/About. `CatalogText.actions`: uninstalled/installable Install, update Upgrade to x, installed from this market Uninstall (dialog Keep data), other origin tells user to change on hub machine. Install/upgrade first preview downloads/verifies, sheet version/capabilities/warnings; extra upgrade permissions red warning/New section/red Agree to new permissions and upgrade, nothing installs before consent. A preview past the earliest `expires_at` of it and its bundle members (by the phone's clock, also while the sheet is open) says it expired and offers **Preview Again** instead of the yes: a waiting operation is canceled and the package previewed afresh, never agreed with the old preview (#1316); the hub still refuses an expired preview by its own clock, and a preview without `expires_at` (older hubs) is confirmable as before. Manage markets (`ExtensionMarketsRoute`): address/count/token/read time/error/warning, actions Refresh now/Set or replace token/Open home/Remove market. Add sheet passes address unchanged to hub (no App validation, #1373), explains https, github:owner/repo, git:…; write-only token; hub refusal inline.
- **Command-line tools and sign-in** (#4509, protocol #4507): install `tools` step Installing command-line tools, each program row (reused Uses this machine's, downloading bytes/progress via `OperationText.toolDetail` / `toolFraction`), `tools_failed` Tool installation failed:…. Success `next: "auth"` opens detail and starts sign-in (`ExtensionRoute(id, signIn = true)`). With `auth`, detail Sign in below status (`ExtensionSignIn.kt`): Signed out / Waiting for confirmation / Signed in: account / Expired / Error: reason / Extension not running; Sign in / Sign in again / confirmed Sign out. Bottom card uses connector steps `ConnectorSignInModel` + `ConnectorSignInStep`; deviceLink `device_code` adds clickable in-App login URL/QR. device_code/qr repoll same step every interval. `extension_auth` event (client hello declares feature) updates detail/list in place; list appends Signed out; About lists CLI tools, reused Existing on machine; `auth.page` excluded from Pages. Tests `ExtensionAuthStateTest`, `HttpExtensionAuthTest`; `ExtensionAuthShotsTest`, three styles × light / dark, `feature/settings/build/extension-auth-shots`.
- **Quota** (#1748, iOS `LauncherQuotaSection`, web `LauncherQuota.tsx`, `docs/integrations/launchers.md` ("Quota")): `MachineManagement.launcherQuota` `GET /api/v1/launchers/{name}/quota?machine=<machine>`; pull/top ↻ sends `refresh=1`, hub waits max 45 sec, timeout50. Routes machine suffix, response reading-machine id translated back.
  Title Quota · gateway_name (legacy gateway); group description, key budget, accounts/providers (bad-state caution, collapsed per model), key usage, unlimited note; footer gateway key name/reader machine/age.
  ≥70% caution, ≥90%/exhausted danger. `QuotaText` in `LauncherQuotaState.kt` matches iOS/web `lib/quota.ts`.
  Unsupported shows hub reason/no refresh; failed refresh retains old result with reason. Tests `LauncherQuotaTest`, `HttpMachineManagementTest`; `LauncherQuotaShotsTest`, styles × light / dark, `feature/settings/build/launcher-quota-shots/`.
- **Auto-upgrade, machine toggles and uninstall** (#3049, web/iOS, [extensions.md](../extensions/extensions.md) ("Auto-upgrade", "Machines (`format: machine`)")):
  - Market extension `HubExtension.autoUpgrade` detail adds Upgrade below Enabled, toggle `PUT …/{id}/-/auto-upgrade`; new version row with `ExtensionText.upgradeDetail`, Confirm (pending/rolled-back caution) or Upgrade opens catalog preview/confirmation; no `market` means text only. Toggle failure says Couldn't enable/disable auto-upgrade: reason; no update shows last automatic-upgrade/rollback result. List pending version appends New version needs confirmation.
  - `auto_upgrade_paused` (switch off and greyed out) picks the footer by value (`ExtensionText.upgradeFooter`, #1895): `disabled` paused while the extension is off; `market_removed` its market was removed, so no upgrades until it is added again with the same address; any other value only "paused". Removing a market says its extensions stop upgrading.
  - Manage markets top Auto-upgrade extensions master (`GET/PUT /api/v1/extensions/-/upgrades`), explanatory copy follows value; hidden for old404/no markets.
  - While that master is off (`auto: false`), each extension's Auto-upgrade switch keeps its own saved value but is greyed and can't be flipped; the note says it applies again once automatic updates are back on (#4748, as the web). Unknown (loading, an old hub) leaves it editable. Tests `ExtensionGlobalAutoTest`; shots `ExtensionSettingsShotsTest.marketNotesWithUpdatesOffEverywhere` (`example-notes-updates-off-*`).
  - Form `type: array`, `items.format: machine` uses `MachineSwitches`: filter OS by `items.os`, repository machine id/name/OS/online only so metric changes don't redraw; online/offline text. `node_machines` enabled machines add program status (`ExtensionText.programStatus`); computer-providing extension (cua) adds other online Mac Cua Driver status (Not installed / Not running / Missing permission / Locked / Ready), from `Machine.computer` → `ExtensionText.cuaStatus`, web `machineStatus`, omitted before node response. Unknown ids still listed/removable. Immediate `ExtensionModel.saveNow` changes only this saved config field, preserves other unsaved draft; rejection rolls back and explains above. `confirm` / `x-confirm` asks before enabling only. Forms with only these fields have no Save.
  - Market-origin `market:…` detail bottom red Uninstall even config-read-only, shared `UninstallDialog`; wide adjacent list reloads via `ExtensionChanges` after uninstall/toggle/auto-upgrade/save. **Keep data checked by default**, like web; `ExtensionMarkets.uninstall.keepData` now defaults true.
  - **A pack with its members** (#1154, as the web's `UninstallDialog`; [extensions.md](../extensions/extensions.md) "Dependencies, exports and packs (#1854)"):
    - For a pack (non-empty `extension_pack`), `UninstallDialog` adds **Also remove the pack's members**, listing them by name (`rememberExtensionNames` reads the installed list while a dialog is open; an id only when that member isn't installed). It is off by default, so they stay. The market page reads the members from the installed detail when the dialog opens, since the catalog doesn't list them.
    - Ticked, `uninstall` and `startUninstall` send `members=1`. The `has_dependents` question and its async Retry keep the choice (`ExtensionCascade.members`). An operation's own Retry is the hub's, which reruns with the options it saved.
    - Done, the notice counts the members from `removed_members` (`ExtensionText.uninstalled`). A hub that doesn't list them says no result came back. An operation, which carries no list but ends only once every member is gone, says the members went too.
    - A 409 `partial_uninstall` becomes `ExtensionPartialUninstall`, and `PackPartialDialog` says what was removed, what wasn't (with the hub's reason) and what wasn't tried, by name. The pack is still installed and nothing was put back. An async one shows the hub's own `error` on the operation card, with Retry.
    - Tests: `HttpExtensionOperationsTest.aPackGoesWithItsMembersAndAPartialUninstallSaysWhichWent`, `ExtensionOperationStateTest.removingAPackWithItsMembersKeepsTheChoice`. `ExtensionPackUninstallShotsTest` taps the dialog (off by default, then ticked) and writes `extension-pack-uninstall-*` and `extension-pack-partial-*` in three styles × light / dark into `build/i18n-shots/`.
  - Tests `HttpExtensionManagementTest` (fields/toggle paths/bodies/old master unavailable/machine/node_machines), `ExtensionsStateTest` (upgrade copy/machine instant save/rejection/uninstall/Cua/auto failure/list follows detail), `ExtensionMarketStateTest` (master). `ExtensionUpgradeShotsTest` (detail locked Mac, adjacent-list row removal, enable confirmation, uninstall, manage markets), styles × light / dark, `feature/settings/build/extension-upgrade-shots/`.
- Debug `--es debug.openPage plugin-ui:<extension>:<ui>`, `market`, `market-plugin:<market>:<extension>`, `markets`, `launcher:<machine>:<launcher>`; `SHOTS_PLUGINS=1` captures `plugin-example-gateway`, `plugin-ui`, `plugin-market*`, `plugin-markets`, `launcher`. Mock example-gateway has always-unsupported `launcher_section` elsewhere, must not appear.

Bars grouped/stacked, negatives down; max 4 lines. Full-page charts rounded right ticks, sparse actual-width labels with no endpoint crowding (`ChartMath.labelIndices`); compact cards no ticks, first/last labels only. Tap/horizontal drag selects column, shows label and series display (or unit formatting, `1.2M tokens`), tap clears. stat_grid 2–3 per row, four as 2×2. `JSegmented` immediately sends ui.action `{name: selected}` (included in submit), optimistic rollback.
- **Inbox item pages** (`inbox_item`, #1683): extension connectors such as Lark can render their item body. After general slots, separately `uiPlaces("inbox_item")` (hub lists only by placement), keep connector-bearing places in `ExtensionSlotsState.inboxItems`, reread on Inbox tab `loadInboxItems`. LarkChatRoute links not tracked/swapped. `LocalInboxItemPages` → `InboxItemPages` (`feature:inbox/InboxItemPages.kt`): source matching connector opens `InboxItemUiRoute(itemId, extensionId, uiId, title)` for list/deep link, right pane/left selected. Others/old hubs retain chat/mail/Moments (`inboxItemPage`).

Item version/update/count/done changes redraw. Extension does not duplicate title/tier/summary/done; reply composer/submit and View in chat are extension buttons, no App extra bar, all clients same.

After successful render, refresh/action failure only notices and keeps page. No demo.

### Host-owned extension settings (#2397)

Android follows [the shared settings contract](../extensions/extension-settings.md). Every `ExtensionPage`, including direct navigation,
installation/sign-in returns and the standalone Settings fallback, delegates the standard sections to `ExtensionSettingsPage`:
**Enabled → Extension configuration (including Save) → Information → Updates → Danger zone**. The configuration slot retains schema fields,
secrets, sign-in, machine selection, custom-page links and connector/channel configuration links. Contribution switches, isolation and
permissions remain host diagnostics after the identity rows. Custom extension views, connector/channel business pages and the marketplace
consent screen are not replacement extension settings pages.

- Built-in, configured, development, attached, market-installed and other installed sources all use the same renderer. Unsupported check,
  auto-upgrade and uninstall controls stay visible, disabled and explained. Unknown manager capabilities fail closed. Source metadata, not
  extension-owned settings or arbitrary URLs, determines lifecycle authority. `attached` is decoded from existing manager metadata.
- Information always includes installed version, selectable extension ID, source, runtime and installation provenance. Missing values say
  **Not provided**; local installation directories are never displayed. Homepages must be valid HTTPS URLs without embedded credentials.
- Save belongs to configuration, before Information. Enabled, Auto-upgrade and Check for updates never submit dirty configuration. A failed
  canonical read keeps the last detail and draft but blocks lifecycle writes and Save until a successful retry.
- Checks refresh the owning manager's market catalog and then reload its canonical extension detail. Missing markets, catalog errors and
  missing catalog entries are not reported as current. The saved auto-upgrade preference stays on while paused; disabled-extension,
  removed-market and manager-global pauses are explained separately. A global preference read failure is visible rather than assumed on.
- Catalog/upgrade routes retain the manager through consent, installation and sign-in; operation pushes are filtered by that owner.
  The application facade forwards the existing per-extension and global auto-upgrade APIs. Uninstall confirmation includes installed version
  and defaults to keeping data; dependency refusal still uses the existing explicit cascade confirmation.

**Existing API boundaries; no server changes:** the detail response does not provide a declared runtime kind (`kind` is its first contribution
point), so Runtime says **Not provided**. Rollback has an execution endpoint but no consent preview; when a previous version exists, Roll back
is visible and disabled with that reason. There is no non-mutating uninstall/dependency preflight endpoint; Android never issues DELETE to
probe before confirmation. Pack-member uninstall, a complete history list and detailed installed dependency/pack inspection remain outside
this change; the existing Android member-removal gap is described below. Adding safe runtime metadata or preview contracts requires a
separate server decision, not a fabricated field or operation in the client.

Regression tests: `ExtensionSettingsStateTest`, `ExtensionSettingsShotsTest`, `CatalogExtensionManagerTest` and
`ExtensionCatalogRouteCompatibilityTest`. Screenshots render actual `ExtensionPage` instances for built-in realtime voice, installed Example
Notes and configured Example Weather, in all three styles × light/dark, using Robolectric native graphics. They are Compose/JVM evidence,
not emulator, physical-device or production deployment evidence. Images go to the settings module's generated build output, in the
`extension-settings-shots` subdirectory, and are attached to the PR, not committed.

### Extension model and declarative contributions (#2226)

Specification [extensions.md](../extensions/extensions.md) ("Turning contributions on and off", "Dependencies, exports and bundles", "Declarative contributions", "Sandbox"). Android UI/HTTP only, no extension program execution or generated `ExtensionWire.kt` edits.

- **Contribution toggles**: detail contributions listed individually, disableable via `setContribution` `PUT …/-/contributions/{point}/{id}`, update from detail response; unsaved settings retained, failure preserves state/reason. can_disable false status-only; via connector says controlled in connector settings; manager.json read-only. Legacy absent field uses contributes/Provides list.
- **Dependencies/bundles**: preview depends/bundle/unresolved, manifest extension_pack. `BundlePreview` confirmation lists dependency status/members/every extension installed-upgraded/permissions/additions/warnings. Disabled/unknown dependencies or missing members block confirmation; flattened bundle evaluates member dependencies as group. Confirm returns all `{preview_id, permissions_digest}` in original order, never installs only main silently. Legacy previews unchanged.
- **Installed dependencies and pack members** (#1315): the manager-bound extension detail retains `depends` and `extension_pack`. Its settings page lists each dependency's name/id, required version and readiness (including the installed version when too old), then each pack member's id, version constraint and market. These sections are read-only and absent when an older hub supplies neither field. Unknown installed dependency states ask to refresh details, without changing installation-preview guidance.
- **Not-ready dependency** (#2469): state not_ready explicitly Dependency not ready, complete dependency setup then preview again including same-bundle dependencies. Only ok Satisfied; unknown conservative/blocking, legacy fields unchanged.
- **Declaration assembly**: Root `ExtensionDeclarationsHost` supplies prompts/themes/triage templates by current hub identity (`ExtensionDeclarationsModel`), resumed-only fetch, immediate on return then 30 sec; reload after local install/upgrade/uninstall/disable/contribution change. New API 404 means unsupported/empty; other errors retain data, never treat outage as disabled. No demo.
- **Quick prompts**: GET …/-/prompts → `LocalExtensionPrompts`, composer slash/+ lists, single-line title, identity extension/id. Choosing replaces text, cursor end/focus, preserves attachments, no send/command callback. Built-in behavior unchanged, shared main/Agent composer.
- **Theme colors**: GET …/-/themes → Appearance Theme color; hub problems excluded, `ExtensionThemeColors` validates light / dark ×3 styles on page/composited translucent cards/solid panels/raised-sunken/notice fills (accent≥3, supplied text≥4.5). Only accent/positive/caution/danger/ai, other roles/layout unchanged. Unselected original pixel-identical; selection per hub identity, no same-name cross-hub reuse; missing after disable clears selection/default. Only declared roles, not Agent states; custom-role buttons/checkmarks use readable design foreground.
- **Triage templates**: GET …/-/triage-templates → From template on top-level triage_rules text field. Preview title/source/full text; confirm edits only `ExtensionModel` draft, no setConfig, still Save; other fields untouched, multiline rule editing. No templates, no button.
- **Sandbox**: `HubExtension.sandbox` actual list/detail status; Runtime isolation distinguishes active/requested-not-active/off, why/outbound counts. Legacy omitted hidden. No blanket claims extensions are unsandboxed or can read/write all user files; install says check detail afterward, no phone toggle.
- **Not wired to Android UI**: `exports` schema / `contributions_to_me` and uninstalling bundle members with `members=1` have no new edit / delete entry. On `has_dependents`, disabling or uninstalling from the extension detail page lists dependents for confirmation, then retries with `cascade` (#5192); uninstalling from the marketplace does the same: a stepwise uninstall that fails with `has_dependents` asks (the operation does not name the dependents, so the dialog says "other extensions") and starts again with `cascade=1` (#5611); its Retry asks the same way, since the hub would retry without `cascade`. Existing bundle uninstall removes only the bundle itself and leaves members. Skills and instruction prompts are hub assistant preparation, not App execution. Triage templates use the existing extension configuration form, without a new source-rule browser.
- **Validation** `HttpExtensionModelTest` (new-old paths/toggles/sandbox/dependency digest/404-503), `ExtensionModelTest` (legacy absent/flat dependencies/draft retention/incomplete-preview blocking), `ExtensionThemesTest`, `UnselectedExtensionThemeTest` (installed-unselected components pixel-identical ×styles/light-dark), `ExtensionDeclarationsHostTest` disabled theme defaults, `ExtensionPromptsTest`. `ExtensionModelShotsTest` / `ExtensionPromptsShotsTest` native Robolectric actual pages/install/template/theme/composer, styles  × light / dark, module `build/extension-model-shots/`, `build/extension-prompt-shots/`. Not emulator/device measurement; separate device validation required.
- **Screenshot lesson**: Native Robolectric outer ModalBottomSheet may produce black PNG despite valid semantics/dimensions; capture actual install content, check nonbackground pixels, then inspect. Dialog capture dialog node, not obscured page.

## What sessions are waiting for (Settings › Session triage, #1837)

After hub triages stopped sessions ([agent-work.md](../assistant/agent-work.md)), `Session.work` adds a line on Agents and Knowledge project-Agent rows,
replacing latest text when stopped (done "✓ conclusion", waiting "waiting for… (wake in N minutes / retry now)", `Session.workLine`);
hub-managed waits (`WorkState.hubWaits`) enter Waiting. Settings › <name>'s settings › Session triage (`TriageSettingsPage`, GET/PUT `/api/v1/agents/triage`, all off by default),
Triage model opens model purpose agent.triage. Debug `--es debug.openPage triage`.

## Background workflows (#2935)

Hub marks Agents working while Workflow continues after main turn; no state change needed. Optional `Session.workflows` (legacy null), `background[].expires_at / run_id` in core/contracts;
unknown Workflow status treated complete (`Workflow.state`). Running workflows also in background kind workflow, avoid double count: backgroundCount
= nonworkflow entries + running workflows (legacy counts background). Copy `WorkflowSummary` core/domain.

- Agents `SlotRow`: note under preview `AgentRow(note = RowNote)`, ai `WorkflowIcon`, "Background workflow “parity-audit” · Verify 2/2 · 169/185+ · 14 min" (`+` while it runs, #3435), plus N more; legacy workflow-only background says Running in background. The note's numbers count to new ones when only they change (`JChangingText`).
- Main task card below sub-Agent row same summary.
- Shots `:feature:agents:testDebugUnitTest --tests '*AgentWorkflowRowShotsTest*'` (`build/workflow-row-shots/`); `WorkflowContractTest`, `WorkflowSummaryTest`.

### Timeline card (#3435)

The same card and rules as the web's (#3434, `web/src/lib/workflows.ts`): the rules are the pure `WorkflowTimeline` in core/domain, the card is
`JWorkflowCallCard` / `JWorkflowCard` in `core/designsystem/Workflows.kt`, written once for the three styles.

- **In the chat**: a Workflow call whose run the session lists (`Workflow.tool_use_id`, `ChatWorkflows` / `LocalChatWorkflows` from `ChatScreen`) draws
  `JWorkflowCallCard` in place of its row, inside the expanded execution trace (#2602). A collapsed work summary never previews the card, during or after the turn.
  The card itself is open while the run runs, folded to its head and summary once it ended; what the reader opened or folded (the run, a phase,
  "n more") lasts while the chat is open (`WorkflowFolds`). No listed run (an older hub, a run over a day old, not read yet): the row it always was.
- **In the status sheet**: `JWorkflowCard` per run, always open, the same parts.
- **Parts**: head (mark, name, status chip as before, `169/185+`, time); "Now <phase>: <agent>" while it runs, else the summary and a failed run's error;
  a progress bar in parts (done, running, failed, stopped) with its counts; one block per phase with `done/started` (`+` while more may start in it),
  open while lit or with agents running, folded once behind the run with all back (a red ✕ when some failed). Agents in start order, at most 12 rows,
  the quiet tail folded into "n more" (anchored to the end once the hub's 60-agent cap cut into the block, the earlier ones said above the rows).
  On a phone a row is its dot, label and time; a wide window (`WindowLayout.expanded`) adds the model and latest tool. A workflow agent's own page
  takes its model.
- **Motion**: only when a value changes, never on the first draw, nothing with reduced motion (`PersonaTheme.reduceMotion`): counts and times count
  (`JChangingText`), the status chip and the lit phase go over in color (`animateColorAsState`), phases and the card open and fold with
  `AnimatedVisibility` + `PersonaTheme.motion.size()`, the bar's parts move with `animateFloatAsState(motion.count)`, a new agent only fades in,
  a new phase mark fades in. No persistent animation.
- Tests: `WorkflowTimelineTest` (the web's cases: counts and `+`, blocks from `phase_stats` and from agents for older hubs, fold rules, the 12-row
  window and the cap anchor, the bar's parts), `WorkflowCardMotionTest` (core:designsystem: first draw, a count counting, reduced motion, a phase
  folding; before / mid / after frames in `build/workflow-motion-shots/`), `WorkflowShotsTest` (feature:chat: running, completed, failed and long cards,
  the status sheet's card and the task line, three styles × light / dark in `build/workflow-shots/`; folding and opening; the card in place of the call
  and the row without a run).
- Known gap: with a hub that pages the Agents list (`sessions_list`), list rows are summaries without `workflows` and Android does not fetch the open
  chat's full row with `sessions_lookup` yet, so the chat's cards (like the status sheet's runs and "View sub-agent") only show from full session rows.

## Translation (Settings › Translation, #1744)

Same as iOS (`docs/apple/ios.md` ("Translation")) / web (`docs/web/web.md`): Auto-translate uses hub model purpose translate for content not in target (default Simplified Chinese). Mail default on, selected Lark chats default none, others' messages only.

- Local settings survive hub change/re-pair: `TranslationSettings` / `TranslationPrefs` core:contracts, targets same assistantwire/inbox.go TranslateLanguages, unknown defaults; `StoredTranslationSettings` App SharedPreferences persona-translate, iOS translate.* keys, selected chat names JSON, malformed empty.
- Sync: `WiredDeviceSettings` reads device prefs translate on each connect (`GET /api/v1/devices/{id}/prefs`), sends PUT translate if local enabled/target/mail differ, all 3 including false; sends on changes, unreadable hub treated absent; failed retries next change/connect (`syncTranslation`). Hub pretranslates new mail (`assistant/inbox_translate.go`) into item translations[target]. Selected Lark chats stay local.
- `TranslationSettingsPage`: AI below Model configuration, target/Off summary; toggle, model translate summary/link, enabled target sheet, scope mail toggle/Lark N chats or No translation, selected rows with ✕. Picker selected first (even absent recently), pinned then recent via `ConnectorsManagement.larkChatChoices`: `/api/v1/lark/pinned` skip legacy, `/api/v1/lark/conversations?scope=chats&limit=60`, search name. Hide Lark section if `LocalLarkFeatures.chats` false. Demo explanation only.
- Inbox translations (#1745):
  - Mail rows pretranslated `InboxRecord.translations` JSON/list cache shows translated title/preview, title translation icon (`MessageItem.titleIcon`, absent unchanged). Android one preview still AI summary if present, then only title translated; iOS/web separate AI summary and sender:translated preview.
  - Mail page non-target title/body (HTML-to-text fallback) `POST /api/v1/translate/mail`, translated title/plain body, Translated to Simplified Chinese · Show original toggle; Translating…/Couldn't translate… while original kept; clipped partial translation note matches iOS/web. Failure only Hub cannot translate now; showing original, no raw hub/network error.
  - Selected Lark chats' other-people non-target text gray `LarkTranslationLine` in bubble footer; own/recalled/encrypted olk excluded (`translatable`). Offline no request, next appearance retry. Visible requests batched within300 ms, max 30 via POST `/api/v1/translate`.
  - Local language guess: no iOS NaturalLanguage; `LanguageGuess` core:domain matches web lib/translate.ts writing systems then common Latin words/accents; <6 letters unknown, Simplified/Traditional same, uncertain Latin foreign only for non-Latin target.
  - `Translator` feature:inbox / LocalTranslator created TranslationModule with selected hub `HttpTranslationTransport`; per-run text/mail cache, failures/empty not reasked; demo null in Root.
- Debug translate, optional debug.translateChats 1 opens picker, or `--es debug.openLink 'persona://open?target=settings&page=translation'`.
- Tests `HttpDeviceSettingsTest` (translate read/three fields-only write/legacy), `HttpConnectorsManagementTest` (pins/dedupe/id fallback/legacy), App `StoredTranslationSettingsTest` (defaults/persist/clear/malformed), `TranslationSyncTest` (no redundant/no chats/legacy-failure retry), feature:settings `TranslationSettingsShotsTest` (toggle/scope/language/mail/chat names/remove/picker order/search); Inbox `LanguageGuessTest`, `HttpTranslationTransportTest`, `TranslatorTest` (batch/cap/target split/failure cache/offline/mail once), `InboxTranslationShotsTest` (off/unselected/own/already-Chinese excluded, original toggle).

## Voice chat (hub voice extension)

Full-duplex chat with hub voice extension ([extensions.md](../extensions/extensions.md) ("Voice")); listens while page open, speaking directly interrupts Persona. `feature/voice`, transport `HttpVoiceExtensions` core/hub, contract `VoiceExtensions` core/contracts, `VoiceRoute(extensionId,title)`.

- Entry: two public APIs. `voiceEntries(navigator, extensions, identity, onModelConfiguration = null)` registers route (purpose-id callback, Root like monitoring; demo no service/explanation). `rememberVoiceExtension(extensions, active)` when active reads first running voice extension, failures404 treated none, re-pair reload; host decides Voice chat menu. `ShellSession.voice` like metrics rebuilds transport per current route/pinned client/token, pairing change cancels.
- Permission RECORD_AUDIO first visit, declares MODIFY_AUDIO_SETTINGS. Missing empty state Voice chat needs microphone / Listens only here, leaving ends, no background recording. Denied says No microphone permission / Enable Persona microphone in system settings. Allow microphone asks again if possible, after two denials opens App system settings, rechecks on return.
- Model: purpose plugin.id via modelChoice and extension /models. Non-unverified status error shows unavailable/reason; missing configured model says choose again; old404 uses extension default or omits model; other read error only Retry, never treated no config ([model-config.md](../assistant/model-config.md) ("When changes take effect")). Read only start/restart. Host onModelConfiguration(purposeId), `voicePurposeId`, enables menu/error-page Model configuration; absent top-right directly Voice, error page only Retry.
- Voice picker lists model voices (Female/Male · Chinese, English · Default), check current, tap closes. Failed list notice, chat uses prior voice. Saved per pairing `hub-data/<pairing>/voice.json`, each extension, deleted unpair; absent voice falls back without deleting preference (other models may restore). Voice/model change restarts chat.
- Page top extension/model·voice; `VoiceOrb` fast pulse speaking, slow breathing/volume listening, spinner connecting, gray mute/end; Glass glowing orb, Brief ink disk with highlighter speaking ring, Board state square/five levels. `VoiceStatus` Connecting / Listening / Speaking—start talking to interrupt / Muted / tool… / Ended, errors red. `VoiceCaption` user right bubble, Persona body, provisional dim, latest auto-scroll. `VoiceControl` three labeled round Mute/Unmute, Interrupt only speaking, Hang up; ended left Restart. Glass glow blue listening/mint speaking.
- `VoiceProtocol.kt`: start frame with optional model/voice once connected; ready before recording/playback, sample rates from ready with defaults16000/24000 for missing/invalid. Uplink20 ms PCM16 mono binary, zeros when muted; binary downlink plays immediately. Transcript same-id replace, blank new lines ignored; multiple tools show latest unfinished; clear flushes playback; error shown, fatal ends; Interrupt sends type interrupt and flushes. WS no read timeout,15 sec ping.
- End/error: leave/hangup/ON_STOP ends, no background recording; keep screen on during chat then release.1001 extension restart or midchat loss says Connection lost (service restart/network); restart below. Upgrade404 No voice extension; prestart failure Cannot connect: localized HTTP/network (403 read-only,5xx service not responding); microphone failure Can't open microphone; other close reason shown.
- `AndroidVoiceAudio`: VOICE_COMMUNICATION plus system echo cancellation/noise suppression, MODE_IN_COMMUNICATION, wired/Bluetooth/USB/LE/hearing aid preferred else loudspeaker not earpiece; transient call audio focus pauses other music; restores mode/device/focus. `VoiceAudio` abstraction permits fake audio/extension JVM `VoiceSession`/`VoicePage` tests.
- Old differences: background ends (old recorded while page existed), audio focus, hearing aid routing, two-denial settings, per-pair voice not global preferences, no legacy local model choice, direct Voice button without model navigation rather than single-item menu.

Mock `MOCK_VOICE=1` running extension/models/voices/plugin.voice and scripted WS: ready then1.5 sec user caption,4 sec Check calendar tool,7 sec Persona speaking+silent audio,11 sec tool end,30 sec speech end, interrupt returns clear. Modes drop1001 at 14 sec, bad-model, missing-model, old-hub404 purpose, fatal error, off404 WS, slow ready8 sec. `GET /mock/voice?mode=<mode>` live change (restart changes hub_id invalidates pairing), GET same logs start/interrupt/audio count. Emulator `-no-audio`, debug.voiceAudio fake yields silent uplink/discarded downlink/fake slowly varying level, Release none:

```sh
MOCK_VOICE=1 PORT=<port> python3 scripts/mock/mock_hub.py
adb -s <emulator> reverse tcp:<port> tcp:<port>
# Pair via debug.mockAddress as in App shell and direct page entry, then:
adb -s <emulator> shell pm grant <package> android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO
adb -s <emulator> shell am start -n <package>/persona.android.debug.DebugEntryActivity \
  --es debug.openPage voice --es debug.voiceAudio fake --es debug.style glass --es debug.theme dark
```

Voice debug opens id voice in Persona. Debug catalog Voice chat includes orbs/status/captions/buttons.

Verification 2026-11-02: `VoiceProtocolTest` rewritten old VoiceTest plus rates/missing frames/tools/levels, `VoiceTextTest` states/orbs/voice description/failures/models/routes, `VoiceSessionTest` noaudio-before-ready/start-once/mute-zeros/transcript-tools/clear-interrupt/nonfatal-fatal/1001-drop/prestart/microphone/normalclose/ignore-after-stop, `VoicePageTest` legacy-default/no unavailable fallback/read-error not empty/voice persistence-restart/missing voice omitted/list-failure usable/restart reread/background end, `VoicePrefsTest`, core:hub `HttpVoiceExtensionsTest` list/voices/models/config/pairing cancel/bidirectional WS/single-close/upgrade status. API 35 fake-audio emulator captures styles × light / dark permissions/denied/connecting/listening/user/tool/speaking/end/mute/voice/drop/badmodel, Glass dark extra missing/fatal/off/old. Tested Interrupt received/flush, voice restart+paired file, background-return ended, Restart/Hangup, KEEP_SCREEN_ON set/cleared. Device-only recording/echo cancellation/loudspeaker self-hearing, headset/Bluetooth routing, focus, real extension latency/interrupt feel all untested; latency not measured.

Ported 2026-11-02 from old App/iOS `ShellNeedsYou`. feature/assistant NeedsYou.kt/AssistantData.kt/AssistantScreen.kt; host Root.kt/NeedsYouLinks.kt.

- Needs you now: Persona new-chat greeting below, suggestions above, both coexist. Waiting Agent requests (question/plan/permission/terminal, not Persona, hub mute/space exceptions respected) in hub order; then unhandled Inbox Needs-you newest first. Max3 rows, overflow "N more in Inbox" counts excess Agents too, opens Inbox. Empty section hidden. Left request-type needs-you mark or gray Lark/mail/document; title session name/directory-last/Agent or item title; subtitle request summary/AI summary/preview, both single line. Agent opens Agents chat, Inbox same page as list. Pure needsYou, tests NeedsYouTest/NeedsYouLinksTest.
- History drawer/wide sidebar: first row below search main chat named avatar default Persona, face prefix (matching the iOS sidebar, #2004 / #5110), no group title/tag. No main says Start main chat. Display only, actual title/id unchanged #2003. Inbox bottom tab, not drawer. `AssistantDrawerShotsTest` styles × light / dark `feature/assistant/build/drawer-shots/`. Row ⋯/long-press same Pin/Unpin (not main), Copy session ID, running End chat. Pins hub-shared with Agents, not old local iOS/App; Pinned ⋯ Unpin all only Persona. Recent first 30 then View all N (total), expands all; search all.
- Persona title menu Assistant settings/Knowledge/Voice chat when running extension (query GET `/api/v1/extensions`, pre#1844 `/api/v1/plugins`, each connected Persona-tab entry), Session info, open-chat Copy session ID, notice Copied. Its heading is iOS's (`AssistantCopyText.menuTitle`): "Main chat · claude · Opus 4.5", a side conversation's title with its launcher, "New chat · claude" on a blank page, the selected hub group's name last while several are paired; the launcher and model come from the conversation's stored pick (`model_pick`) first, and the model is named by its short name (`modelShortName`) as the pill and the Agents rows name it, where iOS shows the id. On Glass every item has iOS's icon (`AssistantMenuItem.icon`).
- Handoff title first `<persona-context … title="X">`, hub names from it; history Handed to Persona: X (decode quot/lt/gt/amp), no title Handed to Persona. Missing session title derives last-message first line, parse tags before 30-character truncation (old truncate-first broke long tags). AssistantTest.
- Mock default 5 requests+Inbox, three+overflow; MOCK_NEEDS=two/none; MOCK_HISTORY=1 long30+all, handoff/pin, persists written pins. Persona defaults main; home via Chats→Back home.

## Wide two panes: draggable divider, space page, landscape side insets

Requested 2026-11-01 draggable center/larger right/not device-specific; old Fold.kt/Panes.kt removed #632, ported new shell 2026-11-02.

- material3-adaptive1.3.0 Navigation3 `rememberListDetailSceneStrategy` paneExpansionState/paneExpansionDragHandle per tab (`rememberShellListDetail`, PaneDividers.kt). Built-in paneExpansionDraggable always snaps nearest anchor, cannot snap only within28 dp hinge, so custom draggable sets `PaneExpansionState.setFirstPaneWidth` per frame, relayout only no page recomposition.
- When (#2951, `RegularWindow.kt`, `RegularWindowTest`): every regular window (≥ 600dp wide and ≥ 480dp tall, an open foldable or a tablet either way up; the open iPhone Duo splits at about 669pt wide), and a phone on its side at least 760dp wide, as before. An open foldable upright (about 700dp) used to be one pane.
- Sidebar column (#2951): beside the Assistant page in a regular window, `sidebarSplit` (PaneSplit.kt): the default list width capped at 380dp (the phone drawer's widest); with a separating fold the column ends at the fold and the page starts past it; flat folds do not move it. Not draggable, no saved width; the avatar hides and shows it.
- Settings sheet (#2951): its own list-detail scene with the same splitter, two panes only in a regular window, no saved share (`PaneSplitter(tab = null)`).
- Pure PaneSplit.kt/PaneSplitTest old values: left min 280 dp,right320; default 38% clamped320–420, preserve400right if possible (771 inner320/451,1280 tablet420/860). Tracks finger, haptic crossing flat hinge, release≤28 dp snaps else exact. Per-tab ratio SharedPreferences persona-panes split.tab, local/style-like, resize ratio then minimum clamp.
- Collapse left Agents only with right content chat/space: drag64 dp past left min then release, right full, accent handle at left, drag/tap restores; closing right auto-restores. Inbox/Settings never collapse.
- Separating hinge book/dual-screen fixed split, no handle; let library excludedBounds work by clear() expansion width (otherwise overrides). Flat restores prior drag. Edge-too-near hinge ignored.
- Design `PaneDivider` catalog Two-pane divider: tiny handle extends hit8 dp each side, horizontal-resize cursor, wider/darker dragging, accent nearing/collapsed. Glass translucent ink pill, Brief hairline, Board bordered cell. TalkBack Adjust pane widths; Widen/Narrow left64 dp; Collapse/Expand.
- Space now `SpaceRoute(spaceId)` instead of whole-window JFullScreen, SpaceEntries.kt/detail destination: right pane wide, push phone; session over space, Back space. Destination owns AgentsViewModel/NewAgentViewModel, shared menus/dialogs.
- Landscape camera hole/three-button side navigation: content rail/panes/dock pads left/right `WindowInsets.safeDrawing`, PageBackdrop full bleed; windowInsetsPadding consumes so child navigationBarsPadding no duplicate.
- Screenshots flat book-fold (default/hinge snap/collapse/restore), half-fold debug.posture book fixed/no handle, tri-fold, landscape tablet/phone, styles × light / dark, phone hole left/right/side three-button. Manually test drag feel on foldable/tablet emulator.

## Files and Git in chat

Agent chat ⋯ Files / Git · N opens session-directory files/Git (`feature/files`), read-only except Rename and Move to Trash (below). Layer inside chat, not navigation page: Back closes preview, then ascends folders to breadcrumb root, then chat; top-left Back directly chat. No assistant entry. Hub forwards node `GET /api/v1/machines/{mid}/fs|file|git/status|git/diff|git/log|git/show` (`core/hub/.../HubSessionFiles.kt`).

- Direct path `SessionFilesScreen(..., initialPath = path)` optional final parameter for tags/tool paths/diff headers. Relative completed against cwd, ~ unchanged since only hub knows home. File previews immediately, Back chat without listing; trailing slash or hub is_dir opens folder as breadcrumb root, Back chat. Absolute/~ work before cwd known.
- Browser breadcrumb chips current solid, auto-scroll last; ⋯ Parent folder ascends to / moving root; Show hidden files local persona-files preferences, hidden names dim. Directories first, sizes/relative times now/minutes/hours/days/date, Git letters for files, Changed folders amber if conflict. Visited folders show cached then refresh. Node reasons displayed (macOS Desktop permission guidance), network only Cannot reach hub; failed refresh retains list.
- Rename and Move to Trash (phase 3 F3, first step; iOS `FolderEdits` / `FilesPane`; `docs/protocol/protocol.md` ("Changing files")): a row's ⋯ or long press adds **Rename** and **Move to Trash** when the hub lists `file_edit` in hello's features, or lists none (an older hub's reply: offered, and the answer tells), through `SessionFiles.editsFiles` (forwarded by the app's group wrappers `GroupTranslation.kt` and `MergedServices.kt` `routedFiles`). Rename opens a dialog with the name (Rename / Cancel); an empty name, `.`, `..` or one with `/` is said in an error toast before anything is sent, the same name sends nothing; then `POST …/files/rename {path, cwd, name}` and the folder is read again. Move to Trash asks first ("Move “name” to the Trash?", it goes to the computer's Trash, Undo right after or from that Trash), then `POST …/files/trash {paths, cwd}`, reads the folder again and shows a toast "Moved “name” to the Trash" with **Undo** (`POST …/files/restore {trash_ids}`, then the folder again; a failure says "Couldn't put it back: …"). No permanent delete. Refusals in iOS's words (`FileEdits.kt` `editRefusal`): `read_only` (outside the home, ~/.persona, ~/Library) says only home files can be changed from the phone; `not_found` says it is gone (Android has the Refresh button, not pull to refresh); `node_outdated` shows the hub's words; a plain 404 says the hub is too old; 502 `internal` says the service failed (HTTP status, then the hub's `X-Request-Id` as iOS adds it to a 5xx); `exists` names the hub's `name`, else the name asked for; a connection failure as folder reads say it. A change runs to its end when the page closes meanwhile (its own scope), so its toast and Undo still come. Requests wait 130 s (the hub gives the machine 2 minutes). Not yet: Move, upload into a folder, selecting several, the review page. Demo mode has no Files page. Mock hub: `MOCK_FILE_OPS=1` (home only; `MOCK_FILE_OPS_OLD_NODE=1` for `node_outdated`).
- Preview Markdown rendered by default, README img/a/br/p converted, bare links end at first non-ASCII; ⋯ Source / Render Markdown. Local-machine links open Files, images fetched through hub (no network images/App image networking stack). Long docs block-lazy. Code/text monospace line numbers/two-axis scroll, zoomable images, PDF background `PdfRenderer` per page at display width; other Cannot preview with type/size. Size header, First2 MB for truncated text, not whole images/PDF/video/docs.
- Whole files (#2981, web fileURL/fileBytes, iOS fetchBytes): `stream=1` previews (`SessionFiles.filePreview`, feature file_stream), image/PDF/video/audio/Office/xlsx descriptor only stream true/no data; bytes file/download → `cache/files-image|files-pdf-whole|files-video|files-office|files-sheet/` (`WholeFile.kt` rememberWholeFile), named path/size/revision (size+mtime #4821), last 1–2 retained. Shares download too. Legacy base64 reused if complete, >2 MB download. Unsupported/old409 node guidance. Large image Coil `BitmapFactoryDecoder` display-size sampling from file, no entire memory read.
- Video (#2981, web MediaView, iOS VideoFileView): video/* or mp4/mov/m4v/mpeg/webm/mkv/avi download whole then system VideoView/MediaController, background pauses; unsupported codec explained. No progressive streaming, audio rationale below.
- Office (#2981, web OfficeView PDF fallback): docx/docm/dotx/doc/dot/pptx/pptm/ppsx/potx/ppt/pps/odt/odp/wps/wpd/pages/key/numbers via hub file/convert?to=pdf, machine LibreOffice,130 sec beyond hub2 min, PDF pages. - Two versions of documents and pictures (#2805, iOS #1579, web #1452): when the hub has the `git_blob` feature (`SessionFiles.comparesVersions`), a changed or committed picture, PDF, docx, pptx or xlsx opens a compare page (`ComparePage.kt`) instead of the text diff; without it, the text diff stays. Versions: unstaged is index ↔ working tree, staged is HEAD ↔ index, a commit's file is `hash^` ↔ that commit; a rename's old version is read under its old name, a file deleted in the tree has no new version, and `git/blob` answers `missing` when a version has no such file. The old version goes into the node's cache through `GET …/git/blob`, then both are downloaded with `file/download` into this module's cache. Pictures side by side, or stacked under a slider (only when both versions exist); documents are reduced to text (`DocumentText.kt`: docx by page, one line per table row, deleted revisions left out; pptx in slide order with notes; xlsx per sheet reusing `Xlsx.read`; PDF text through `PdfRenderer`, which needs Android 15, older systems say so) and compared line by line like the web (`DocumentCompare.kt`: Myers, two lines of context around a change, the rest folded into "… N unchanged lines", same-width table rows mark only the changed cells); identical text says the change is in formatting or pictures. ⋯ Open old / Open new opens them in the file page. Unit tests `DocumentCompareTest`, `FilesControllerTest`, `HubSessionFilesTest`; screenshots `CompareShotsTest` (`build/compare-shots/`).
