### Agents herdr startup notice (#1916)

Visible Agents queries online classic herdr/herdr-both `GET /api/v1/machines/{mid}/herdr` every 5 sec;
normal machine list lacks this state. Notice only known true/running false, respecting hub-group/machine filters;
not offline/Herdr App/history/unknown/unsupported. Shared design JNotice all styles.
Start tap only sends POST herdr/start, no auto-start setting change; button hidden in-flight,
confirmed running removes notice, failure explains and retains. Earlier queries cannot overwrite confirmed start; no automatic start retry.


## Cross-app floating capsule (#2124)

Settings › Floating capsule defaults off, Show with tasks / Always show; first enable grants system Display over other Apps.
Native TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY, independent of status-bar Live Updates, supported on project Android12+.

- Collapsed 56×36 dp: the avatar centered, the unread dot on its top-end corner; drag snaps to the nearest edge and remembers the relative height. Expanding grows the card out of the capsule and fades it in (the V7 design's ease-out); collapsing pops the capsule back; reduced motion skips both.
- One tap opens the current conversation ready to type: the app's current conversation first, else the newest unread, else the main chat. The bar has the conversation title (opens the unread list), **Next** (a tinted pill), **Expand**, **More** and a collapse button; tapping outside also collapses. **More › Close capsule** turns the capsule off (Settings › Floating capsule turns it back on).
- Unread is a list grouped like the Agents page: one herdr workspace per computer, else the session's repository, and Feishu conversations in one **Feishu messages** group; each group has **Mark all read**. The main Inbox list is item-based, but this capsule intentionally remains conversation-based because it shows the message window and reply composer. A subtask (a Codex subagent, an agent another one created) is not in it, its count or the badge, and its finishing is no notice: it is read under its parent in Agents (#4769, see **Subtasks** in "Aligning the Agents list and the Machines page (#3038)"). The main chat is the other section. Inbox documents, moments and doc comments have no read receipt here and stay in the app's inbox.
- Agent and Persona replies render as Markdown without a bubble; people's messages and your own stay bubbles. A reply on screen for about 0.75 s marks the conversation read through the app's read path (Agents: seen versions; Feishu: the conversation's read position, sent as a string as the extension expects); the dots go at once and return if the receipt fails. **Next** marks the current conversation read and moves on, even before a Feishu window has loaded.
- Conversations read or passed with Next are remembered with their newest message and stay out of the list, the badge and Next until a newer message arrives (Feishu counts move between the hub's inbox record and Feishu, so they are no signal); the record survives restarts and updates. Mark all read for Feishu acknowledges each conversation's newest page directly, four at a time; a topic group whose thread replies Feishu still counts stays read here.
- New completion temporarily shows one line session · Completed (or · New message), about 4 s, then the small capsule; queued sequentially, tap opens the target. A separate non-focus notice: no transcript, no IME, no change to the manual choice or draft.
- Tracks the turn observed Working while online, including completion after a NeedsYou wait; first fresh snapshot after reconnect is only a baseline, never guesses unseen runs. Independent of system long-task notification filters.
- Independent session drafts through the existing persistent outbox / group routing. Transcripts are observed only while expanded. A message the hub refused reads "Not sent. Open the chat" under it, never the hub's code; the chat says why and offers **Retry** (#2821).
- Stay on screen: an optional accessibility service (`CapsuleKeeperService`, Settings › Floating capsule › Stay on screen) that reads no window content and handles no events. The system binds it and rebinds it after the process dies, which an overlay alone does not survive on phones that clear background apps on lock; on connect and each unlock it asks for the resident connection. A force-stop turns any accessibility service off; some systems also turn it off when the app updates itself, so after an update (and on start) the app turns it back on when `WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS` was granted once over adb, or posts a notification that opens the accessibility settings.
- Performance with the process resident: session events write the first-paint snapshot at most every 10 s; the capsule catalog and inbox rows rebuild from the latest inputs only; unopened Feishu windows are not prefetched and only four idle windows stay in memory, oversized historical window snapshots are left untouched and skipped before decoding, and closed chat bodies stop synchronizing a minute after closing. Hidden primary tabs keep their navigation and scroll state under a CREATED lifecycle so page collectors and timers stop; the capsule catalog computes on Dispatchers.Default. With any accessibility service on, Compose diffs the semantics tree on every recomposition, so the Agents page redraws at most every 300 ms.

Code `android/platform/capsule/` window/permissions/policy/public CapsuleContentSource;
`android/app/src/main/java/persona/android/capsule/` connects sessions/messages/pairing; Settings only FloatingCapsuleSettings.
Completion uses merged live sessions gated by group's online state; VerifiedSessionsRepository exposes existing verified snapshot solely for baseline, no extra fetch/WS. Completion tap own target route, not current manual main/Inbox choice.

Extension contributes.ui[].placement capsule_card compact cards/actions via existing ui.render/ui.action;
rendered only in expanded main/session detail, unread/Inbox lists compact; confirmations/errors/receipts inside card. Install/upgrade accepts outside-App UI capability.
Full App opens extension pages/links; extension runs no phone code, overlay permission owned by user/native host. [extensions.md](../extensions/extensions.md).

Delivered: overlay, toggle, conversations with read sync, the grouped unread list, staying resident and the declarative slot. Long-press voice, a double-tap screenshot preview and every approval type in place remain #2124 follow-ups; the issue stays open.
